# P5 Reviews and Case Reports Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Take a case report, a systematic review and a scoping or narrative review through the P4 engine (roles, assemble, audit, G4, hook) to a reviewed, audited `.docx`, with every case fact, PRISMA count, pooled estimate, I², τ², prediction interval and absolute effect taken from the results ledger — spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-22-p5-reviews-case-reports-design.md` (authoritative; read it at commit a2bd347 or later).

**Architecture:** Shared pieces first: a new ledger status `observed`, a stdlib result log (`crolib/resultlog.py`, the P2 log format without pandas) and a merge mode in `results_ledger.py import`, so case facts, PRISMA counts, meta-analysis results and GRADE absolute effects each own their records in one ledger. Then six stdlib CLIs (`case_facts.py`, `screening.py`, `extract_check.py`, `meta.py`, `grade.py`, `case_lint.py`), figure types (`prisma`, `rob`, forest diamond and prediction bar), two deliverables (`case-report`, `review`) wired through `crolib/drafts.py`, `assemble.py`, `audit.py`, `worklog.py`, `guideline_checklist.py`, `gate.py`, `gate_hook.py`, `init_project.py`; references, role briefs, agent edits, skills and commands; five scenarios; two fictional end-to-end examples; release 0.6.0. R with `metafor` is an optional cross-check (`meta.py r-status`, `r-check`); the Python engine is the reference and every feature works without R. The agent reasons; scripts measure.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.10+ (standard library only for every new or changed script except `figures.py`, which keeps its optional matplotlib), Markdown skills and references, JSON item lists, `unittest`, optional R ≥ 4 with `metafor` (maintainer goldens and the opt-in `r-check` only), Claude Code CLI (`claude -p`) for scenarios and the end-to-end runs.

## Global Constraints

- Everything in the Global Constraints of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-p4-write.md` (lines 11–28) still applies unless this plan overrides it.
- **Python 3.10+ standard library only** in every new or changed script of this plan (`results_ledger.py`, `analysis_plan.py`, `crostats.py` constant only, `crolib/{numfmt,resultlog,projectcfg,drafts,reviews}.py`, `case_facts.py`, `screening.py`, `extract_check.py`, `meta.py`, `grade.py`, `case_lint.py`, `assemble.py`, `audit.py`, `worklog.py`, `guideline_checklist.py`, `gate.py`, `gate_hook.py`, `init_project.py`). `figures.py` keeps matplotlib as its optional dependency. No stdlib API newer than 3.10.
- **Exit codes** for every script: 0 = ok / clean / written; 1 = findings with at least one `block`, or an action refused (gate closed, G3-locked ledger, unresolved conflicts); 2 = usage error, missing or unreadable input, missing dependency (R, metafor, matplotlib), network or internal error. Results to stdout, errors to stderr.
- **Finding format**: `<CODE> <block|warn> <file>:<line>: <msg>`, project-relative POSIX paths. Exit 1 only when a `block` exists. New codes: `E-CASE` (case report anonymisation, consent, structure), `E-SCR` (screening, extraction, RoB and charting log integrity), `E-GRADE` (claim strength vs GRADE certainty, warn only), `E-META` (Python vs metafor mismatch in `meta.py r-check`, warn only).
- Every script calls `sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")` and the same for stderr inside `try/except` at import time.
- **LF only**: every text file a script writes is opened with `open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")`; files under `examples/**` and `tests/scenarios/fixtures/**` are byte-stable (`.gitattributes -text`).
- **Skills**: kebab-case folder = `name`; model skills `description: 'Use when … Triggers: "<vi>", "<en>", ….'` (Vietnamese and English triggers, never the workflow), single-quoted; command skills add `disable-model-invocation: true` and call model skills with "use the Skill tool with `<name>`"; body ≤ 500 words (`python tools/lint_skills.py`); script calls written `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/<script>.py" …` from the project folder; every `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/…` link resolves (`test_reference_library.py`). A skill change ships with a scenario (Task 12) or a stated reason.
- **Never type a PMID or DOI from memory.** Citations only from tool lookups (`search_lit.py`, `verify_refs.py`) made during the implementation or the E2E run; guideline citations are the `guidelines.json` records already VERIFIED (D24).
- **Never type a result from memory**: results numbers are `{{R-…}}` placeholders; unit-test expectations are hand-computable from the test's own inputs, recomputed independently inside the test, or read from `tests/unit/fixtures/meta/metafor-goldens.json` (generated by `metafor`, D16).
- **No real patient data** anywhere: fixtures, examples and scenarios are fictional and say so.
- **No machine- or user-specific paths** in plugin code, skills, references, tests or examples. R is found by OS-aware discovery (D15); goldens record the R and metafor versions, never a path.
- **Unit tests are offline** (network mocked; `verify_refs.py`/`search_lit.py` never called) and **never need R** (`test_meta_r.py` skips cleanly when R or metafor is absent, as in CI).
- Scripts never write inside `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`; they write only under the project given on the command line.
- **Commits**: one final paragraph ending `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. A commit that changes `build_docx.py`, `references/statistics/*`, `skills/sample-size-sap/*` or `skills/protocol-design/*` puts `Co-authored-by: Amelia Le <267516761+ngoclnl189-amelia@users.noreply.github.com>` on the line before it, in the same paragraph. (No task in this plan is expected to touch those files; if one does, add the trailer.)
- Owner name in any document, commit or README: **Trần Quang Hưng**.
- Before every commit: `python tools/lint_skills.py` and `python -m unittest discover -s tests/unit -v` → PASS.
- Version 0.6.0 at release (Task 14).

### Plan decisions (where the spec is open or self-contradictory)

| # | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | Each producer writes its own log + results document and merges it: `results_ledger.py import <p> --from DOC --merge` replaces the ledger records whose `source_file` is one of the incoming documents' source files, keeps every other record, refuses an id already owned by another source, and writes the records sorted by (analysis number, result number) | the P2 `import` replaces the whole ledger; case facts, PRISMA counts, meta results and GRADE absolute effects come from four tools (spec §5.1, §6.2, §6.4, §6.5) |
| D2 | `validate_doc` accepts `data_file` = `04_data/*.deid.csv` (P2) or one of `01_case/facts.csv`, `02_evidence/sr/screening.csv`, `04_data/sr/extraction.csv`, `05_analysis/meta/grade.json` | the P2 rule allows only de-identified patient data files |
| D3 | `observed` is valid only with `analysis_id: A0`; `analysis_plan.check` counts an `observed` record as reported and skips every other status rule for it; the register is read from `03_protocol/sap.md`, else `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md`; results from `05_analysis/output/results.json`, else `ledgers/results-ledger.json` | spec §5.1 "the SAP register check skips it"; reviews have no SAP and no `results.json` |
| D4 | New ledger kinds `absolute` (0 dp), `smd` (2), `tau2` (3), `measure` (1) in `numfmt.KINDS` and `crostats.KINDS` (kept equal); an optional record field `decimals` (int 0–4) overrides the kind precision in `numfmt.render` | spec §6.5 `kind: absolute`; case facts keep their printed decimals (78 g/L, 1.07 mg/dL); τ² needs 3 decimals |
| D5 | Case fact kind from the unit: `%` → `percent`; a count unit (empty, `n`, `cases`, `case`, `ca`, `lần`, `times`, `episodes`, `đợt`) with an integer value → `n`; anything else → `measure`; `decimals` = decimals written in `facts.csv`. Ids `R-A0-001…` in row order (spec "0NN" = the 3-digit ledger id); the fact id is kept in the label (`… [C01]`) and `day` is stored on the record | spec §5.1 "kind from the unit" leaves the mapping open |
| D6 | `crolib/resultlog.py` (stdlib) writes the P2 log line format of `crostats.Recorder` (`R-A1-001 label: value=… ci=lo,hi p=… n=… count=…`) | `crostats.py` imports pandas; P5 scripts are stdlib only |
| D7 | Review-work briefs (`screen-suggest`, `extract-draft`, `rob-draft`) start with `orchestra-prep: <name>`; the hook denies an Agent/Task prompt with that marker unless G2 is open; `orchestra-role:` keeps needing G3 | screening precedes G3, so the G3 rule would block every suggestion pass; spec §6.1 makes G2 the screening gate |
| D8 | `screening.csv` = `stage,rec_id,reviewer,decision,reason,study,at`, append-only; the latest row per (stage, record, reviewer) counts; reviewer `consensus` resolves; a final decision needs a consensus row or two agreeing human include/exclude decisions (`maybe` is a conflict); full-text exclusions need a reason from the protocol's `## Full-text exclusion reasons` list or `not retrieved` (counted as PRISMA "not retrieved"); `--study ID` on full-text includes groups reports into studies; record ids `rec-0001…` | spec §6.2 leaves the file format open |
| D9 | `review.reviewers` is a one-line flow list (`[Reviewer A, Reviewer B]`) read by `projectcfg.parse_list`; names matching `ai`, `claude`, `gpt`, `assistant`, `model`, `llm`, `bot` (whole word, any case) are refused even when listed | spec §6.2 and decision 5 (AI never a reviewer); `projectcfg` reads no YAML lists |
| D10 | PRISMA count ids: `R-A0-001`…`011` fixed (databases, registers, duplicates, screened, excluded, sought, not retrieved, assessed, reports excluded, studies included, reports included), `021`–`049` per source (sorted name), `051`–`099` per full-text exclusion reason (sorted); a source whose name contains `regist`, `clinicaltrials` or `ictrp` counts as a register; `counts` refuses to write while any reached record is unresolved | a stable layout for the flow figure and the text |
| D11 | `extract_check.py` also checks `rob.csv` (RoB 2 D1–D5, ROBINS-I D1–D7, plus `overall`; overall never better than the worst domain; `reviewer` must name both human reviewers) and, for scoping reviews, `charting.csv` (`extracted_by`, `verified_by` two different listed reviewers); extractor files are `extraction-<slug>.csv`, slug = lower-case name with non-alphanumerics → `-` | spec §6.3 puts extraction, RoB and charting under E-SCR without a script for the last two |
| D12 | The SR/scoping register is the `## Analysis register` table of `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md` with columns `ID, Type, Outcome, Timepoint, Measure, Model, HKSJ, Experimental, Comparator, Factor`; `Type` ∈ `counts` (A0), `pool`, `subgroup`, `leave-one-out`; `Measure` ∈ OR, RR, RD, MD, SMD, GEN, GEN-OR, GEN-RR, GEN-HR; `Model` ∈ RE-REML, RE-DL, CE-IV, CE-MH (OR/RR only); status `deviation` when `decisions.md` has `DEVIATION A<n>:`, else `prespecified`; post-hoc meta-analyses are not run by `meta.py` (the skill records `POST-HOC` and refers to a statistician) | the spec names the register but not its columns |
| D13 | Meta result ids: pool `001` pooled, `002` τ², `003` I², `004` Q (with p), `005` prediction interval (value = pooled, ci = PI; k ≥ 3), `006` Egger intercept (k ≥ 10), `011…` study effects in study order; subgroup `001` test for differences, level j at `10j+1` pooled, `10j+2` τ², `10j+3` I²; leave-one-out `001…` one per omitted study; fewer than 2 studies → no pooled ids, a narrative log line, the single study at `011` | a stable layout for figures and text |
| D14 | Estimators: zero-cell 0.5 added to all four cells of that study only, double-zero studies excluded (OR, RR, RD); MH pooled from uncorrected counts, its Q from the IV-weighted study effects around the MH estimate; SMD = Hedges' g with the exact J (`lgamma`) and `vi = 1/n1 + 1/n2 + g²/(2(n1+n2))`; I² = 100·τ²/(τ² + s²), s² = (k−1)Σw/((Σw)² − Σw²) (equals (Q−df)/Q under DL; common-effect models use (Q−df)/Q); PI = μ ± t(k−2) · √(τ² + se²) with the reported se (HKSJ se when `HKSJ: yes`); subgroup test = Q between separate per-level fits (weights 1/se²); Egger = WLS of yᵢ on seᵢ with weights 1/vᵢ, the slope on seᵢ being Egger's intercept (equal to `lm(yi ~ sqrt(vi), weights = 1/vi)`) | the spec names the methods, not the formulas; these are the metafor-compatible choices the goldens check |
| D15 | R discovery order: `project.yaml → tools.rscript`, `PATH`, `R_HOME/bin`, then per OS — Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\R\R-*\bin`, `%ProgramFiles%\R\R-*\bin` (newest version first); macOS `/Library/Frameworks/R.framework/Resources/bin`, `/opt/homebrew/bin`, `/usr/local/bin`; Linux `/usr/bin`, `/usr/local/bin`. `r-check` compares every pooled estimate (pool analyses, subgroup levels with k ≥ 2, leave-one-out fits) on the model scale; mismatches are `E-META warn` (exit 0) | spec §6.4 update (a2bd347) |
| D16 | Goldens: `tests/unit/fixtures/meta/make_goldens.R` runs metafor on metafor's own datasets (`dat.bcg`, `dat.normand1999`) and one constructed zero-cell set written in the script, and writes every input next to every output in `metafor-goldens.json`; Egger goldens come from base R `lm`; the Python tests read inputs from the JSON (nothing typed) at relative tolerance 1e-4 | spec §6.4 "no number is typed from memory" |
| D17 | GRADE absolute effects are `R-A<n>-901` (comparator risk per 1000), `902` (intervention risk per 1000, CI), `903` (difference per 1000, CI), status of the pooled result; `sof.md` is a section of the systematic review order (after `04-results.md`); E-GRADE: a paragraph citing a low-certainty result needs a word from the low or very-low list, very low needs the very-low list | spec §6.5 gives the kind and file but no ids or matching rule |
| D18 | Figures: forest gains `pooled` (result id) and optional `prediction` (result id whose `ci` is the PI); `type: prisma` takes ledger ids per PRISMA box (required `identified`, `duplicates`, `screened`, `excluded`, `sought`, `not_retrieved`, `assessed`, `reports_excluded`, `studies`; optional `registers`, `reasons` list of `{text, n}`, `reports`) and draws stage labels; `type: rob` reads `04_data/sr/rob.csv` (fixed path) for one `outcome` and one `tool` | spec §6.6 names the figures, not their spec keys |
| D19 | `case_lint.py` scans `01_case/dossier.md` and the case-report drafts; identifiers via phi-guard's `phi_scan.py --file` (strong → block, weak → warn); missing consent is `warn` without `--strict` and `block` with it; `gate.export_problems(…, "case-report")` adds the strict consent problem, so `assemble.py --final`, `build_docx.py out/case-report.docx` and the hook all refuse; `audit.py` runs case_lint without the phi part (E-PHI already scans) and adds `E-REF` for `similar-cases.csv`; new optional key `institution.city` | spec §5.3 and §8 ("case_lint --strict blocks the final export") |
| D20 | In the `case-report` deliverable only, relative-time tokens (`day +3`, `ngày 14`, `week 6`, `tuần 6`, `3 days later`, `sau 3 ngày`) are not E-NUM numbers | a case report's timeline is relative time by rule (spec §5.1) and is not a result |
| D21 | Section files: case report CARE `00-title, 01-abstract, 02-introduction, 03-patient-information, 04-clinical-findings, 05-timeline, 06-diagnostic-assessment, 07-therapeutic-intervention, 08-follow-up, 09-discussion, 10-patient-perspective, 11-consent` (required 03, 04, 05, 09, 11); BMJ mode (`target.journal_style: bmj`) `00-title, 01-summary, 02-background, 03-case-presentation, 04-investigations, 05-differential-diagnosis, 06-treatment, 07-outcome, 08-discussion, 09-learning-points, 10-patient-perspective, 11-consent` (required 03, 08, 09, 11); review systematic `00-title, 01-abstract, 02-introduction, 03-methods, 04-results, sof.md, 05-discussion, 06-conclusion, 07-other-information`; scoping the same without `sof.md`; narrative `00-title, 01-abstract, 02-introduction, 03-methods, 04-*.md, 05-discussion, 06-conclusion` (required introduction, methods, conclusion; systematic/scoping require introduction, methods, results, discussion); review kind from `project.yaml → design` | spec §5.2 and §8 give orders by name only |
| D22 | `audit.py`: E-STAT only for thesis, manuscript and systematic/scoping reviews; E-CASE for `case-report`; E-SCR and E-GRADE for `review` (E-SCR only systematic/scoping); the audit also hashes `01_case/facts.csv`, `01_case/similar-cases.csv`, `02_evidence/sr/{records,screening}.csv`, `04_data/sr/{extraction,rob,charting}.csv`, `05_analysis/meta/grade.json` when present | a case report has no register; G4 must bind the inputs of the numbers |
| D23 | `init_project.py`: type/design pairs enforced (`case-report`↔`case-report`, `systematic-review`↔`systematic-review`, `review`↔`scoping-review`/`narrative-review`); folders `01_case/` + `06_writing/case-report/`, or `02_evidence/sr/` + `04_data/sr/` + `05_analysis/meta/` + `06_writing/review/` (narrative: `06_writing/review/` only); YAML adds `institution.city`, `target.journal_style: null`, `review: {reviewers: [], registration: null}`, `tools: {rscript: null}`; the template `.gitignore` re-includes `04_data/sr/` (published-study data, no patients) | spec §8 |
| D24 | The four guideline citations already exist in `guidelines.json`, VERIFIED on 2026-11-21: `gagnier2013` (CARE), `page2021` (PRISMA 2020), `tricco2018` (PRISMA-ScR), `baethge2019` (SANRA); Task 2 re-checks them online once (`verify_refs.py`) and types no identifier | spec §9 |
| D25 | Item ids follow the official checklists; the implementer confirms each list against the official checklist page before committing (CARE: care-statement.org; PRISMA 2020 and PRISMA-ScR: prisma-statement.org; SANRA: the `baethge2019` article) and, if numbering differs, the official numbering wins and the test list is changed with it | the ids below were written without the checklists open |
| D26 | `meta.py` refuses a scoping review (exit 1, PRISMA-ScR message) and `extract_check.py` warns on a scoping `rob.csv` | spec §6.3 and scenario `scoping-asks-pooling` |
| D27 | Command skills are folders `case` and `sr` (`/orchestra:case`, `/orchestra:sr`); model skills `case-report`, `systematic-review` | a plugin's skill folder is its command name (P4 D1) |
| D28 | `projectcfg.get(project, "a.b")` returns `None` for `""`, `null`, `~`; used for every new nullable key | `read_project_yaml` returns the text `"null"` |
| D29 | `examples/fictional-case` is written in English, CARE mode, chat in Vietnamese; `examples/fictional-sr` is written in Vietnamese with five fictional trials and a simulated export | one example per output language; English is the usual language of case-report journals |
| D30 | In the E2E runs every human act (G1–G4, the consent line, Reviewer A/B screening, extraction and RoB) is a scripted input the controller performs and the README lists; Claude never performs it | spec decision 5 and the gates are about who decides; an example must not show Claude deciding |
| D31 | `drafts.lang_of` uses `target.language` for every deliverable except the thesis (P4: manuscript only) | journal case reports and reviews follow the target journal's language like a manuscript |


## Execution batches

Tasks in a batch touch disjoint files and may run in parallel worktrees.

- **Batch A: Tasks 1, 2** in parallel.
- **Batch B: Tasks 3, 5, 6, 7, 8** in parallel (each needs Task 1), **then Task 4** (needs `projectcfg.parse_list` and `p5_helpers.py` from Task 3).
- **Batch C: Tasks 9 and 10** in parallel (9 needs 3, 4, 6, 8; 10 needs nothing but Task 2's item files for its tests).
- **Batch D: Task 11** (needs 9, 10), **then Task 12**, **then Tasks 13 and 14** (14 last: release).

File ownership in Batch B: Task 3 `screening.py`, `crolib/projectcfg.py`, `tests/unit/p5_helpers.py`, `test_screening.py`; Task 5 `meta.py`, `tests/unit/fixtures/meta/`, `test_meta.py`, `test_meta_r.py`; Task 6 `grade.py`, `references/review/grade-wording.md`, `test_grade.py`; Task 7 `figures.py`, `references/presenting/figures-spec.md`, `test_figures.py`; Task 8 `case_lint.py`, `test_case_lint.py`. Batch C: Task 9 owns every script listed under it and its tests; Task 10 owns `references/**` (except `grade-wording.md`, `figures-spec.md`), `agents/*.md`, `test_agents.py`, `test_reference_library.py`.

**Controller prerequisites.** Task 5 step "generate goldens" needs R with metafor on the controller's machine (the maintainer machine has R 4.6.1 with metafor 5.2.1 in the user library, found by `meta.py r-status` through the Windows discovery path — a fact about that machine, not plugin behaviour). If an implementer subagent reports R or metafor missing, the controller installs them by hand (`install.packages("metafor")` in R) and reruns that step; no plan step installs software. Tasks 13–14 need network access (literature lookups) and the `claude` CLI; budget guide USD 150 for both runs together; a stage stalled more than 45 minutes is killed and rerun once.

---
### Task 1: Ledger status `observed`, merge import, result log, `case_facts.py` (spec 5.1, 6.2 counts route, 13.2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/crolib/numfmt.py` (PRECISION, `render`), `plugins/orchestra/scripts/crostats.py:63-64` (KINDS), `plugins/orchestra/scripts/results_ledger.py` (STATUSES, data files, `decimals`, `--merge`), `plugins/orchestra/scripts/analysis_plan.py` (`register_path`, `results_records`, `check`, `main`)
- Create: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/crolib/resultlog.py`, `plugins/orchestra/scripts/case_facts.py`, `tests/unit/test_case_facts.py`
- Test: `tests/unit/test_case_facts.py` (also covers the ledger and analysis_plan changes)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `numfmt.KINDS` ⊇ `{"absolute", "smd", "tau2", "measure"}`; `numfmt.render(rec, part, lang)` honours `rec["decimals"]` (int 0–4).
- Produces `results_ledger.STATUSES = ("prespecified", "posthoc", "deviation", "observed")`, `results_ledger.DATA_FILES` (tuple of the four P5 data files), `results_ledger.import_results(project, src=None, verify_raw=False, accept_relabel=False, merge=False) -> tuple[int, list[str]]` (raises `LockedError`, `RelabelError` as before); CLI `results_ledger.py import <project> [--from FILE] [--verify-raw] [--accept-relabel] [--merge]`.
- Produces `analysis_plan.register_path(project) -> Path`, `analysis_plan.results_records(project) -> list`.

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import analysis_plan
import case_facts
import gate
import init_project
import results_ledger
from crolib import numfmt, resultlog

FACTS = ("id,label,value,unit,day,source\n"
         "C01,haemoglobin at admission,78,g/L,0,lab report\n"
         "C02,C-reactive protein,12.5,mg/L,0,lab report\n"
         "C03,oxygen saturation,91,%,0,nursing chart\n"
         "C04,days in hospital,9,,+9,discharge summary\n"
         "C05,creatinine,1.07,mg/dL,-14,outpatient record\n")
REGISTER = ("# Protocol (fictional)\n\n## Analysis register\n\n| ID | Type | Outcome |\n|---|---|---|\n"
            "| A0 | counts | PRISMA flow |\n| A1 | pool | mortality |\n")


def quiet(fn, *args):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        code = fn(*args)
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


def rec(rid, aid, status, src, label="x", kind="n"):
    return {"id": rid, "analysis_id": aid, "status": status, "label": label, "value": 1, "kind": kind,
            "source_file": src, "locator": "line 1", "raw": f"{rid} {label}: value=1"}


class TestCaseFacts(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "T", "case-report", "case-report", "en", "vi")
        (self.p / "01_case").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(FACTS, encoding="utf-8")

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def ledger(self):
        return json.loads((self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]

    def test_kind_for(self):
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("%", "91"), ("percent", 0))
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("", "9"), ("n", None))
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("lần", "2"), ("n", None))
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("", "2.5"), ("measure", 1))
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("g/L", "78"), ("measure", 0))
        self.assertEqual(case_facts.kind_for("mg/dL", "1,07"), ("measure", 2))

    def test_facts_become_observed_a0_records(self):
        code, out, err = quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        recs = self.ledger()
        self.assertEqual([r["id"] for r in recs], [f"R-A0-00{i}" for i in range(1, 6)])
        self.assertTrue(all(r["analysis_id"] == "A0" and r["status"] == "observed" for r in recs))
        by = {r["id"]: r for r in recs}
        self.assertEqual((by["R-A0-001"]["kind"], by["R-A0-001"]["decimals"], by["R-A0-001"]["unit"]), ("measure", 0, "g/L"))
        self.assertEqual((by["R-A0-002"]["kind"], by["R-A0-002"]["decimals"]), ("measure", 1))
        self.assertEqual(by["R-A0-003"]["kind"], "percent")
        self.assertEqual(by["R-A0-004"]["kind"], "n")
        self.assertEqual((by["R-A0-004"]["day"], by["R-A0-005"]["day"]), (9, -14))
        self.assertIn("[C01]", by["R-A0-001"]["label"])
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render(by["R-A0-001"], "value", "en"), "78")
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render(by["R-A0-002"], "value", "vi"), "12,5")
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render(by["R-A0-005"], "value", "en"), "1.07")
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render(by["R-A0-003"], "value", "en"), "91%")
        self.assertIn("C01 → R-A0-001", out)
        doc = json.loads((self.p / case_facts.DOC_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(doc["data_file"], "01_case/facts.csv")
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.validate_doc(doc, self.p, verify_raw=True), [])
        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", (self.p / case_facts.LOG_REL).read_bytes())

    def test_rerun_is_byte_stable_and_g3_lock_refuses_changes(self):
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 0)
        led = self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json"
        before = led.read_bytes()
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(led.read_bytes(), before)
        gate.open_gate(self.p, "G3", by="A", files=["ledgers/results-ledger.json", "01_case/facts.csv"])
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(FACTS.replace(",78,", ",79,"), encoding="utf-8")
        code, out, err = quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("G3 is locked", err)
        self.assertEqual(led.read_bytes(), before)

    def test_relabel_needs_flag(self):
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 0)
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(FACTS.replace("haemoglobin at admission", "Hb"), encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 1)
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p), "--accept-relabel"])[0], 0)
        self.assertIn("Hb [C01]", self.ledger()[0]["label"])

    def test_invalid_rows_are_listed_and_nothing_written(self):
        bad = FACTS + "C01,duplicate id,1,,0,x\nC07,calendar day,5,,12/03/2025,x\nC08,,3,,0,x\nC09,text value,high,,0,x\n"
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(bad, encoding="utf-8")
        code, out, err = quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        for s in ("C01: duplicate id", "C07: day", "C08: label", "C09: value"):
            self.assertIn(s, out)
        self.assertNotIn("R-A0", (self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_usage_errors_exit_2(self):
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text("id,label\nC01,x\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 2)
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").unlink()
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.p)])[0], 2)
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(self.t / "nope")])[0], 2)


class TestLedgerP5(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "T", "systematic-review", "systematic-review", "en", "vi")
        out = self.p / "05_analysis" / "output"
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for name in ("a-log.txt", "b-log.txt"):
            (out / name).write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "01_case").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (self.p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(FACTS, encoding="utf-8")
        self.A, self.B = "05_analysis/output/a-log.txt", "05_analysis/output/b-log.txt"

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def doc(self, results, data_file="01_case/facts.csv"):
        return {"engine": "t", "data_file": data_file, "data_sha256": "", "run_at": "t", "results": results, "tables": []}

    def put(self, name, results):
        path = self.p / "05_analysis" / "output" / name
        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.doc(results)), encoding="utf-8")
        return path

    def ids(self):
        return [r["id"] for r in json.loads((self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]]

    def test_observed_only_for_a0(self):
        probs = results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([rec("R-A1-001", "A1", "observed", self.A)]), self.p)
        self.assertTrue(any("only for A0" in x for x in probs), probs)
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([rec("R-A0-001", "A0", "observed", self.A)]), self.p), [])

    def test_new_kinds_and_decimals(self):
        for kind in ("absolute", "smd", "tau2", "measure"):
            self.assertIn(kind, numfmt.KINDS)
            self.assertEqual(results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([rec("R-A1-001", "A1", "prespecified", self.A, kind=kind)]), self.p), [])
        bad = dict(rec("R-A0-001", "A0", "observed", self.A), decimals=7)
        self.assertTrue(any("decimals" in x for x in results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([bad]), self.p)))
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render({"value": 12.0, "kind": "absolute"}, "value", "en"), "12")
        self.assertEqual(numfmt.render({"value": 0.04321, "kind": "tau2"}, "value", "en"), "0.043")

    def test_p5_data_files_allowed(self):
        for f in results_ledger.DATA_FILES:
            probs = results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([], data_file=f), self.p)
            self.assertFalse([x for x in probs if "data_file" in x], f)
        probs = results_ledger.validate_doc(self.doc([], data_file="01_case/other.csv"), self.p)
        self.assertTrue(any("data_file" in x for x in probs))

    def test_merge_keeps_other_sources_and_refuses_clashes(self):
        a = self.put("a.json", [rec("R-A0-001", "A0", "observed", self.A)])
        b = self.put("b.json", [rec("R-A1-001", "A1", "prespecified", self.B)])
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.import_results(self.p, b, merge=True), (1, []))
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.import_results(self.p, a, merge=True), (1, []))
        self.assertEqual(self.ids(), ["R-A0-001", "R-A1-001"])
        a2 = self.put("a.json", [rec("R-A0-001", "A0", "observed", self.A), rec("R-A0-002", "A0", "observed", self.A)])
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.import_results(self.p, a2, merge=True), (2, []))
        self.assertEqual(self.ids(), ["R-A0-001", "R-A0-002", "R-A1-001"])
        clash = self.put("c.json", [rec("R-A1-001", "A1", "prespecified", self.A)])
        n, probs = results_ledger.import_results(self.p, clash, merge=True)
        self.assertEqual(n, 0)
        self.assertTrue(any("R-A1-001" in x and "another source" in x for x in probs))
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.import_results(self.p, b, accept_relabel=True), (1, []))  # P2 mode replaces all
        self.assertEqual(self.ids(), ["R-A1-001"])

    def test_merge_cli_flag(self):
        b = self.put("b.json", [rec("R-A1-001", "A1", "prespecified", self.B)])
        code, out, err = quiet(results_ledger.main, ["import", str(self.p), "--from", str(b), "--merge"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, err)

    def test_resultlog_writes_p2_lines(self):
        log = resultlog.ResultLog(self.p, "01_case/facts.csv", "05_analysis/output/t-log.txt", "test")
        rid = log.add("A1", "prespecified", "pooled RR", 0.7512345678912, "rr", ci=[0.5, 1.1], p=0.04, n=120, count=3)
        self.assertEqual(rid, "R-A1-001")
        self.assertEqual(log.add("A1", "prespecified", "absolute", 12, "absolute", number=901), "R-A1-901")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            log.add("A1", "prespecified", "again", 1, "absolute", number=901)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            log.add("A1", "observed", "x", 1, "n")
        path = log.save("05_analysis/output/t.json")
        lines = (self.p / "05_analysis" / "output" / "t-log.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        self.assertEqual(lines[0], "R-A1-001 pooled RR: value=0.7512345679 ci=0.5,1.1 p=0.04 n=120 count=3")
        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(results_ledger.validate_doc(doc, self.p, verify_raw=True), [])
        self.assertEqual(resultlog.num_str(3), "3")
        self.assertEqual(resultlog.num_str(-0.0), "0")


class TestAnalysisPlanP5(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

        self.assertEqual(analysis_plan.check(self.p, []), [])

        self.write()
        found = analysis_plan.check(self.p, [])
        self.assertTrue(any(s == "block" and "observed" in m for s, _, m in found), found)

    def test_missing_result_blocks_at_the_register_file(self):
        self.recs = self.recs[:1]
        self.write()
        found = analysis_plan.check(self.p, [])
        self.assertIn(("block", "03_protocol/sr-protocol.md"), [(s, loc) for s, loc, _ in found])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`crolib/numfmt.py` — replace the PRECISION line and add the override; in `render` replace `d = _precision(kind)` with `d = _decimals(rec, kind)`:

```python
PRECISION = {"n": 0, "count": 0, "percent": 1, "mean": 1, "sd": 1, "median": 1, "iqr": 1,
             "md": 2, "or": 2, "rr": 2, "hr": 2, "rd": 2, "beta": 2, "auc": 3, "p": 3, "other": 2,
             "smd": 2, "tau2": 3, "absolute": 0, "measure": 1}


def _decimals(rec: dict, kind: str) -> int:
    """A record's own `decimals` (int 0-4, case facts) wins over the kind's precision."""
    d = rec.get("decimals")
    if isinstance(d, int) and not isinstance(d, bool) and 0 <= d <= 4:
        return d
    return _precision(kind)
```

```python
KINDS = frozenset({"n", "percent", "mean", "sd", "median", "iqr", "md", "or", "rr", "hr", "rd", "beta", "auc",
                   "p", "other", "smd", "tau2", "absolute", "measure"})
```

`crolib/resultlog.py` (new):

```python
"""Stdlib twin of crostats.Recorder (P2 log format) for P5 tools: numbered results R-A<n>-NNN, one log line each,
saved as a log file plus a results document that `results_ledger.py import --merge` reads (plan D1, D6)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as _dt
import hashlib
import json
import math
import platform
import re
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from pathlib import Path

from . import numfmt

STATUSES = ("prespecified", "posthoc", "deviation", "observed")


def num_str(v) -> str:
    """ints as is; floats with 10 significant digits, half up, positional (crostats._num_str)."""
    if isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool):
        return str(v)
    d = Decimal(repr(float(v)))
    if d == 0:
        return "0"
    q = d.quantize(Decimal(1).scaleb(min(d.adjusted() - 9, 0)), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
    s = format(q, "f")
    if "." in s:
        s = s.rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
    return "0" if s in ("-0", "") else s


def _finite(x):
    try:
        f = float(x)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return f if math.isfinite(f) else None


class ResultLog:
    def __init__(self, project, data_file: str, log_rel: str, engine: str):
        self.project = Path(project)
        self.data_file = data_file
        self.log_rel = log_rel
        self.engine = engine
        self.lines: list[str] = []
        self.results: list[dict] = []
        self._next: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._used: set[str] = set()

    def log(self, text) -> int:
        parts = str(text).splitlines() or [""]
        first = len(self.lines) + 1
        self.lines.extend(parts)
        return first

    def add(self, analysis_id, status, label, value, kind, *, ci=None, p=None, n=None, count=None, unit="",
            number=None, decimals=None, extra=None) -> str:
        if status not in STATUSES:
            raise ValueError(f"status '{status}' not in {STATUSES}")
        if not (isinstance(analysis_id, str) and re.fullmatch(r"A\d+", analysis_id)):
            raise ValueError(f"analysis_id '{analysis_id}' must be A<n>")
        if status == "observed" and analysis_id != "A0":
            raise ValueError("status 'observed' is only for A0 (recorded data, not an analysis)")
        if kind not in numfmt.KINDS:
            raise ValueError(f"kind '{kind}' not in {sorted(numfmt.KINDS)}")
        label = " ".join(str(label).split())
        if not label:
            raise ValueError("label is empty")
        v = _finite(value)
        if v is None:
            raise ValueError(f"{label}: value {value!r} is not a finite number")
        if kind in ("n", "absolute") and v == int(v):
            v = int(v)
        k = number if number is not None else self._next.get(analysis_id, 0) + 1
        if not (isinstance(k, int) and 1 <= k <= 999):
            raise ValueError(f"{label}: result number {k!r} must be 1-999")
        rid = f"R-{analysis_id}-{k:03d}"
        if rid in self._used:
            raise ValueError(f"{rid} is already used")
        rec = {"id": rid, "analysis_id": analysis_id, "status": status, "label": label, "value": v}
        line = f"{rid} {label}: value={num_str(v)}"
        if ci is not None:
            lo, hi = _finite(ci[0]), _finite(ci[1])
            if lo is None or hi is None or lo > hi:
                raise ValueError(f"{label}: ci {ci!r} must be finite [lo, hi] with lo <= hi")
            rec["ci"] = [lo, hi]
            line += f" ci={num_str(lo)},{num_str(hi)}"
        if p is not None:
            pv = _finite(p)
            if pv is None or not 0 <= pv <= 1:
                raise ValueError(f"{label}: p {p!r} must be between 0 and 1")
            rec["p"] = pv
            line += f" p={num_str(pv)}"
        if n is not None:
            rec["n"] = int(n)
            line += f" n={int(n)}"
        if count is not None:
            rec["count"] = int(count)
            line += f" count={int(count)}"
        rec["unit"] = str(unit)
        rec["kind"] = kind
        if decimals is not None:
            rec["decimals"] = int(decimals)
        for key, val in (extra or {}).items():
            rec[key] = val
        lineno = self.log(line)
        rec.update({"source_file": self.log_rel, "locator": f"line {lineno}", "raw": line})
        self._used.add(rid)
        self._next[analysis_id] = max(k, self._next.get(analysis_id, 0))
        self.results.append(rec)
        return rid

    def save(self, results_rel: str) -> Path:
        log_path = self.project / self.log_rel
        log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
            fh.write("\n".join(self.lines) + "\n")
        data = self.project / self.data_file
        doc = {"engine": f"{self.engine}; Python {platform.python_version()}", "data_file": self.data_file,
               "data_sha256": hashlib.sha256(data.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if data.is_file() else "",
               "run_at": _dt.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"), "results": self.results, "tables": []}
        path = self.project / results_rel
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(doc, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
        return path


def import_merged(project, doc_path, accept_relabel: bool = False) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    import results_ledger  # scripts folder is on sys.path for every caller
    return results_ledger.import_results(Path(project), Path(doc_path), accept_relabel=accept_relabel, merge=True)
```

```python
        probs.append("document: data_file must be a de-identified 04_data/*.deid.csv file or one of "
                     + ", ".join(DATA_FILES))
```

Replace `import_results` with:

```python
def _order_key(rid) -> tuple:
    m = re.fullmatch(r"R-A(\d+)-(\d{3})", rid or "")
    return (int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))) if m else (10 ** 9, 0)


def import_results(project: Path, src: Path | None = None, verify_raw: bool = False,
                   accept_relabel: bool = False, merge: bool = False) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    src = src or project / "05_analysis" / "output" / "results.json"
    doc = json.loads(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    probs = validate_doc(doc, project, verify_raw)
    if probs:
        return 0, probs
    existing = project / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json"
    incoming = [dict(r, data_sha256=doc.get("data_sha256", ""), locked=False) for r in doc["results"]]
    old = json.loads(existing.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) if existing.is_file() else {"results": []}
    old_results = [r for r in (old.get("results", []) if isinstance(old, dict) else []) if isinstance(r, dict)]
    if merge:
        sources = {r["source_file"] for r in incoming}
        kept = [r for r in old_results if r.get("source_file") not in sources]
        clash = sorted({r.get("id") for r in kept} & {r["id"] for r in incoming})
        if clash:
            return 0, [f"{rid}: already in the ledger from another source file; not merged" for rid in clash]
        records = sorted(kept + incoming, key=lambda r: _order_key(r.get("id")))
        compare = {"results": [r for r in old_results if r.get("source_file") in sources]}
    else:
        records, compare = incoming, old
    new_bytes = (json.dumps({"results": records}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    if existing.is_file() and existing.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n") == new_bytes:
        return len(incoming), []  # same content (any line ending): write nothing, so a G3 lock stays valid
    if _g3_locks_ledger(project):
        raise LockedError("G3 is locked; the ledger is unchanged — re-run only after the author re-opens "
                          "G3 for a new analysis")
    if existing.is_file() and not accept_relabel:
        changes = relabel_changes(compare, doc["results"])
        if changes:
            raise RelabelError("\n".join(changes))
    existing.write_bytes(new_bytes)  # LF only, whatever the platform
    return len(incoming), []
```

In `main` add `ap.add_argument("--merge", action="store_true")` and call `import_results(project, src, a.verify_raw, a.accept_relabel, a.merge)`.

```python
def register_path(project: Path) -> Path:
    """03_protocol/sap.md, else the review protocol 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md (P5 D3)."""
    sap = Path(project) / "03_protocol" / "sap.md"
    srp = Path(project) / "03_protocol" / "sr-protocol.md"
    return srp if not sap.is_file() and srp.is_file() else sap


def results_records(project: Path) -> list:
    """05_analysis/output/results.json, else ledgers/results-ledger.json (reviews and case reports)."""
    rj = Path(project) / "05_analysis" / "output" / "results.json"
    led = Path(project) / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json"
    return _load_results(led if not rj.is_file() and led.is_file() else rj)
```

`case_facts.py` (new):

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Case facts → results ledger (A0, status observed), so drafts cite {{R-A0-NNN}} and E-NUM works.

  python case_facts.py <project> [--accept-relabel]

Reads 01_case/facts.csv (id,label,value,unit,day,source), one row per reportable value; `day` is relative
(0, +3, -14), never a date. Kind from the unit: % → percent; an empty/count unit with an integer → n; else
measure, printed with the decimals written in the CSV. Writes 05_analysis/output/case-facts-log.txt and
case-facts.json and merges them into ledgers/results-ledger.json as R-A0-001… in row order.
Exit codes: 0 = written (or unchanged); 1 = invalid rows (listed, nothing written), the ledger is locked by G3,
or a label change refused; 2 = usage error, missing project/file or missing column.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import resultlog  # noqa: E402
import results_ledger  # noqa: E402

FACTS = "01_case/facts.csv"
LOG_REL = "05_analysis/output/case-facts-log.txt"
DOC_REL = "05_analysis/output/case-facts.json"
COLUMNS = ("id", "label", "value", "unit", "day", "source")
COUNT_UNITS = {"", "n", "cases", "case", "ca", "lần", "times", "episodes", "đợt"}
NUMBER = re.compile(r"-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?")
DAY = re.compile(r"[+-]?\d{1,5}")


def _decimals(text: str) -> int:
    t = text.strip().replace(",", ".")
    return len(t.split(".")[1]) if "." in t else 0


def kind_for(unit: str, text: str) -> tuple[str, int | None]:
    u = unit.strip().casefold()
    if u in ("%", "percent", "phần trăm"):
        return "percent", _decimals(text)
    if u in COUNT_UNITS and _decimals(text) == 0:
        return "n", None
    return "measure", _decimals(text)


def read_rows(project: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], list[str]]:
    path = project / FACTS
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
        reader = csv.DictReader(fh)
        missing = [c for c in COLUMNS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or [])]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"{FACTS} lacks column(s) {', '.join(missing)} (needs {','.join(COLUMNS)})")
        rows = list(reader)
    probs, seen = [], set()
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, start=2):
        fid = (r.get("id") or "").strip() or f"line {i}"
        if fid in seen:
            probs.append(f"{fid}: duplicate id ({FACTS}:{i})")
        seen.add(fid)
        if not (r.get("label") or "").strip():
            probs.append(f"{fid}: label is empty ({FACTS}:{i})")
        if not NUMBER.fullmatch((r.get("value") or "").strip()):
            probs.append(f"{fid}: value must be a number, got {r.get('value')!r} ({FACTS}:{i})")
        day = (r.get("day") or "").strip()
        if day and not DAY.fullmatch(day):
            probs.append(f"{fid}: day must be relative (0, +3, -14), never a date; got {day!r} ({FACTS}:{i})")
    return rows, probs


def build(project: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:
    log = resultlog.ResultLog(project, FACTS, LOG_REL, "case_facts.py")
    mapping = []
    for r in rows:
        fid, text, unit = r["id"].strip(), r["value"].strip(), (r.get("unit") or "").strip()
        kind, dec = kind_for(unit, text)
        day = (r.get("day") or "").strip()
        extra = {"day": int(day)} if day else {}
        rid = log.add("A0", "observed", f"{r['label'].strip()} [{fid}]", float(text.replace(",", ".")), kind,
                      unit=unit, decimals=dec, extra=extra)
        mapping.append(f"{fid} → {rid} ({kind}{'' if dec is None else f', {dec} dp'})")
    return log.save(DOC_REL), mapping


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("--accept-relabel", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "ledgers").is_dir():
        print(f"case_facts: {a.project} is not a project folder (no ledgers/)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        rows, probs = read_rows(project)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f"case_facts: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if probs:
        for x in probs:
            print(x)
        print(f"case_facts: {len(probs)} problems; nothing written")
        return 1
    try:
        doc, mapping = build(project, rows)
        n, bad = resultlog.import_merged(project, doc, a.accept_relabel)
    except results_ledger.LockedError as e:
        print(f"case_facts: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except results_ledger.RelabelError as e:
        print(str(e), file=sys.stderr)
        print("case_facts: labels changed; ledger not written (re-run with --accept-relabel)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f"case_facts: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if bad:
        for x in bad:
            print(x)
        return 1
    for line in mapping:
        print(line)
    print(f"case_facts: {n} facts in the results ledger (A0, observed)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```python
class TestP5ItemLists(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = init_project.create(self.t / "p", "T", "systematic-review", "systematic-review", "en", "en")

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_item_lists_load_with_official_ids(self):
        for code, (version, ids, sections) in P5_LISTS.items():
            with self.subTest(guideline=code):
                data = gc.load_items(code)
                self.assertEqual((data["code"], data["version"]), (code, version))
                self.assertEqual([i["id"] for i in data["items"]], ids)
                for it in data["items"]:
                    self.assertIn(it["section"], sections)
                    self.assertTrue(0 < len(it["text"].split()) <= 12, it)
                    self.assertTrue(it["text"].endswith("."), it)

    def test_guideline_records_are_verified(self):
        lib = json.loads((gc.ITEMS_DIR.parent / "guidelines.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        by = {r["id"]: r for r in lib["references"]}
        index = (gc.ITEMS_DIR.parent / "index.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for code, rid in P5_RECORDS.items():
            with self.subTest(guideline=code):
                self.assertTrue(str(by[rid].get("verified_by", "")).endswith("VERIFIED"))
                self.assertIn(f"| {code} |", index)
                self.assertIn(f"items/{code}.json", index)

    def test_checklist_runs_for_each_p5_guideline(self):
        for code, (_, ids, _) in P5_LISTS.items():
            with self.subTest(guideline=code):
                path = self.p / "reviews" / f"cl-{code}.json"
                items = {i: {"status": "na", "reason": "fixture"} for i in ids}
                path.write_text(json.dumps({"guideline": code, "items": items}), encoding="utf-8")
                self.assertEqual(gc.check(self.p, guideline=code, checklist_path=path), [])
                del items[ids[-1]]
                path.write_text(json.dumps({"guideline": code, "items": items}), encoding="utf-8")
                found = gc.check(self.p, guideline=code, checklist_path=path)
                self.assertTrue(any(s == "block" and f"item {ids[-1]}" in m for s, _, m in found), found)

    def test_notice_rows(self):
        notice = (_paths.ROOT / "NOTICE.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for code in P5_LISTS:
            self.assertIn(f"`references/reporting-guidelines/items/{code}.json`", notice)
```

```python
"""Shared fixtures for the P5 unit tests. Everything here is fictional."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path

import gate
import init_project

PROTOCOL = """# Review protocol (fictional, for unit tests)

## Full-text exclusion reasons
- wrong population
- wrong intervention
- wrong outcome
- wrong study design

## Analysis register

| ID | Type | Outcome | Timepoint | Measure | Model | HKSJ | Experimental | Comparator | Factor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | counts | PRISMA flow | | | | | | | |
| A1 | pool | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |
"""


def set_reviewers(p: Path, names: list[str]) -> None:
    y = (p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    line = "  reviewers: [" + ", ".join(names) + "]"
    if re.search(r"(?m)^  reviewers:.*$", y):
        y = re.sub(r"(?m)^  reviewers:.*$", lambda m: line, y)
    else:
        y = y.rstrip("\n") + "\nreview:\n" + line + "\n"
    (p / "project.yaml").write_text(y, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")


def review_project(root: Path, design: str = "systematic-review", reviewers=("Reviewer A", "Reviewer B"),
                   g2: bool = True) -> Path:
    ptype = "systematic-review" if design == "systematic-review" else "review"
    p = init_project.create(Path(root) / "p", "Fictional review", ptype, design, "en", "en")
    for d in ("02_evidence/sr", "04_data/sr", "05_analysis/meta", "06_writing/review"):
        (p / d).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (p / "03_protocol" / "sr-protocol.md").write_text(PROTOCOL, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    set_reviewers(p, list(reviewers))
    if g2:
        gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by="Example author", files=["03_protocol/sr-protocol.md"])
    return p


def case_project(root: Path) -> Path:
    p = init_project.create(Path(root) / "c", "Fictional case", "case-report", "case-report", "en", "en")
    for d in ("01_case", "06_writing/case-report"):
        (p / d).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return p
```

```python
import contextlib

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import screening
from crolib import projectcfg
from p5_helpers import review_project, set_reviewers

TI  - Fictional trial of drug X in adults
AU  - Alpha, A
AU  - Beta, B
PY  - 2020
JO  - Fictional Journal
DO  - 10.0000/fictional.1
AB  - Fictional abstract.
ER  - 
TY  - JOUR
TI  - Fictional cohort of drug X
AU  - Gamma, C
PY  - 2021
JO  - Fictional Journal
AB  - Another fictional abstract.
ER  - 
"""
NBIB = """PMID- 99999901
TI  - Fictional trial of drug X in adults.
AU  - Alpha A
DP  - 2020 Mar
JT  - Fictional Journal
LID - 10.0000/fictional.1 [doi]
AB  - Fictional abstract that wraps
      onto a second line.

PMID- 99999902
TI  - Fictional registry study of drug X.
AU  - Delta D
DP  - 2019
JT  - Fictional Journal
"""
CSVX = ("Title,Authors,Year,Journal,DOI\nFictional cohort of drug X,Gamma C,2021,Fictional Journal,\n"
        "Fictional unrelated report,Epsilon E,2022,Fictional Journal,\n")
SEARCH_LOG = ("database,platform,date_run,string_id,hits,export_file\n"
              "Embase,Ovid,2026-11-01,S2,2,embase.ris\nPubMed,PubMed,2026-11-01,S1,2,pubmed.nbib\n")


def cli(*argv):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        code = screening.main([str(a) for a in argv])
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


class TestProjectcfgLists(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_parse_list_and_get(self):
        self.assertEqual(projectcfg.parse_list("[Reviewer A, 'Reviewer B']"), ["Reviewer A", "Reviewer B"])
        for empty in ("", "[]", "null", "~"):
            self.assertEqual(projectcfg.parse_list(empty), [])
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            (Path(t) / "project.yaml").write_text("tools:\n  rscript: null\ntarget:\n  journal_style: bmj\n",
                                                  encoding="utf-8")
            self.assertIsNone(projectcfg.get(Path(t), "tools.rscript"))
            self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(Path(t), "target.journal_style"), "bmj")
            self.assertEqual(projectcfg.get(Path(t), "missing.key", "x"), "x")


class TestScreening(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = review_project(self.t)
        ex = self.t / "exports"
        ex.mkdir()
        for name, text in (("embase.ris", RIS), ("pubmed.nbib", NBIB), ("other.csv", CSVX)):
            (ex / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / screening.SEARCH_LOG).write_text(SEARCH_LOG, encoding="utf-8")
        self.ex = ex

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def load(self):
        for name, db in (("embase.ris", "Embase"), ("pubmed.nbib", "PubMed"), ("other.csv", "Other")):
            code, out, err = cli(self.p, "import", self.ex / name, "--database", db)
            self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "dedupe")[0], 0)

    def decide(self, stage, reviewer, rec, decision, *extra):
        return cli(self.p, "decide", "--stage", stage, "--reviewer", reviewer, "--rec", rec, "--decision", decision, *extra)

    def rows(self):
        return screening.read_csv(self.p / screening.RECORDS)

    def test_import_parses_and_dedupe_marks(self):
        code, out, _ = cli(self.p, "import", self.ex / "embase.ris", "--database", "Embase")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertIn("2 records imported", out)
        cli(self.p, "import", self.ex / "pubmed.nbib", "--database", "PubMed")
        code, out, _ = cli(self.p, "import", self.ex / "other.csv", "--database", "Other")
        self.assertIn("E-SCR warn 02_evidence/sr/search-log.csv", out)
        self.assertIn("other: 0 hits logged, 2 records imported", out)
        rows = {r["rec_id"]: r for r in self.rows()}
        self.assertEqual(list(rows), [f"rec-000{i}" for i in range(1, 7)])
        self.assertEqual(rows["rec-0003"]["pmid"], "99999901")
        self.assertEqual(rows["rec-0003"]["doi"], "10.0000/fictional.1")
        self.assertEqual(rows["rec-0003"]["abstract"], "Fictional abstract that wraps onto a second line.")
        self.assertEqual(rows["rec-0001"]["authors"], "Alpha, A; Beta, B")
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "dedupe")[0], 0)
        dup = {r["rec_id"]: r["duplicate_of"] for r in self.rows()}
        self.assertEqual(dup, {"rec-0001": "", "rec-0002": "", "rec-0003": "rec-0001", "rec-0004": "",
                               "rec-0005": "rec-0002", "rec-0006": ""})

    def test_decide_refused_while_g2_closed(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t / "p")
        self.p = review_project(self.t, g2=False)
        self.load()
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ta", "Reviewer A", "rec-0001", "include")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("G2: not open", out)
        self.assertFalse((self.p / screening.SCREENING).exists())

    def test_reviewer_rules(self):
        self.load()
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ta", "Someone Else", "rec-0001", "include")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("not in project.yaml review.reviewers", out)
        set_reviewers(self.p, ["Reviewer A", "Claude"])
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ta", "Claude", "rec-0001", "include")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("looks like an AI", out)
        msgs = [m for _, _, m in screening.check(self.p)]
        self.assertTrue(any("looks like an AI" in m for m in msgs))
        self.assertTrue(any("1 human reviewer" in m for m in msgs))
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ta", "Reviewer A", "rec-0003", "include")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("duplicate of rec-0001", out)

    def test_full_flow_conflicts_kappa_counts_and_check(self):
        self.load()
        for rec, a, b in (("rec-0001", "include", "include"), ("rec-0002", "include", "exclude"),
                          ("rec-0004", "exclude", "exclude"), ("rec-0006", "exclude", "exclude")):
            self.assertEqual(self.decide("ta", "Reviewer A", rec, a)[0], 0)
            self.assertEqual(self.decide("ta", "Reviewer B", rec, b)[0], 0)
        code, out, _ = cli(self.p, "conflicts", "--stage", "ta")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("rec-0002: conflict: Reviewer A include, Reviewer B exclude", out)
        code, out, _ = cli(self.p, "kappa", "--stage", "ta")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertIn("kappa ta: 0.50 (n = 4; agreement 75.0%)", out)   # po 3/4, pe 0.5x0.25 + 0.5x0.75
        self.assertEqual(self.decide("ta", "consensus", "rec-0002", "include")[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "conflicts", "--stage", "ta")[0], 0)

        self.assertIn("not decided by Reviewer A, Reviewer B", out)
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ft", "Reviewer A", "rec-0004", "include")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("did not pass title/abstract screening", out)
        self.assertEqual(self.decide("ft", "Reviewer A", "rec-0002", "exclude")[0], 1)
        code, out, _ = self.decide("ft", "Reviewer A", "rec-0002", "exclude", "--reason", "wrong colour")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("not in the protocol's full-text exclusion reasons", out)

            self.assertEqual(self.decide("ft", rv, "rec-0002", "exclude", "--reason", "wrong population")[0], 0)

                    "R-A0-007": 0, "R-A0-008": 2, "R-A0-009": 1, "R-A0-010": 1, "R-A0-011": 1,
                    "R-A0-021": 2, "R-A0-022": 2, "R-A0-023": 2, "R-A0-051": 1}
        self.assertEqual(got, expected)
        labels = {r["id"]: r["label"] for r in led}
        self.assertEqual((labels["R-A0-021"], labels["R-A0-051"]),
                         ("records identified: Embase", "reports excluded: wrong population"))
        found = screening.check(self.p)
        self.assertEqual([f for f in found if f[0] == "block"], [])
        self.assertEqual(self.decide("ft", "consensus", "rec-0002", "include", "--study", "S02")[0], 0)
        found = screening.check(self.p)
        self.assertTrue(any(s == "block" and "differ from a fresh counts run" in m for s, _, m in found), found)

    def test_check_blocks_missing_decisions_and_reasons(self):
        self.load()
        self.decide("ta", "Reviewer A", "rec-0001", "include")

    def test_bulk_decide_is_all_or_nothing(self):
        self.load()

        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "decide", "--stage", "ta", "--reviewer", "Reviewer A")[0], 2)

            cli(self.p, "frobnicate")
```
