`crolib/projectcfg.py` — append:

```python
NULLS = ("", "null", "~", "Null", "NULL")


def parse_list(val) -> list[str]:
    """A one-line YAML flow list ("[a, 'b c']") as strings; "", null, ~ and [] give []. Items cannot contain commas."""
    if not isinstance(val, str):
        return []
    v = val.strip()
    if v in NULLS or v == "[]":
        return []
    if not (v.startswith("[") and v.endswith("]")):
        return [_clean(v)]
    return [_clean(x.strip()) for x in v[1:-1].split(",") if x.strip()]


def get(project: Path, dotted: str, default=None):
    """Value of a top-level or one-level nested key ("tools.rscript"); "", null and ~ give `default`."""
    cur = read_project_yaml(project)
    for part in dotted.split("."):
        if not isinstance(cur, dict) or part not in cur:
            return default
        cur = cur[part]
    if isinstance(cur, str) and cur.strip() in NULLS:
        return default
    return cur
```

`screening.py` (new):

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Screening log for systematic and scoping reviews (PRISMA 2020 / PRISMA-ScR flow).

  python screening.py <project> import <file> --database NAME
  python screening.py <project> dedupe
  python screening.py <project> decide --stage ta|ft --reviewer NAME --rec REC --decision include|exclude|maybe
                                        [--reason TEXT] [--study ID]
  python screening.py <project> decide --stage ta|ft --reviewer NAME --file SHEET.csv
  python screening.py <project> sheet --stage ta|ft --reviewer NAME
  python screening.py <project> suggest --stage ta|ft --from FILE
  python screening.py <project> conflicts --stage ta|ft
  python screening.py <project> kappa --stage ta|ft
  python screening.py <project> counts
  python screening.py <project> check

Files in 02_evidence/sr/: records.csv (rec_id,database,pmid,doi,title,authors,year,journal,abstract,duplicate_of);
screening.csv (stage,rec_id,reviewer,decision,reason,study,at; append-only, the latest row per stage, record and
reviewer counts); ai-suggestions.csv (never read as a decision); search-log.csv
(database,platform,date_run,string_id,hits,export_file). import reads RIS (.ris), PubMed MEDLINE (.nbib/.txt) or
CSV and warns (E-SCR) when the records per database differ from the hits in search-log.csv. dedupe marks duplicates
by DOI or PMID, then by normalised title + year + first author (both rows stay). decide is refused while gate G2 is
not open, for a reviewer not in project.yaml review.reviewers (AI names never count), for a duplicate, for a
full-text decision on a record that did not pass title/abstract screening, and for a full-text exclusion without a
reason from the protocol's "## Full-text exclusion reasons" list (or "not retrieved"); reviewer "consensus"
resolves a disagreement. sheet writes reviews/tmp/screening-<stage>-<reviewer>.csv with the AI suggestion as a
separate column; decide --file reads its decision column back (blank rows skipped; all rows or none). suggest
validates a role-briefed AI pass into ai-suggestions.csv. counts writes the PRISMA flow as results-ledger records
R-A0-NNN (status observed) and refuses while a reached record is unresolved. check prints the E-SCR findings.
Exit codes: 0 = done / clean; 1 = refused, conflicts listed, or E-SCR block findings; 2 = usage or I/O error.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import datetime as dt
import io
import json
import re
import sys
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import projectcfg, resultlog  # noqa: E402
import gate  # noqa: E402
import results_ledger  # noqa: E402

SR = "02_evidence/sr"
RECORDS = f"{SR}/records.csv"
SCREENING = f"{SR}/screening.csv"
SUGGESTIONS = f"{SR}/ai-suggestions.csv"
SEARCH_LOG = f"{SR}/search-log.csv"
PROTOCOL = "03_protocol/sr-protocol.md"
LOG_REL = "05_analysis/output/screening-log.txt"
DOC_REL = "05_analysis/output/screening-counts.json"
RECORD_COLS = ["rec_id", "database", "pmid", "doi", "title", "authors", "year", "journal", "abstract", "duplicate_of"]
SCREEN_COLS = ["stage", "rec_id", "reviewer", "decision", "reason", "study", "at"]
SUGGEST_COLS = ["stage", "rec_id", "suggestion", "reason", "source"]
SHEET_COLS = ["rec_id", "title", "year", "journal", "abstract", "ai_suggestion", "ai_reason", "decision", "reason", "study"]
STAGES = ("ta", "ft")
DECISIONS = ("include", "exclude", "maybe")
NOT_RETRIEVED = "not retrieved"
CONSENSUS = "consensus"
AI_NAME = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:ai|claude|gpt|chatgpt|assistant|model|llm|bot)\b")
REASONS_HEAD = re.compile(r"(?im)^#{2,3}\s*(?:full-text exclusion reasons|lý do loại trừ (?:ở bước )?toàn văn)\s*$")
REGISTER_NAME = re.compile(r"(?i)regist|clinicaltrials|ictrp")
RIS_TAG = re.compile(r"^([A-Z][A-Z0-9])  -\s?(.*)$")
NB_TAG = re.compile(r"^([A-Z]{2,4})\s*- (.*)$")
CSV_KEYS = {"title": ("title",), "authors": ("authors", "author"), "year": ("year", "publication year", "py"),
            "journal": ("journal", "source", "source title", "journal/book"), "abstract": ("abstract",),
            "doi": ("doi",), "pmid": ("pmid", "pubmed id")}
FIXED = [  # PRISMA 2020 flow boxes, ids R-A0-001..011 (plan D10)
    ("databases", "records identified from databases"),
    ("registers", "records identified from registers"),
    ("duplicates", "duplicate records removed"),
    ("screened", "records screened"),
    ("excluded", "records excluded at title and abstract"),
    ("sought", "reports sought for retrieval"),
    ("not_retrieved", "reports not retrieved"),
    ("assessed", "reports assessed for eligibility"),
    ("reports_excluded", "reports excluded at full text"),
    ("studies", "studies included"),
    ("reports", "reports included"),
]


class Refused(Exception):
    """An action the screening rules do not allow (exit 1)."""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- files

def read_csv(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    if not Path(path).is_file():
        return []
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def write_csv(path: Path, cols: list[str], rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=cols, extrasaction="ignore", lineterminator="\n")
        w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow({c: r.get(c, "") for c in cols})


def append_csv(path: Path, cols: list[str], rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    new = not path.is_file()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=cols, extrasaction="ignore", lineterminator="\n")
        if new:
            w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow({c: r.get(c, "") for c in cols})


def slug(name: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", name.casefold()).strip("-")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- import

def _clean_doi(s: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"(?i)^(?:https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/|doi:\s*)", "", (s or "").strip())


def _first(d: dict, *tags: str) -> str:
    for t in tags:
        if d.get(t):
            return d[t][0]
    return ""


def _year(text: str) -> str:
    m = re.search(r"\b(1[89]\d\d|20\d\d)\b", text or "")
    return m.group(1) if m else ""


def parse_ris(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    recs, cur, last = [], {}, None
    for line in text.splitlines():
        m = RIS_TAG.match(line)
        if not m:
            if last and line.strip() and cur:
                cur[last][-1] += " " + line.strip()
            continue
        tag, val = m.group(1), m.group(2).strip()
        if tag == "ER":
            if cur:
                recs.append(cur)
            cur, last = {}, None
            continue
        cur.setdefault(tag, []).append(val)
        last = tag
    if cur:
        recs.append(cur)
    return [{"pmid": "", "doi": _clean_doi(_first(d, "DO")), "title": _first(d, "TI", "T1"),
             "authors": "; ".join(d.get("AU") or d.get("A1") or []), "year": _year(_first(d, "PY", "Y1", "DA")),
             "journal": _first(d, "JO", "T2", "JF", "JA"), "abstract": _first(d, "AB", "N2")} for d in recs]


def parse_nbib(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    recs, cur, last = [], {}, None
    for line in text.splitlines() + [""]:
        if not line.strip():
            if cur:
                recs.append(cur)
            cur, last = {}, None
            continue
        if line.startswith("      ") and last:
            cur[last][-1] += " " + line.strip()
            continue
        m = NB_TAG.match(line)
        if m:
            last = m.group(1)
            cur.setdefault(last, []).append(m.group(2).strip())
    out = []
    for d in recs:
        doi = next((_clean_doi(v[:-5]) for v in d.get("LID", []) + d.get("AID", []) if v.endswith("[doi]")), "")
        out.append({"pmid": _first(d, "PMID"), "doi": doi, "title": _first(d, "TI"), "authors": "; ".join(d.get("AU", [])),
                    "year": _year(_first(d, "DP")), "journal": _first(d, "JT", "TA"), "abstract": _first(d, "AB")})
    return out


def parse_csv(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for r in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text)):
        low = {(k or "").strip().casefold(): (v or "").strip() for k, v in r.items()}
        rec = {key: next((low[n] for n in names if low.get(n)), "") for key, names in CSV_KEYS.items()}
        rec["doi"] = _clean_doi(rec["doi"])
        rec["year"] = _year(rec["year"])
        out.append(rec)
    return out


def hits_findings(project: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    log = read_csv(Path(project) / SEARCH_LOG)
    if not log:
        return [("warn", f"{SEARCH_LOG}:1", "no search log: record database, platform, date, string and hits (PRISMA-S)")]
    found, logged, lines, per = [], {}, {}, {}
    for r in rows:
        db = (r.get("database") or "").strip().casefold()
        per[db] = per.get(db, 0) + 1
    for i, r in enumerate(log, start=2):
        db = (r.get("database") or "").strip().casefold()
        try:
            hits = int((r.get("hits") or "").strip())
        except ValueError:
            found.append(("warn", f"{SEARCH_LOG}:{i}", f"hits {r.get('hits')!r} is not a whole number"))
            continue
        logged[db] = logged.get(db, 0) + hits
        lines.setdefault(db, i)
    for db in sorted(set(per) | set(logged)):
        if per.get(db, 0) != logged.get(db, 0):
            found.append(("warn", f"{SEARCH_LOG}:{lines.get(db, 1)}",
                          f"{db}: {logged.get(db, 0)} hits logged, {per.get(db, 0)} records imported"))
    return found


def import_file(project: Path, path: Path, database: str) -> tuple[int, list]:
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    if suffix == ".ris":
        recs = parse_ris(text)
    elif suffix in (".nbib", ".txt"):
        recs = parse_nbib(text)
    elif suffix == ".csv":
        recs = parse_csv(text)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: unknown export format (use .ris, .nbib/.txt or .csv)")
    if not recs:
        raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: no records found")
    rows = read_csv(project / RECORDS)
    start = max((int(r["rec_id"][4:]) for r in rows if re.fullmatch(r"rec-\d+", r.get("rec_id", ""))), default=0)
    for i, r in enumerate(recs, start=start + 1):
        rows.append(dict(r, rec_id=f"rec-{i:04d}", database=database.strip(), duplicate_of=""))
    write_csv(project / RECORDS, RECORD_COLS, rows)
    return len(recs), hits_findings(project, rows)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- dedupe

def norm(text: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "").casefold()
    s = "".join(c for c in s if not unicodedata.combining(c))
    return " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", s).split())


def first_author(authors: str) -> str:
    first = (authors or "").split(";")[0].strip()
    return norm(re.split(r"[,\s]", first)[0]) if first else ""


def dedupe(project: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
    rows = read_csv(project / RECORDS)
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"no {RECORDS}: run screening.py import first")
    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
    marked = 0
    for r in rows:
        keys = []
        if r.get("doi"):
            keys.append("doi:" + r["doi"].casefold())
        if r.get("pmid"):
            keys.append("pmid:" + r["pmid"].strip())
        t = norm(r.get("title", ""))
        if t and r.get("year"):
            keys.append(f"t:{t}|{r['year']}|{first_author(r.get('authors', ''))}")
        orig = next((seen[k] for k in keys if k in seen), None)
        r["duplicate_of"] = orig or ""
        marked += bool(orig)
        for k in keys:
            seen.setdefault(k, orig or r["rec_id"])
    write_csv(project / RECORDS, RECORD_COLS, rows)
    return len(rows), marked


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- decisions

def reviewers(project: Path) -> list[str]:
    rev = projectcfg.read_project_yaml(project).get("review", {})
    return projectcfg.parse_list(rev.get("reviewers", "") if isinstance(rev, dict) else "")


def human_reviewers(project: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [r for r in reviewers(project) if not AI_NAME.search(r)]


def protocol_reasons(project: Path) -> list[str]:
    path = Path(project) / PROTOCOL
    if not path.is_file():
        return []
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    m = REASONS_HEAD.search(text)
    out: list[str] = []
    for line in (text[m.end():].splitlines() if m else []):
        s = line.strip()
        if s.startswith("#"):
            break
        if s.startswith(("- ", "* ")):
            out.append(s[2:].strip().casefold())
        elif s and out:
            break
    return out


def records(project: Path) -> dict[str, dict]:
    return {r["rec_id"]: r for r in read_csv(Path(project) / RECORDS)}


def latest(project: Path) -> dict[tuple, dict]:
    out = {}
    for i, r in enumerate(read_csv(Path(project) / SCREENING), start=2):
        out[(r.get("stage"), r.get("rec_id"), r.get("reviewer"))] = dict(r, _line=i)
    return out


def resolve(project: Path) -> dict[str, dict[str, dict]]:
    """stage -> rec_id -> {"rows": {reviewer: latest row}, "final": include|exclude|None, "why": text}."""
    recs, humans, lat = records(project), human_reviewers(project), latest(project)
    out: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = {"ta": {}, "ft": {}}
    for stage in STAGES:
        if stage == "ta":
            reached = [rid for rid, r in recs.items() if not r.get("duplicate_of")]
        else:
            reached = [rid for rid, e in out["ta"].items() if e["final"] == "include"]
        for rid in reached:
            rows = {rv: lat[(stage, rid, rv)] for rv in humans + [CONSENSUS] if (stage, rid, rv) in lat}
            e = {"rows": rows, "final": None, "why": ""}
            if CONSENSUS in rows:
                d = rows[CONSENSUS].get("decision")
                e["final"], e["why"] = (d, "consensus") if d in ("include", "exclude") else (None, "consensus row says maybe")
            elif len(humans) < 2:
                e["why"] = f"two human reviewers are needed (review.reviewers lists {len(humans)})"
            else:
                missing = [h for h in humans if h not in rows]
                ds = [rows[h]["decision"] for h in humans if h in rows]
                if missing:
                    e["why"] = "not decided by " + ", ".join(missing)
                elif len(set(ds)) == 1 and ds[0] in ("include", "exclude"):
                    e["final"] = ds[0]
                else:
                    e["why"] = "conflict: " + ", ".join(f"{h} {rows[h]['decision']}" for h in humans)
            out[stage][rid] = e
    return out


def decide(project: Path, stage: str, reviewer: str, rows_in: list[dict]) -> int:
    ok, why = gate.check_gate(project, "G2")
    if not ok:
        raise Refused("G2: not open — decisions are recorded only after the author locks the protocol "
                      "(gate.py open . G2 --by NAME --files 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md …): " + "; ".join(why))
    if AI_NAME.search(reviewer):
        raise Refused(f"{reviewer!r} looks like an AI; only the human reviewers named in project.yaml review.reviewers "
                      "record decisions (AI suggestions go to ai-suggestions.csv)")
    allowed = human_reviewers(project)
    if reviewer != CONSENSUS and reviewer not in allowed:
        raise Refused(f"reviewer {reviewer!r} is not in project.yaml review.reviewers ({', '.join(allowed) or 'empty'})")
    recs, reasons = records(project), protocol_reasons(project)
    state = resolve(project) if stage == "ft" else None
    probs = []
    for r in rows_in:
        rid = (r.get("rec_id") or "").strip()
        dec = (r.get("decision") or "").strip().casefold()
        reason = (r.get("reason") or "").strip()
        if rid not in recs:
            probs.append(f"{rid or '(blank)'}: no such record")
            continue
        if recs[rid].get("duplicate_of"):
            probs.append(f"{rid}: duplicate of {recs[rid]['duplicate_of']}; decide on the original")
            continue
        if dec not in DECISIONS:
            raise ValueError(f"{rid}: decision {dec!r} must be include, exclude or maybe")
        if stage == "ft" and state["ta"].get(rid, {}).get("final") != "include":
            probs.append(f"{rid}: did not pass title/abstract screening")
        if stage == "ft" and dec == "exclude":
            if not reason:
                probs.append(f"{rid}: a full-text exclusion needs --reason from the protocol list")
            elif reason.casefold() != NOT_RETRIEVED and reason.casefold() not in reasons:
                probs.append(f"{rid}: reason {reason!r} is not in the protocol's full-text exclusion reasons "
                             f"({'; '.join(reasons) or 'none listed'})")
    if probs:
        raise Refused("\n".join(probs))
    at = dt.date.today().isoformat()
    append_csv(Path(project) / SCREENING, SCREEN_COLS,
               [{"stage": stage, "rec_id": r["rec_id"].strip(), "reviewer": reviewer,
                 "decision": r["decision"].strip().casefold(), "reason": (r.get("reason") or "").strip(),
                 "study": (r.get("study") or "").strip(), "at": at} for r in rows_in])
    return len(rows_in)


def sheet(project: Path, stage: str, reviewer: str) -> Path:
    recs, st = records(project), resolve(project)
    sug = {(r.get("stage"), r.get("rec_id")): r for r in read_csv(Path(project) / SUGGESTIONS)}
    rows = []
    for rid in st[stage]:
        rec, s = recs[rid], sug.get((stage, rid), {})
        rows.append({"rec_id": rid, "title": rec.get("title", ""), "year": rec.get("year", ""),
                     "journal": rec.get("journal", ""), "abstract": rec.get("abstract", ""),
                     "ai_suggestion": s.get("suggestion", ""), "ai_reason": s.get("reason", "")})
    path = Path(project) / "reviews" / "tmp" / f"screening-{stage}-{slug(reviewer)}.csv"
    write_csv(path, SHEET_COLS, rows)
    return path


def suggest(project: Path, stage: str, src: Path) -> int:
    recs, probs, out = records(project), [], []
    for i, r in enumerate(read_csv(src), start=2):
        rid = (r.get("rec_id") or "").strip()
        s = (r.get("suggestion") or "").strip().casefold()
        if rid not in recs:
            probs.append(f"line {i}: {rid or '(blank)'} is not a record")
        if s not in DECISIONS:
            probs.append(f"line {i}: suggestion {s!r} must be include, exclude or maybe")
        out.append({"stage": stage, "rec_id": rid, "suggestion": s, "reason": (r.get("reason") or "").strip(),
                    "source": "AI (role screen-suggest)"})
    if probs:
        raise Refused("\n".join(probs))
    keep = [r for r in read_csv(Path(project) / SUGGESTIONS) if r.get("stage") != stage]
    write_csv(Path(project) / SUGGESTIONS, SUGGEST_COLS, keep + out)
    return len(out)


def kappa(project: Path, stage: str) -> tuple[float | None, int, float]:
    humans = human_reviewers(project)
    if len(humans) != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"kappa needs exactly two reviewers in review.reviewers (found {len(humans)})")
    a, b = humans
    lat = latest(project)
    pairs = [(lat[(stage, rid, a)]["decision"], lat[(stage, rid, b)]["decision"]) for rid in resolve(project)[stage]
             if (stage, rid, a) in lat and (stage, rid, b) in lat]
    n = len(pairs)
    if not n:
        return None, 0, 0.0
    po = sum(x == y for x, y in pairs) / n
    cats = {x for pr in pairs for x in pr}
    pe = sum((sum(x == c for x, _ in pairs) / n) * (sum(y == c for _, y in pairs) / n) for c in cats)
    return (None if pe >= 1 else (po - pe) / (1 - pe)), n, po


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- counts and check

def _final_field(e: dict, field: str) -> str:
    rows = e["rows"]
    if CONSENSUS in rows:
        return (rows[CONSENSUS].get(field) or "").strip()
    return next(((r.get(field) or "").strip() for k, r in rows.items() if k != CONSENSUS and (r.get(field) or "").strip()), "")


def compute_counts(project: Path) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
    recs, st = records(project), resolve(project)
    unresolved = [f"{s} {rid}: {e['why']}" for s in STAGES for rid, e in st[s].items() if e["final"] is None]
    by_db: dict[str, int] = {}
    for r in recs.values():
        by_db[r.get("database", "")] = by_db.get(r.get("database", ""), 0) + 1
    reg = sum(n for d, n in by_db.items() if REGISTER_NAME.search(d))
    reasons: dict[str, int] = {}
    nr, inc = 0, []
    for rid, e in st["ft"].items():
        if e["final"] == "exclude":
            why = _final_field(e, "reason").casefold()
            if why == NOT_RETRIEVED:
                nr += 1
            else:
                reasons[why] = reasons.get(why, 0) + 1
        elif e["final"] == "include":
            inc.append(_final_field(e, "study") or rid)
    fixed = {"databases": sum(by_db.values()) - reg, "registers": reg,
             "duplicates": sum(1 for r in recs.values() if r.get("duplicate_of")),
             "screened": len(st["ta"]), "excluded": sum(1 for e in st["ta"].values() if e["final"] == "exclude"),
             "sought": len(st["ft"]), "not_retrieved": nr, "assessed": len(st["ft"]) - nr,
             "reports_excluded": sum(reasons.values()), "studies": len(set(inc)), "reports": len(inc)}
    return {"fixed": fixed, "sources": dict(sorted(by_db.items())), "reasons": dict(sorted(reasons.items()))}, unresolved


def labelled(data: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
    out = {label: data["fixed"][key] for key, label in FIXED}
    out.update({f"records identified: {db}": n for db, n in data["sources"].items()})
    out.update({f"reports excluded: {why}": n for why, n in data["reasons"].items()})
    return out


def write_counts(project: Path, data: dict) -> Path:
    if len(data["sources"]) > 29 or len(data["reasons"]) > 49:
        raise ValueError("more than 29 sources or 49 exclusion reasons do not fit the id layout (plan D10)")
    log = resultlog.ResultLog(project, SCREENING, LOG_REL, "screening.py counts")
    for i, (key, label) in enumerate(FIXED, start=1):
        log.add("A0", "observed", label, data["fixed"][key], "n", number=i)
    for i, (db, n) in enumerate(data["sources"].items(), start=21):
        log.add("A0", "observed", f"records identified: {db}", n, "n", number=i)
    for i, (why, n) in enumerate(data["reasons"].items(), start=51):
        log.add("A0", "observed", f"reports excluded: {why}", n, "n", number=i)
    return log.save(DOC_REL)


def check(project: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    if not (project / RECORDS).is_file():
        return [("block", f"{RECORDS}:1", "no screening log: import the search exports (screening.py import)")]
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    names = reviewers(project)
    for name in names:
        if AI_NAME.search(name):
            found.append(("block", "project.yaml:1", f"{name!r} in review.reviewers looks like an AI; AI never counts as a reviewer"))
    humans = human_reviewers(project)
    if len(humans) < 2:
        found.append(("block", "project.yaml:1", f"review.reviewers lists {len(humans)} human reviewer(s); two are needed"))
    st = resolve(project)
    for stage in STAGES:
        for rid, e in st[stage].items():
            line = max((r["_line"] for r in e["rows"].values()), default=1)
            if e["final"] is None:
                found.append(("block", f"{SCREENING}:{line}", f"{rid} ({stage}): {e['why']}"))
            elif stage == "ft" and e["final"] == "exclude":
                rows = [e["rows"][CONSENSUS]] if CONSENSUS in e["rows"] else [r for k, r in e["rows"].items() if k != CONSENSUS]
                if any(not (r.get("reason") or "").strip() for r in rows):
                    found.append(("block", f"{SCREENING}:{line}", f"{rid}: full-text exclusion without a reason"))
    found += hits_findings(project, list(records(project).values()))
    if not any(s == "block" for s, _, _ in found):
        data, _ = compute_counts(project)
        fresh = labelled(data)
        path = project / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json"
        led_recs = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")).get("results", []) if path.is_file() else []
        led = {r.get("label"): r.get("value") for r in led_recs if isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("source_file") == LOG_REL}
        if not led:
            found.append(("block", "ledgers/results-ledger.json:1", "PRISMA counts are not in the results ledger: run screening.py counts"))
        elif led != fresh:
            diff = [f"{k}: ledger {led.get(k)}, now {fresh.get(k)}" for k in sorted(set(led) | set(fresh)) if led.get(k) != fresh.get(k)]
            found.append(("block", "ledgers/results-ledger.json:1",
                          "ledger counts differ from a fresh counts run (" + "; ".join(diff[:5]) + "); run screening.py counts"))
    return found


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- CLI

def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    s = sub.add_parser("import")
    s.add_argument("file")
    s.add_argument("--database", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("dedupe")
    s = sub.add_parser("decide")
    s.add_argument("--stage", choices=STAGES, required=True)
    s.add_argument("--reviewer", required=True)
    s.add_argument("--rec")
    s.add_argument("--decision", choices=DECISIONS)
    s.add_argument("--reason", default="")
    s.add_argument("--study", default="")
    s.add_argument("--file")
    s = sub.add_parser("sheet")
    s.add_argument("--stage", choices=STAGES, required=True)
    s.add_argument("--reviewer", required=True)
    s = sub.add_parser("suggest")
    s.add_argument("--stage", choices=STAGES, required=True)
    s.add_argument("--from", dest="src", required=True)
    for name in ("conflicts", "kappa"):
        sub.add_parser(name).add_argument("--stage", choices=STAGES, required=True)
    sub.add_parser("counts")
    sub.add_parser("check")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "project.yaml").is_file():
        print(f"screening: {a.project} is not a project folder (no project.yaml)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "import":
            n, found = import_file(project, Path(a.file), a.database)
            for sev, loc, msg in found:
                print(f"E-SCR {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
            print(f"screening: {n} records imported from {Path(a.file).name} ({a.database})")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "dedupe":
            n, d = dedupe(project)
            print(f"screening: {n} records, {d} marked as duplicates (both rows kept)")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "decide":
            if a.file:
                rows = [r for r in read_csv(Path(a.file)) if (r.get("decision") or "").strip()]
            elif a.rec and a.decision:
                rows = [{"rec_id": a.rec, "decision": a.decision, "reason": a.reason, "study": a.study}]
            else:
                print("screening: decide needs --rec and --decision, or --file", file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
            n = decide(project, a.stage, a.reviewer, rows)
            print(f"screening: {n} decision(s) recorded for {a.reviewer} ({a.stage})")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "sheet":
            path = sheet(project, a.stage, a.reviewer)
            print(f"screening: wrote {path.relative_to(project).as_posix()} (fill 'decision'; the AI columns are "
                  "suggestions, never decisions)")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "suggest":
            n = suggest(project, a.stage, Path(a.src))
            print(f"screening: {n} AI suggestions for {a.stage} written to {SUGGESTIONS} (suggestions, not decisions)")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "conflicts":
            lines = [f"{rid}: {e['why']}" for rid, e in resolve(project)[a.stage].items() if e["final"] is None]
            for ln in lines:
                print(ln)
            print(f"screening: {len(lines)} unresolved at {a.stage}")
            return 1 if lines else 0
        if a.cmd == "kappa":
            k, n, po = kappa(project, a.stage)
            if not n:
                print(f"kappa {a.stage}: no record decided by both reviewers")
            elif k is None:
                print(f"kappa {a.stage}: undefined (all decisions in one category; n = {n})")
            else:
                print(f"kappa {a.stage}: {k:.2f} (n = {n}; agreement {po * 100:.1f}%)")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "counts":
            data, unresolved = compute_counts(project)
            if unresolved:
                for ln in unresolved:
                    print(ln)
                print("screening: counts not written; resolve every record first (conflicts --stage …)")
                return 1
            n, bad = resultlog.import_merged(project, write_counts(project, data))
            if bad:
                for x in bad:
                    print(x)
                return 1
            for label, v in labelled(data).items():
                print(f"{label} = {v}")
            print(f"screening: {n} PRISMA counts in the results ledger (A0, observed)")
            return 0
        found = check(project)
        for sev, loc, msg in found:
            print(f"E-SCR {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
        blocks = sum(1 for f in found if f[0] == "block")
        print(f"screening: {blocks} block, {len(found) - blocks} warn")
        return 1 if blocks else 0
    except Refused as e:
        print(f"screening: refused: {e}")
        return 1
    except results_ledger.LockedError as e:
        print(f"screening: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except results_ledger.RelabelError as e:
        print(f"{e}\nscreening: labels changed; ledger not written", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, csv.Error) as e:
        print(f"screening: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Note `test_bulk_decide_is_all_or_nothing`: an unknown decision value is a usage error (`ValueError`, exit 2) raised before anything is written; an unknown record is a refusal (exit 1); in both cases `screening.csv` stays empty because rows are appended only after every row passed.

### Task 4: `extract_check.py` — double extraction, consensus, risk of bias, charting (spec 6.3)

**Files:**
- Create: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/extract_check.py`, `tests/unit/test_extract_check.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `screening.human_reviewers(project)`, `screening.AI_NAME`, `screening.read_csv`, `screening.slug` (Task 3); `crolib.projectcfg.read_project_yaml`; `p5_helpers.review_project`.
- Produces CLI `extract_check.py <project>`; importable `extract_check.check(project) -> list[tuple[level, loc, msg]]` (E-SCR, used by `audit.py` in Task 9), `extract_check.KEY = ("study_id", "outcome", "timepoint", "group")`, `extract_check.TOOLS` (RoB 2 and ROBINS-I domains and levels), constants `EXTRACTION = "04_data/sr/extraction.csv"`, `ROB = "04_data/sr/rob.csv"`, `CHARTING = "04_data/sr/charting.csv"`.
- Rules (D11): extractor files `04_data/sr/extraction-<slug>.csv` for exactly the two human reviewers; a differing cell is `warn`; differences without `extraction.csv` → `block`; a consensus cell matching neither extractor in a row without `consensus_note` → `block`; numbers compare numerically (`1.50` = `1.5`, `1,5` = `1.5`). `rob.csv` per (study, outcome): one tool from `TOOLS`, every domain plus `overall`, judgements from the tool's levels, overall never better than the worst domain (RoB 2: `low` only when every domain is low), `reviewer` names both human reviewers (`;`-separated) — an AI or single name is `block`; empty `support` is `warn`; a numeric judgement is `block` ("never a summed score"). Scoping (`design: scoping-review`): `charting.csv` rows need `extracted_by` and `verified_by`, two different human reviewers; a `rob.csv` is `warn`.

```python
import contextlib
import io
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import extract_check
from p5_helpers import review_project

HEAD = "study_id,outcome,timepoint,group,n,events,mean,sd,es,se\n"
A = HEAD + "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,5,,,,\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9,,,,\nS02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,\n"
B = HEAD + "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,6,,,,\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9.0,,,,\nS02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,\n"
ROB_HEAD = "study_id,outcome,tool,domain,judgement,support,reviewer\n"


def rob_rows(overall="some concerns", reviewer="Reviewer A; Reviewer B", d3="some concerns"):
    doms = {"D1": "low", "D2": "low", "D3": d3, "D4": "low", "D5": "low", "overall": overall}
    return ROB_HEAD + "".join(f'S01,mortality,RoB 2,{d},{j},"quote p. 4",{reviewer}\n' for d, j in doms.items())


def cli(p):
    out = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
        code = extract_check.main([str(p)])
    return code, out.getvalue()


class TestExtractCheck(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = review_project(self.t)
        self.d = self.p / "04_data" / "sr"

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def w(self, name, text):
        (self.d / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")

    def msgs(self, level=None):
        return [m for s, _, m in extract_check.check(self.p) if level in (None, s)]

    def test_differences_listed_and_consensus_needed(self):
        self.w("extraction-reviewer-a.csv", A)
        self.w("extraction-reviewer-b.csv", B)
        warns = self.msgs("warn")
        self.assertTrue(any("S01 / mortality / 30 days / drug: events Reviewer A=5, Reviewer B=6" in m for m in warns), warns)
        self.assertFalse(any("placebo" in m for m in warns))          # 9 and 9.0 are the same number
        self.assertTrue(any("no consensus file" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p)[0], 1)

    def test_consensus_rule(self):
        self.w("extraction-reviewer-a.csv", A)
        self.w("extraction-reviewer-b.csv", B)
        cons = HEAD.rstrip("\n") + ",consensus_note\n"
        self.w("extraction.csv", cons + "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,6,,,,,\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9,,,,,\n"
                                         "S02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,,\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.msgs("block"), [])
        self.w("extraction.csv", cons + "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,7,,,,,\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9,,,,,\n"
                                         "S02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,,\n")
        self.assertTrue(any("events = 7 matches neither extractor" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("extraction.csv", cons + "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,7,,,,,re-read table 2 with both reviewers\n"
                                         "S01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9,,,,,\nS02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,,\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.msgs("block"), [])
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p)[0], 0)

    def test_extractor_files_must_be_the_two_reviewers(self):
        self.w("extraction-reviewer-a.csv", A)
        self.assertTrue(any("two extraction files" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("extraction-claude.csv", B)
        self.assertTrue(any("extraction-claude.csv" in m and "not a reviewer" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))

    def test_rob_rules(self):
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows())
        self.assertEqual(self.msgs("block"), [])
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows(overall="low"))
        self.assertTrue(any("overall 'low' is better than the worst domain 'some concerns'" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows(reviewer="AI draft"))
        self.assertTrue(any("both reviewers" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows(d3="3"))
        self.assertTrue(any("never a summed score" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows().replace("S01,mortality,RoB 2,D5,low", "S01,mortality,RoB 2,D9,low"))
        blocks = self.msgs("block")
        self.assertTrue(any("D5 missing" in m for m in blocks), blocks)
        self.assertTrue(any("D9" in m for m in blocks), blocks)
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows().replace('"quote p. 4"', '""'))
        self.assertTrue(any("no supporting quote" in m for m in self.msgs("warn")))

    def test_robins_i_overall_is_worst_domain(self):
        rows = ROB_HEAD + "".join(f'S02,mortality,ROBINS-I,D{i},{j},"q",Reviewer A; Reviewer B\n'
                                  for i, j in enumerate(["low", "serious", "low", "moderate", "no information", "low", "low"], 1))
        self.w("rob.csv", rows + 'S02,mortality,ROBINS-I,overall,moderate,"q",Reviewer A; Reviewer B\n')
        self.assertTrue(any("better than the worst domain 'serious'" in m for m in self.msgs("block")))
        self.w("rob.csv", rows + 'S02,mortality,ROBINS-I,overall,serious,"q",Reviewer A; Reviewer B\n')
        self.assertEqual(self.msgs("block"), [])

    def test_scoping_charting(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t / "p")
        self.p = review_project(self.t, design="scoping-review")
        self.d = self.p / "04_data" / "sr"
        self.w("charting.csv", "source_id,concept,extracted_by,verified_by\nS01,x,Reviewer A,Reviewer B\nS02,y,Reviewer A,\n"
                               "S03,z,Reviewer A,Reviewer A\n")
        blocks = self.msgs("block")
        self.assertTrue(any("line 3" in m and "verified_by" in m for m in blocks), blocks)
        self.assertTrue(any("line 4" in m and "the same person" in m for m in blocks), blocks)
        self.w("rob.csv", rob_rows())
        self.assertTrue(any("scoping reviews do not assess risk of bias" in m for m in self.msgs("warn")))

    def test_nothing_to_check_and_usage(self):
        self.assertEqual(extract_check.check(self.p), [])
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.t / "none")[0], 2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `extract_check.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Double extraction, risk of bias and charting checks for systematic and scoping reviews (E-SCR).

  python extract_check.py <project>

Extraction: 04_data/sr/extraction-<reviewer>.csv, one file per human reviewer in project.yaml review.reviewers
(<reviewer> = lower-case name, non-alphanumerics → "-"), rows keyed by study_id,outcome,timepoint,group. Every
differing cell is listed (warn); the consensus 04_data/sr/extraction.csv must exist when they differ, and each of
its cells must match one extractor unless the row has a consensus_note (block). Risk of bias:
04_data/sr/rob.csv (study_id,outcome,tool,domain,judgement,support,reviewer); tools RoB 2 (D1-D5) and ROBINS-I
(D1-D7), each with an "overall" row that is never better than the worst domain (the tool's algorithm, never a
summed score); the reviewer column names both human reviewers. Scoping reviews: 04_data/sr/charting.csv needs
extracted_by and verified_by, two different human reviewers; no risk of bias.
Exit codes: 0 = no block (warnings allowed); 1 = block findings; 2 = usage or I/O error.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import projectcfg  # noqa: E402
import screening  # noqa: E402

SRD = "04_data/sr"
EXTRACTION = f"{SRD}/extraction.csv"
ROB = f"{SRD}/rob.csv"
CHARTING = f"{SRD}/charting.csv"
KEY = ("study_id", "outcome", "timepoint", "group")
SKIP = set(KEY) | {"consensus_note", "notes"}
TOOLS = {
    "RoB 2": {"domains": [f"D{i}" for i in range(1, 6)], "levels": ["low", "some concerns", "high"]},
    "ROBINS-I": {"domains": [f"D{i}" for i in range(1, 8)],
                 "levels": ["low", "moderate", "serious", "critical", "no information"]},
}
NUM = re.compile(r"-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?")


def _same(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    a, b = (a or "").strip(), (b or "").strip()
    if NUM.fullmatch(a) and NUM.fullmatch(b):
        return float(a.replace(",", ".")) == float(b.replace(",", "."))
    return a.casefold() == b.casefold()


def _keyed(path: Path, rel: str, found: list) -> dict[tuple, tuple[int, dict]]:
    out: dict[tuple, tuple[int, dict]] = {}
    for i, r in enumerate(screening.read_csv(path), start=2):
        k = tuple((r.get(c) or "").strip() for c in KEY)
        if k in out:
            found.append(("block", f"{rel}:{i}", f"duplicate row for {' / '.join(k)}"))
        out[k] = (i, r)
    return out


def check_extraction(project: Path, humans: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    files = sorted((project / SRD).glob("extraction-*.csv")) if (project / SRD).is_dir() else []
    if not files:
        return found
    by_slug = {screening.slug(h): h for h in humans}
    named = []
    for f in files:
        s = f.stem[len("extraction-"):]
        if s not in by_slug or screening.AI_NAME.search(s):
            found.append(("block", f"{SRD}/{f.name}:1", f"{f.name}: '{s}' is not a reviewer in project.yaml review.reviewers"))
        else:
            named.append((by_slug[s], f))
    if len(named) != 2:
        found.append(("block", f"{SRD}:1", f"two extraction files are needed, one per human reviewer (found {len(named)})"))
        return found
    (na, fa), (nb, fb) = named
    ra, rb = (_keyed(f, f"{SRD}/{f.name}", found) for f in (fa, fb))
    diffs = 0
    for k in sorted(set(ra) | set(rb)):
        label = " / ".join(k)
        if k not in ra or k not in rb:
            who, f, (line, _) = (na, fb, rb[k]) if k not in ra else (nb, fa, ra[k])
            found.append(("warn", f"{SRD}/{f.name}:{line}", f"{label}: not extracted by {who}"))
            diffs += 1
            continue
        (la, xa), (_, xb) = ra[k], rb[k]
        for col in sorted((set(xa) | set(xb)) - SKIP):
            if not _same(xa.get(col, ""), xb.get(col, "")):
                found.append(("warn", f"{SRD}/{fa.name}:{la}",
                              f"{label}: {col} {na}={xa.get(col, '')}, {nb}={xb.get(col, '')}"))
                diffs += 1
    cons_path = project / EXTRACTION
    if not cons_path.is_file():
        if diffs:
            found.append(("block", f"{EXTRACTION}:1", f"no consensus file: {diffs} difference(s) between the extractors"))
        return found
    for k, (line, row) in _keyed(cons_path, EXTRACTION, found).items():
        if (row.get("consensus_note") or "").strip():
            continue
        label = " / ".join(k)
        a, b = ra.get(k, (0, {}))[1], rb.get(k, (0, {}))[1]
        if not a and not b:
            found.append(("block", f"{EXTRACTION}:{line}", f"{label}: not in either extraction and no consensus_note"))
            continue
        for col in sorted(set(row) - SKIP):
            v = row.get(col, "")
            if not (_same(v, a.get(col, "")) or _same(v, b.get(col, ""))):
                found.append(("block", f"{EXTRACTION}:{line}",
                              f"{label}: {col} = {v} matches neither extractor and the row has no consensus_note"))
    return found


def _rank(tool: str, level: str) -> int:
    order = [x for x in TOOLS[tool]["levels"] if x != "no information"]
    return order.index(level) if level in order else -1


def check_rob(project: Path, humans: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    groups: dict[tuple, list[tuple[int, dict]]] = {}
    for i, r in enumerate(screening.read_csv(project / ROB), start=2):
        groups.setdefault(((r.get("study_id") or "").strip(), (r.get("outcome") or "").strip()), []).append((i, r))
    want = {h.casefold() for h in humans}
    for (study, outcome), rows in groups.items():
        where = f"{ROB}:{rows[0][0]}"
        label = f"{study} / {outcome}"
        tools = {(r.get("tool") or "").strip() for _, r in rows}
        if len(tools) != 1:
            found.append(("block", where, f"{label}: one tool per study and outcome (found {', '.join(sorted(tools))})"))
            continue
        tool = tools.pop()
        if tool not in TOOLS:
            found.append(("warn", where, f"{label}: tool {tool!r} is not checked by this script; check it by hand"))
            continue
        spec, doms = TOOLS[tool], {}
        for line, r in rows:
            dom, j = (r.get("domain") or "").strip(), (r.get("judgement") or "").strip().casefold()
            loc = f"{ROB}:{line}"
            if NUM.fullmatch(j):
                found.append(("block", loc, f"{label} {dom}: numeric judgement {j}; judgements follow the tool's algorithm, never a summed score"))
                continue
            if dom not in spec["domains"] + ["overall"]:
                found.append(("block", loc, f"{label}: domain {dom!r} is not a {tool} domain"))
                continue
            if j not in spec["levels"]:
                found.append(("block", loc, f"{label} {dom}: judgement {j!r} not in {spec['levels']}"))
                continue
            doms[dom] = j
            names = {x.strip().casefold() for x in (r.get("reviewer") or "").split(";") if x.strip()}
            if len(want) < 2 or names != want:
                found.append(("block", loc, f"{label} {dom}: confirmed by both reviewers ({'; '.join(humans)}) is required; "
                                            f"found {r.get('reviewer')!r} (an AI draft is never final)"))
            if not (r.get("support") or "").strip():
                found.append(("warn", loc, f"{label} {dom}: no supporting quote"))
        for dom in spec["domains"] + ["overall"]:
            if dom not in doms:
                found.append(("block", where, f"{label}: {dom} missing"))
        if "overall" in doms:
            worst = max((doms[d] for d in spec["domains"] if d in doms), key=lambda x: _rank(tool, x), default=None)
            if worst and _rank(tool, doms["overall"]) < _rank(tool, worst):
                found.append(("block", where, f"{label}: overall {doms['overall']!r} is better than the worst domain {worst!r}"))
    return found


def check_charting(project: Path, humans: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    ok = {h.casefold() for h in humans}
    for i, r in enumerate(screening.read_csv(project / CHARTING), start=2):
        e, v = (r.get("extracted_by") or "").strip(), (r.get("verified_by") or "").strip()
        loc = f"{CHARTING}:{i}"
        if not e or not v:
            found.append(("block", loc, f"line {i}: extracted_by and verified_by are both required"))
        elif e.casefold() == v.casefold():
            found.append(("block", loc, f"line {i}: extracted_by and verified_by are the same person"))
        elif e.casefold() not in ok or v.casefold() not in ok:
            found.append(("block", loc, f"line {i}: {e!r} or {v!r} is not a human reviewer in review.reviewers"))
    return found


def check(project: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    humans = screening.human_reviewers(project)
    scoping = projectcfg.read_project_yaml(project).get("design") == "scoping-review"
    found = check_extraction(project, humans)
    if scoping:
        found += check_charting(project, humans)
        if (project / ROB).is_file():
            found.append(("warn", f"{ROB}:1", "scoping reviews do not assess risk of bias (PRISMA-ScR); rob.csv is not used"))
    elif (project / ROB).is_file():
        found += check_rob(project, humans)
    return found


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "project.yaml").is_file():
        print(f"extract_check: {a.project} is not a project folder (no project.yaml)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        found = check(project)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f"extract_check: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    for sev, loc, msg in found:
        print(f"E-SCR {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
    blocks = sum(1 for f in found if f[0] == "block")
    print(f"extract_check: {blocks} block, {len(found) - blocks} warn")
    return 1 if blocks else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `analysis_plan.register_path`, `analysis_plan.parse_register`, `analysis_plan.declared` (Task 1 / P2); `crolib.resultlog` (Task 1); `crolib.projectcfg.get` (Task 3); `crolib.derived.ledger_by_id`; `screening.read_csv` (Task 3); `p5_helpers.review_project`.
- Produces CLI `meta.py <project> {run,forest-spec,r-script,r-status,r-check}`; files `05_analysis/output/meta-log.txt` (P2 log format), `05_analysis/output/meta-results.json` (results document, merged into the ledger), `05_analysis/meta/python-results.json` (model-scale values per key for r-check), `05_analysis/meta/meta-input.csv`, `05_analysis/meta/meta-plan.csv`, `05_analysis/meta/metafor.R`, `05_analysis/meta/metafor-results.json` (written by R), figure entries `F-A<n>` in `07_present/figures/figures.json`.
- Produces importable: `meta.study_effect(measure: str, s: dict) -> tuple[float, float] | None` (None = double-zero study); `meta.pool(y: list[float], v: list[float], model: str, hksj: bool = False) -> dict` and `meta.pool_mh(studies: list[dict], measure: str) -> dict`, both with keys `k, estimate, se, ci, p, tau2, i2, q, q_df, q_p, pi` (model scale; `pi` None unless random effects with k ≥ 3); `meta.tau2_dl(y, v)`, `meta.tau2_reml(y, v)`; `meta.egger(y, v) -> dict` (`intercept, se, t, p`); `meta.subgroup_test(fits: list[dict]) -> dict` (`q, df, p`); `meta.t_ppf(q, df)`, `meta.t_p2(t, df)`, `meta.chi2_sf(x, df)`, `meta.betai(a, b, x)`; `meta.rscript_candidates(project, env=None, system=None, which=shutil.which) -> list[Path]`, `meta.find_rscript(project, env=None, system=None, which=shutil.which, is_file=None) -> Path | None`, `meta.r_versions(rscript) -> tuple[str, str | None]`, `meta.install_hint(system: str) -> str`; constants `MEASURES`, `MODELS`, `KIND`, `RATIO`.
- Result ids and estimator definitions: plan D13, D14. Register columns: D12. R discovery: D15.

**Testing rule (spec 6.4, D16).** No expected number in these tests is typed from memory. Three kinds of checks only: (1) hand-computable or closed-form values recomputed inside the test from the test's own inputs (DerSimonian–Laird τ² recomputed independently; Cauchy and t(2) quantiles in closed form; χ²(2) tail = e^(−x/2)); (2) internal consistency and invariances (the REML fixed point satisfies the REML score equation; the HKSJ interval is at least as wide as the Wald interval when the HKSJ scale factor is ≥ 1; MH and IV agree on identical studies; swapping arms negates the effect; order does not matter); (3) `metafor-goldens.json`, produced by metafor from datasets that ship with metafor plus one constructed set, with the inputs stored next to the outputs (relative tolerance 1e-4).

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import math
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import NormalDist
from unittest import mock

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import gate
import meta
from p5_helpers import review_project

GOLD = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "meta" / "metafor-goldens.json"
Y3, V3 = [0.1, 0.5, 0.9], [0.04, 0.09, 0.01]
YH, VH = [-0.5, 0.2, 0.9, 0.1, 1.2], [0.02, 0.03, 0.025, 0.04, 0.05]
REGISTER = ("# Review protocol (fictional)\n\n## Full-text exclusion reasons\n- wrong population\n\n"
            "## Analysis register\n\n| ID | Type | Outcome | Timepoint | Measure | Model | HKSJ | Experimental | Comparator | Factor |\n"
            "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| A0 | counts | PRISMA flow | | | | | | | |\n"
            "| A1 | pool | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n"
            "| A2 | subgroup | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | design |\n"
            "| A3 | leave-one-out | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n"
            "| A4 | pool | pain | 6 weeks | MD | RE-DL | yes | drug | placebo | |\n")
EXTRACTION = ("study_id,outcome,timepoint,group,n,events,mean,sd,es,se,design\n"
              "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,50,5,,,,,rct\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,50,9,,,,,rct\n"
              "S02,mortality,30 days,drug,40,4,,,,,rct\nS02,mortality,30 days,placebo,41,8,,,,,rct\n"
              "S03,mortality,30 days,drug,60,10,,,,,nrsi\nS03,mortality,30 days,placebo,58,12,,,,,nrsi\n"
              "S01,pain,6 weeks,drug,50,,3.1,1.2,,,rct\nS01,pain,6 weeks,placebo,50,,3.9,1.4,,,rct\n")


def close(a, b, rel=1e-4):
    if a is None or b is None:
        return a is None and b is None
    return abs(a - b) <= rel * max(abs(a), abs(b)) or abs(a - b) <= 1e-8


def cli(*argv):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        code = meta.main([str(a) for a in argv])
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


class TestDistributions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_closed_forms(self):
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.t_ppf(0.975, 1), math.tan(math.pi * 0.475), places=6)      # Cauchy
        for q in (0.9, 0.975, 0.995):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.t_ppf(q, 2), (2 * q - 1) / math.sqrt(2 * q * (1 - q)), places=6)
        for x in (0.5, 3.0, 10.0):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.chi2_sf(x, 2), math.exp(-x / 2), places=10)
        for df in (3, 7, 30):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.t_p2(meta.t_ppf(0.975, df), df), 0.05, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.t_ppf(0.975, 100000), NormalDist().inv_cdf(0.975), places=3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.betai(2.5, 4.0, 0.3) + meta.betai(4.0, 2.5, 0.7), 1.0, places=12)


class TestEstimators(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_dl_tau2_recomputed_independently(self):
        w = [1 / v for v in V3]
        mu = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, Y3)) / sum(w)
        q = sum(wi * (yi - mu) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(w, Y3))
        c = sum(w) - sum(x * x for x in w) / sum(w)
        expected = max(0.0, (q - 2) / c)
        f = meta.pool(Y3, V3, "RE-DL")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["tau2"], expected, places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["q"], q, places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["i2"], max(0.0, (q - 2) / q) * 100, places=9)   # DL: I² = (Q - df)/Q
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.tau2_dl(Y3, V3), expected, places=12)

    def test_reml_fixed_point_solves_the_score_equation(self):
        f = meta.pool(YH, VH, "RE-REML")
        t = f["tau2"]
        self.assertGreater(t, 0)
        w = [1 / (v + t) for v in VH]
        mu = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, YH)) / sum(w)
        score = sum(wi * wi * (yi - mu) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(w, YH)) - sum(w) + sum(x * x for x in w) / sum(w)
        self.assertLess(abs(score), 1e-8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["estimate"], mu, places=10)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["se"], math.sqrt(1 / sum(w)), places=10)

    def test_hksj_at_least_as_wide_as_wald_when_scale_factor_ge_1(self):
        wald, hk = meta.pool(YH, VH, "RE-REML"), meta.pool(YH, VH, "RE-REML", hksj=True)
        t = wald["tau2"]
        w = [1 / (v + t) for v in VH]
        qh = sum(wi * (yi - wald["estimate"]) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(w, YH)) / (len(YH) - 1)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(qh, 1.0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(hk["ci"][1] - hk["ci"][0], wald["ci"][1] - wald["ci"][0])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(hk["estimate"], wald["estimate"], places=12)

    def test_identical_studies_mh_iv_agree_and_no_heterogeneity(self):
        s = {"a": 10, "n1": 50, "c": 20, "n2": 50}
        studies = [dict(s, study=f"S{i}") for i in range(3)]
        y, v = meta.study_effect("OR", s)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(y, math.log(10 * 30 / (40 * 20)), places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v, 1 / 10 + 1 / 40 + 1 / 20 + 1 / 30, places=12)
        iv = meta.pool([y] * 3, [v] * 3, "CE-IV")
        mh = meta.pool_mh(studies, "OR")
        reml = meta.pool([y] * 3, [v] * 3, "RE-REML")
        for f in (iv, mh, reml):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(f["estimate"], y, places=10)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(f["i2"], 0.0, places=10)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(f["q"], 0.0, places=10)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(reml["tau2"], 0.0, places=10)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.pool_mh(studies, "RR")["estimate"], math.log((10 / 50) / (20 / 50)), places=10)

    def test_invariances(self):
        s2 = {"a": 7, "n1": 40, "c": 12, "n2": 44}
        swapped = {"a": 12, "n1": 44, "c": 7, "n2": 40}
        for m in ("OR", "RR", "RD"):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.study_effect(m, s2)[0], -meta.study_effect(m, swapped)[0], places=12)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.study_effect(m, s2)[1], meta.study_effect(m, swapped)[1], places=12)
        c = {"m1": 3.1, "sd1": 1.2, "n1": 50, "m2": 3.9, "sd2": 1.4, "n2": 52}
        cs = {"m1": 3.9, "sd1": 1.4, "n1": 52, "m2": 3.1, "sd2": 1.2, "n2": 50}
        for m in ("MD", "SMD"):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.study_effect(m, c)[0], -meta.study_effect(m, cs)[0], places=12)
        a = meta.pool(YH, VH, "RE-REML")
        b = meta.pool(YH[::-1], VH[::-1], "RE-REML")
        for k in ("estimate", "tau2", "i2", "q"):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(a[k], b[k], places=9)

    def test_zero_cells_and_double_zero(self):
        self.assertIsNone(meta.study_effect("OR", {"a": 0, "n1": 20, "c": 0, "n2": 20}))
        self.assertIsNone(meta.study_effect("RR", {"a": 20, "n1": 20, "c": 15, "n2": 15}))
        y, v = meta.study_effect("OR", {"a": 0, "n1": 20, "c": 3, "n2": 20})
        a, b, c, d = 0.5, 20.5, 3.5, 17.5
        self.assertAlmostEqual(y, math.log(a * d / (b * c)), places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v, 1 / a + 1 / b + 1 / c + 1 / d, places=12)

    def test_smd_exact_correction(self):
        c = {"m1": 3.1, "sd1": 1.2, "n1": 11, "m2": 3.9, "sd2": 1.4, "n2": 11}
        m = 20
        sp = math.sqrt((10 * 1.2 ** 2 + 10 * 1.4 ** 2) / m)
        g, v = meta.study_effect("SMD", c)
        j = g / ((3.1 - 3.9) / sp)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(j, 1 - 3 / (4 * m - 1), places=3)          # the usual approximation
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v, 1 / 11 + 1 / 11 + g * g / (2 * 22), places=12)

    def test_prediction_interval_rules(self):
        self.assertIsNone(meta.pool(Y3[:2], V3[:2], "RE-REML")["pi"])
        self.assertIsNone(meta.pool(Y3, V3, "CE-IV")["pi"])
        f = meta.pool(YH, VH, "RE-REML")
        h = meta.t_ppf(0.975, 3) * math.sqrt(f["tau2"] + f["se"] ** 2)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(f["pi"][0], f["estimate"] - h, places=12)
        self.assertLess(f["pi"][0], f["ci"][0])
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            meta.pool([0.1], [0.04], "RE-REML")

    def test_egger_equals_classic_regression_of_y_over_se_on_precision(self):
        y = [0.10, 0.35, -0.20, 0.55, 0.05, 0.40, 0.25, -0.05, 0.60, 0.15]
        v = [0.010, 0.040, 0.020, 0.090, 0.015, 0.060, 0.030, 0.012, 0.080, 0.025]
        z = [yi / math.sqrt(vi) for yi, vi in zip(y, v)]
        x = [1 / math.sqrt(vi) for vi in v]
        mx, mz = sum(x) / len(x), sum(z) / len(z)
        slope = sum((a - mx) * (b - mz) for a, b in zip(x, z)) / sum((a - mx) ** 2 for a in x)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(meta.egger(y, v)["intercept"], mz - slope * mx, places=10)

    def test_subgroup_test_matches_definition(self):
        fits = [{"estimate": 0.2, "se": 0.1}, {"estimate": 0.6, "se": 0.2}]
        w = [100.0, 25.0]
        mu = (100 * 0.2 + 25 * 0.6) / 125
        qb = 100 * (0.2 - mu) ** 2 + 25 * (0.6 - mu) ** 2
        t = meta.subgroup_test(fits)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(t["q"], qb, places=12)
        self.assertEqual(t["df"], 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(t["p"], meta.chi2_sf(qb, 1), places=12)
        self.assertTrue(all(isinstance(x, float) for x in w))


@unittest.skipUnless(GOLD.is_file(), "metafor goldens not generated yet (Task 5 step 4)")
class TestMetaforGoldens(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        data = json.loads(GOLD.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        cls.engine, cls.cases = data["engine"], data["cases"]

    def fit(self, c, studies=None, model=None):
        studies = c["studies"] if studies is None else studies
        model = model or c["model"]
        if model == "CE-MH":
            return meta.pool_mh(studies, c["measure"])
        eff = [e for e in (meta.study_effect(c["measure"], s) for s in studies) if e is not None]
        return meta.pool([e[0] for e in eff], [e[1] for e in eff], model, c["hksj"])

    def same(self, got, want, keys=("estimate", "se", "p", "tau2", "i2", "q", "q_p")):
        for k in keys:
            if k in want:
                self.assertTrue(close(got[k], want[k]), f"{k}: python {got[k]!r}, metafor {want[k]!r}")
        for k in ("ci", "pi"):
            if k in want:
                if want[k] is None:
                    self.assertIsNone(got[k], k)
                else:
                    for a, b in zip(got[k], want[k]):
                        self.assertTrue(close(a, b), f"{k}: python {got[k]!r}, metafor {want[k]!r}")

    def test_engine_recorded_without_paths(self):
        self.assertIn("metafor", self.engine)
        self.assertNotRegex(self.engine, r"[\\/]")

    def test_pooled_cases(self):
        n = 0
        for name, c in self.cases.items():
            if any(k in c for k in ("egger", "levels", "loo")):
                continue
            with self.subTest(case=name):
                self.same(self.fit(c), c)
                n += 1
        self.assertGreaterEqual(n, 9)

    def test_egger(self):
        c = self.cases["bcg_rr_egger"]
        eff = [meta.study_effect("RR", s) for s in c["studies"]]
        got = meta.egger([e[0] for e in eff], [e[1] for e in eff])
        for k in ("intercept", "se", "t", "p"):
            self.assertTrue(close(got[k], c["egger"][k]), k)

    def test_subgroups(self):
        c = self.cases["bcg_rr_subgroup_alloc"]
        fits = []
        for lev, want in c["levels"].items():
            sub = [s for s in c["studies"] if c["factor"][s["study"]] == lev]
            got = self.fit(c, sub)
            with self.subTest(level=lev):
                self.same(got, want)
            fits.append(got)
        t = meta.subgroup_test(fits)
        self.assertTrue(close(t["q"], c["qb"]))
        self.assertEqual(t["df"], c["qb_df"])
        self.assertTrue(close(t["p"], c["qb_p"]))

    def test_leave_one_out(self):
        c = self.cases["bcg_rr_loo"]
        for row in c["loo"]:
            rest = [s for s in c["studies"] if s["study"] != row["omit"]]
            got = self.fit(c, rest)
            with self.subTest(omit=row["omit"]):
                self.same(got, {"estimate": row["estimate"], "ci": row["ci"]}, keys=("estimate",))


class TestMetaCli(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = review_project(self.t)
        (self.p / "03_protocol" / "sr-protocol.md").write_text(REGISTER, encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr" / "extraction.csv").write_text(EXTRACTION, encoding="utf-8")

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def ledger(self):
        recs = json.loads((self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]
        return {r["id"]: r for r in recs}

    def test_run_writes_ledger_records_and_files(self):
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        led = self.ledger()
        for rid in ("R-A1-001", "R-A1-002", "R-A1-003", "R-A1-004", "R-A1-005", "R-A1-011", "R-A1-012", "R-A1-013",
                    "R-A2-001", "R-A2-021", "R-A3-001", "R-A3-002", "R-A3-003"):
            self.assertIn(rid, led)
        self.assertNotIn("R-A1-006", led)                        # Egger only when k >= 10
        self.assertNotIn("R-A2-011", led)                        # level "nrsi" has one study: not pooled
        self.assertNotIn("R-A4-001", led)                        # one study: narrative line, no pooled id
        self.assertIn("R-A4-011", led)
        self.assertIn("A4 pain: 1 study; not pooled", (self.p / meta.LOG_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        studies = [{"a": 5, "n1": 50, "c": 9, "n2": 50}, {"a": 4, "n1": 40, "c": 8, "n2": 41},
                   {"a": 10, "n1": 60, "c": 12, "n2": 58}]
        eff = [meta.study_effect("RR", s) for s in studies]
        f = meta.pool([e[0] for e in eff], [e[1] for e in eff], "RE-REML")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-001"]["value"], math.exp(f["estimate"]), places=9)
        self.assertEqual((led["R-A1-001"]["kind"], led["R-A1-001"]["status"], led["R-A1-001"]["count"]), ("rr", "prespecified", 3))
        self.assertEqual(led["R-A1-002"]["kind"], "tau2")
        self.assertEqual(led["R-A1-003"]["kind"], "percent")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-005"]["ci"][0], math.exp(f["pi"][0]), places=9)
        self.assertEqual(led["R-A1-011"]["label"], "S01 RR")
        self.assertEqual(led["R-A3-001"]["label"], "pooled RR without S01")
        py = json.loads((self.p / meta.PY_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertIn("A1", py)
        self.assertIn("A2|rct", py)
        self.assertIn("A3|-S01", py)
        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", (self.p / meta.LOG_REL).read_bytes())

    def test_deviation_status_and_g3_lock(self):
        (self.p / "decisions.md").write_text("# Decisions\n\n- DEVIATION A1: model changed to REML before data, 2026-11-20\n",
                                             encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "run")[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.ledger()["R-A1-001"]["status"], "deviation")
        gate.open_gate(self.p, "G3", by="A", files=["ledgers/results-ledger.json"])
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr" / "extraction.csv").write_text(EXTRACTION.replace("drug,50,5", "drug,50,6"), encoding="utf-8")
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("G3 is locked", err)

    def test_scoping_review_is_refused(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t / "p")
        self.p = review_project(self.t, design="scoping-review")
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("do not pool", out)

    def test_forest_spec_and_r_script(self):
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "run")[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "forest-spec")[0], 0)
        spec = json.loads((self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / "figures.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        fig = next(f for f in spec["figures"] if f["id"] == "F-A1")
        self.assertEqual((fig["type"], fig["pooled"], fig["prediction"], fig["log_scale"], fig["null"]),
                         ("forest", "R-A1-001", "R-A1-005", True, 1))
        self.assertEqual([r["result"] for r in fig["rows"]], ["R-A1-011", "R-A1-012", "R-A1-013"])
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "forest-spec")[0], 0)            # idempotent: one entry per id
        spec = json.loads((self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / "figures.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(sum(f["id"] == "F-A1" for f in spec["figures"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "r-script")[0], 0)
        script = (self.p / meta.R_SCRIPT).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("library(metafor)", script)
        self.assertIn('"05_analysis/meta/meta-input.csv"', script)
        self.assertNotIn(str(self.p), script)
        self.assertNotIn(str(self.t), script)
        plan = (self.p / meta.PLAN_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        self.assertEqual(plan[0], "key,measure,model,hksj")
        self.assertIn("A1,RR,RE-REML,no", plan)

    def test_usage_errors(self):
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr" / "extraction.csv").unlink()
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "run")[0], 2)
        (self.p / "03_protocol" / "sr-protocol.md").write_text(REGISTER.replace("| RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n| A2",
                                                                                "| XX | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n| A2"),
                                                                encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "run")[0], 2)
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.t / "none", "run")[0], 2)


class TestRDiscovery(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = review_project(self.t)

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def fake(self, *parts) -> Path:
        f = self.t.joinpath(*parts)
        f.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        f.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
        return f

    def test_windows_newest_version_first(self):
        for v in ("R-4.5.0", "R-4.10.1", "R-4.6.1"):
            self.fake("lad", "Programs", "R", v, "bin", "Rscript.exe")
        got = meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={"LOCALAPPDATA": str(self.t / "lad")}, system="Windows", which=lambda n: None)
        self.assertEqual(got.parent.parent.name, "R-4.10.1")
        pf = self.fake("pf", "R", "R-4.4.0", "bin", "Rscript.exe")
        got = meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={"ProgramFiles": str(self.t / "pf")}, system="Windows", which=lambda n: None)
        self.assertEqual(got, pf)

    def test_order_config_path_r_home(self):
        cfg = self.fake("cfg", "Rscript")
        rhome = self.fake("rhome", "bin", "Rscript")
        with open(self.p / "project.yaml", "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(f'tools:\n  rscript: "{cfg.as_posix()}"\n')
        on_path = self.fake("path", "Rscript")
        got = meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={"R_HOME": str(self.t / "rhome")}, system="Linux", which=lambda n: str(on_path))
        self.assertEqual(got, cfg)
        cands = meta.rscript_candidates(self.p, env={"R_HOME": str(self.t / "rhome")}, system="Linux",
                                        which=lambda n: str(on_path))
        self.assertEqual(cands[:3], [cfg, on_path, rhome])

    def test_macos_and_linux_standard_locations(self):
        got = meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={}, system="Darwin", which=lambda n: None,
                                is_file=lambda p: p.as_posix() == "/opt/homebrew/bin/Rscript")
        self.assertEqual(got.as_posix(), "/opt/homebrew/bin/Rscript")
        cands = [c.as_posix() for c in meta.rscript_candidates(self.p, env={}, system="Darwin", which=lambda n: None)]
        self.assertEqual(cands[0], "/Library/Frameworks/R.framework/Resources/bin/Rscript")
        got = meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={}, system="Linux", which=lambda n: None,
                                is_file=lambda p: p.as_posix() == "/usr/bin/Rscript")
        self.assertEqual(got.as_posix(), "/usr/bin/Rscript")
        self.assertIsNone(meta.find_rscript(self.p, env={}, system="Linux", which=lambda n: None, is_file=lambda p: False))

    def test_status_and_check_without_r_exit_2_with_hint(self):
        for system in ("Windows", "Darwin", "Linux"):
            self.assertIn('install.packages("metafor")', meta.install_hint(system))
            self.assertIn("cran.r-project.org", meta.install_hint(system))
        with mock.patch.object(meta, "find_rscript", return_value=None):
            code, out, err = cli(self.p, "r-status")
            self.assertEqual(code, 2)
            self.assertIn("R not found", err)
            self.assertIn("tools.rscript", err)
            self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "r-check")[0], 2)
        with mock.patch.object(meta, "find_rscript", return_value=Path("Rscript")), \
                mock.patch.object(meta, "r_versions", return_value=("4.6.1", None)):
            code, out, err = cli(self.p, "r-status")
            self.assertEqual(code, 2)
            self.assertIn("metafor not installed", err)
        with mock.patch.object(meta, "find_rscript", return_value=Path("Rscript")), \
                mock.patch.object(meta, "r_versions", return_value=("4.6.1", "5.2.1")):
            code, out, err = cli(self.p, "r-status")
            self.assertEqual(code, 0)
            self.assertIn("metafor 5.2.1", out)

    def test_compare_flags_mismatch_as_warn(self):
        py = {"A1": {"estimate": 0.5, "ci": [0.1, 0.9], "tau2": 0.02, "i2": 40.0, "q": 3.0, "pi": None, "k": 3}}
        rr = {"A1": {"estimate": 0.5000001, "ci": [0.1, 0.9], "tau2": 0.03, "i2": 40.0, "q": 3.0, "pi": None, "k": 3}}
        found = meta.compare(py, rr)
        self.assertEqual(len(found), 1)
        self.assertEqual(found[0][0], "warn")
        self.assertIn("A1 tau2", found[0][2])
        self.assertIn("missing", meta.compare(py, {})[0][2])
```

```python
class TestMetaR(unittest.TestCase):

        if cls.rs is None:
            raise unittest.SkipTest("R not found (optional)")
        try:
            _, mf = meta.r_versions(cls.rs)

            raise unittest.SkipTest(f"R not usable: {e}")
        if mf is None:

    def test_r_check_agrees_with_python(self):

        try:
            p = review_project(t)

            (p / "04_data" / "sr" / "extraction.csv").write_text(EXTRACTION, encoding="utf-8")
            out = io.StringIO()

                self.assertEqual(meta.main([str(p), "run"]), 0)
                self.assertEqual(meta.main([str(p), "r-check"]), 0)
            self.assertNotIn("E-META", out.getvalue())
            self.assertIn("r-check: 0 mismatches", out.getvalue())
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(t, ignore_errors=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
```
