```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Meta-analysis for a systematic review (standard library only), with an optional cross-check in R metafor.

  python meta.py <project> run           pooled estimates from 04_data/sr/extraction.csv and the register
  python meta.py <project> forest-spec   forest-plot entries F-A<n> in 07_present/figures/figures.json
  python meta.py <project> r-script      writes 05_analysis/meta/metafor.R (reads the files `run` wrote)
  python meta.py <project> r-status      where Rscript is and whether metafor is installed
  python meta.py <project> r-check       runs metafor.R and compares with the Python values (E-META warn)

The register is the "## Analysis register" table of 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md (columns ID, Type, Outcome,
Timepoint, Measure, Model, HKSJ, Experimental, Comparator, Factor; Type pool | subgroup | leave-one-out; A0 =
PRISMA counts, skipped). Measures: OR, RR, RD (2x2 counts; 0.5 added to every cell of a study with a zero cell;
studies with no events, or only events, in both groups excluded with a log line), MD, SMD (Hedges' g, exact
correction), GEN (es, se), GEN-OR / GEN-RR / GEN-HR (log-scale es, se). Models: RE-REML (default), RE-DL,
CE-IV, CE-MH (OR, RR); HKSJ yes = Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman interval. Reported: estimate and 95% CI, tau²,
I², Q and its p, 95% prediction interval (random effects, k >= 3), Egger's test (k >= 10), one subgroup factor
(test for subgroup differences), leave-one-out. Fewer than 2 studies: no pooling, a narrative log line.
Results go to 05_analysis/output/meta-log.txt (P2 log format) and are merged into the results ledger as
R-A<n>-NNN (status prespecified, or deviation when decisions.md has "DEVIATION A<n>:"). A scoping review is
refused (PRISMA-ScR: chart, do not pool).
R is optional: Rscript is looked for in project.yaml tools.rscript, PATH, R_HOME, then the usual install folders
of the OS. The Python engine stays the reference for the ledger; r-check only reports differences (rel. tol. 1e-4).
Exit codes: 0 = done (r-check: warnings allowed); 1 = refused (scoping review, ledger locked by G3, label change);
2 = usage error, bad register or extraction, R or metafor missing (r-status, r-check), I/O error.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import platform
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import NormalDist

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import derived, projectcfg, resultlog  # noqa: E402
import analysis_plan  # noqa: E402
import results_ledger  # noqa: E402
import screening  # noqa: E402

EXTRACTION = "04_data/sr/extraction.csv"
LOG_REL = "05_analysis/output/meta-log.txt"
DOC_REL = "05_analysis/output/meta-results.json"
PY_REL = "05_analysis/meta/python-results.json"
INPUT_REL = "05_analysis/meta/meta-input.csv"
PLAN_REL = "05_analysis/meta/meta-plan.csv"
R_SCRIPT = "05_analysis/meta/metafor.R"
R_OUT = "05_analysis/meta/metafor-results.json"
FIG_REL = "07_present/figures/figures.json"
MEASURES = ("OR", "RR", "RD", "MD", "SMD", "GEN", "GEN-OR", "GEN-RR", "GEN-HR")
MODELS = ("RE-REML", "RE-DL", "CE-IV", "CE-MH")
TYPES = ("pool", "subgroup", "leave-one-out")
RATIO = {"OR", "RR", "GEN-OR", "GEN-RR", "GEN-HR"}
KIND = {"OR": "or", "RR": "rr", "RD": "rd", "MD": "md", "SMD": "smd", "GEN": "other", "GEN-OR": "or",
        "GEN-RR": "rr", "GEN-HR": "hr"}
MODEL_TEXT = {"RE-REML": "random effects, REML", "RE-DL": "random effects, DerSimonian-Laird",
              "CE-IV": "common effect, inverse variance", "CE-MH": "common effect, Mantel-Haenszel"}
INPUT_COLS = ["key", "study", "a", "n1", "c", "n2", "m1", "sd1", "m2", "sd2", "yi", "vi"]
Z = NormalDist()
Z975 = Z.inv_cdf(0.975)


class Refused(Exception):
    """exit 1"""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- distributions (stdlib)

def _betacf(a: float, b: float, x: float) -> float:
    tiny = 1e-300
    qab, qap, qam = a + b, a + 1.0, a - 1.0
    c, d = 1.0, 1.0 - qab * x / qap
    d = 1.0 / (d if abs(d) > tiny else tiny)
    h = d
    for m in range(1, 500):
        m2 = 2 * m
        aa = m * (b - m) * x / ((qam + m2) * (a + m2))
        d = 1.0 + aa * d
        d = 1.0 / (d if abs(d) > tiny else tiny)
        c = 1.0 + aa / c
        c = c if abs(c) > tiny else tiny
        h *= d * c
        aa = -(a + m) * (qab + m) * x / ((a + m2) * (qap + m2))
        d = 1.0 + aa * d
        d = 1.0 / (d if abs(d) > tiny else tiny)
        c = 1.0 + aa / c
        c = c if abs(c) > tiny else tiny
        de = d * c
        h *= de
        if abs(de - 1.0) < 3e-16:
            break
    return h


def betai(a: float, b: float, x: float) -> float:
    """Regularised incomplete beta I_x(a, b)."""
    if x <= 0:
        return 0.0
    if x >= 1:
        return 1.0
    bt = math.exp(math.lgamma(a + b) - math.lgamma(a) - math.lgamma(b) + a * math.log(x) + b * math.log1p(-x))
    if x < (a + 1) / (a + b + 2):
        return bt * _betacf(a, b, x) / a
    return 1.0 - bt * _betacf(b, a, 1.0 - x) / b


def t_p2(t: float, df: float) -> float:
    """Two-sided p of Student's t."""
    return betai(df / 2, 0.5, df / (df + t * t))


def t_ppf(q: float, df: float) -> float:
    """Quantile of Student's t for q in (0.5, 1), by bisection on the CDF."""
    lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
    while 1 - 0.5 * t_p2(hi, df) < q:
        hi *= 2
    for _ in range(200):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if 1 - 0.5 * t_p2(mid, df) < q:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return (lo + hi) / 2


def _gammaincc(a: float, x: float) -> float:
    if x <= 0:
        return 1.0
    lead = -x + a * math.log(x) - math.lgamma(a)
    if x < a + 1:
        ap, s = a, 1.0 / a
        d = s
        for _ in range(2000):
            ap += 1
            d *= x / ap
            s += d
            if abs(d) < abs(s) * 1e-16:
                break
        return max(0.0, 1.0 - s * math.exp(lead))
    tiny = 1e-300
    b = x + 1 - a
    c, d = 1 / tiny, 1 / b
    h = d
    for i in range(1, 2000):
        an = -i * (i - a)
        b += 2
        d = an * d + b
        d = 1 / (d if abs(d) > tiny else tiny)
        c = b + an / c
        c = c if abs(c) > tiny else tiny
        de = d * c
        h *= de
        if abs(de - 1) < 1e-16:
            break
    return math.exp(lead) * h


def chi2_sf(x: float, df: float) -> float:
    return _gammaincc(df / 2, x / 2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- effect sizes and pooling

def _double_zero(s: dict) -> bool:
    return (s["a"] == 0 and s["c"] == 0) or (s["a"] == s["n1"] and s["c"] == s["n2"])


def study_effect(measure: str, s: dict) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
    if measure in ("OR", "RR", "RD"):
        a, n1, c, n2 = (float(s[k]) for k in ("a", "n1", "c", "n2"))
        b, d = n1 - a, n2 - c
        if min(a, b, c, d) < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{s.get('study', '?')}: events larger than the group size")
        if _double_zero(s):
            return None
        if 0 in (a, b, c, d):
            a, b, c, d = a + 0.5, b + 0.5, c + 0.5, d + 0.5
            n1, n2 = a + b, c + d
        if measure == "OR":
            return math.log(a * d / (b * c)), 1 / a + 1 / b + 1 / c + 1 / d
        if measure == "RR":
            return math.log((a / n1) / (c / n2)), 1 / a - 1 / n1 + 1 / c - 1 / n2
        return a / n1 - c / n2, a * b / n1 ** 3 + c * d / n2 ** 3
    if measure in ("MD", "SMD"):
        m1, sd1, n1, m2, sd2, n2 = (float(s[k]) for k in ("m1", "sd1", "n1", "m2", "sd2", "n2"))
        if measure == "MD":
            return m1 - m2, sd1 ** 2 / n1 + sd2 ** 2 / n2
        m = n1 + n2 - 2
        j = math.exp(math.lgamma(m / 2) - 0.5 * math.log(m / 2) - math.lgamma((m - 1) / 2))
        sp = math.sqrt(((n1 - 1) * sd1 ** 2 + (n2 - 1) * sd2 ** 2) / m)
        g = j * (m1 - m2) / sp
        return g, 1 / n1 + 1 / n2 + g * g / (2 * (n1 + n2))
    if measure.startswith("GEN"):
        return float(s["yi"]), float(s["vi"])
    raise ValueError(f"unknown measure {measure!r}")


def tau2_dl(y: list[float], v: list[float]) -> float:
    w = [1 / x for x in v]
    sw = sum(w)
    mu = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, y)) / sw
    q = sum(wi * (yi - mu) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(w, y))
    return max(0.0, (q - (len(y) - 1)) / (sw - sum(x * x for x in w) / sw))


def tau2_reml(y: list[float], v: list[float], tol: float = 1e-12, maxit: int = 10000) -> float:
    """REML fixed point tau² = Σw²((y-μ)² - v)/Σw² + 1/Σw, truncated at 0, started at DL."""
    t = tau2_dl(y, v)
    for _ in range(maxit):
        w = [1 / (vi + t) for vi in v]
        sw = sum(w)
        mu = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, y)) / sw
        new = max(0.0, sum(wi * wi * ((yi - mu) ** 2 - vi) for wi, yi, vi in zip(w, y, v)) / sum(x * x for x in w) + 1 / sw)
        if abs(new - t) < tol * max(1.0, t):
            return new
        t = new
    raise ValueError("REML did not converge")


def pool(y: list[float], v: list[float], model: str, hksj: bool = False) -> dict:
    k = len(y)
    if k < 2:
        raise ValueError("pooling needs at least 2 studies")
    w = [1 / x for x in v]
    sw = sum(w)
    mu_ce = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, y)) / sw
    q = sum(wi * (yi - mu_ce) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(w, y))
    df = k - 1
    if model == "CE-IV":
        tau2 = 0.0
        i2 = max(0.0, (q - df) / q) * 100 if q > 0 else 0.0
    elif model in ("RE-DL", "RE-REML"):
        tau2 = tau2_dl(y, v) if model == "RE-DL" else tau2_reml(y, v)
        s2 = df * sw / (sw ** 2 - sum(x * x for x in w))
        i2 = 100 * tau2 / (tau2 + s2)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"model {model!r} needs pool_mh or is unknown")
    ws = [1 / (vi + tau2) for vi in v]
    sws = sum(ws)
    mu = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(ws, y)) / sws
    se = math.sqrt(1 / sws)
    if hksj:
        qh = sum(wi * (yi - mu) ** 2 for wi, yi in zip(ws, y)) / df
        se = math.sqrt(qh / sws)
        crit, p = t_ppf(0.975, df), t_p2(mu / se, df) if se > 0 else 0.0
    else:
        crit, p = Z975, 2 * Z.cdf(-abs(mu / se))
    out = {"k": k, "estimate": mu, "se": se, "ci": [mu - crit * se, mu + crit * se], "p": p, "tau2": tau2,
           "i2": i2, "q": q, "q_df": df, "q_p": chi2_sf(q, df), "pi": None}
    if model != "CE-IV" and k >= 3:
        h = t_ppf(0.975, k - 2) * math.sqrt(tau2 + se ** 2)
        out["pi"] = [mu - h, mu + h]
    return out


def pool_mh(studies: list[dict], measure: str) -> dict:
    keep = [s for s in studies if not _double_zero(s)]
    k = len(keep)
    if k < 2:
        raise ValueError("pooling needs at least 2 studies")
    if measure == "OR":
        r_ = s_ = pr = psqr = qs = 0.0
        for s in keep:
            a, n1, c, n2 = (float(s[x]) for x in ("a", "n1", "c", "n2"))
            b, d, n = n1 - a, n2 - c, n1 + n2
            ri, si = a * d / n, b * c / n
            pi_, qi = (a + d) / n, (b + c) / n
            r_, s_ = r_ + ri, s_ + si
            pr, psqr, qs = pr + pi_ * ri, psqr + pi_ * si + qi * ri, qs + qi * si
        if r_ <= 0 or s_ <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Mantel-Haenszel OR is not estimable (zero sums)")
        est = math.log(r_ / s_)
        var = pr / (2 * r_ * r_) + psqr / (2 * r_ * s_) + qs / (2 * s_ * s_)
    elif measure == "RR":
        r_ = s_ = vv = 0.0
        for s in keep:
            a, n1, c, n2 = (float(s[x]) for x in ("a", "n1", "c", "n2"))
            n = n1 + n2
            r_, s_ = r_ + a * n2 / n, s_ + c * n1 / n
            vv += (n1 * n2 * (a + c) - a * c * n) / (n * n)
        if r_ <= 0 or s_ <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Mantel-Haenszel RR is not estimable (zero sums)")
        est = math.log(r_ / s_)
        var = vv / (r_ * s_)
    else:
        raise ValueError("Mantel-Haenszel is for OR and RR only")
    se = math.sqrt(var)
    eff = [study_effect(measure, s) for s in keep]
    q = sum((yi - est) ** 2 / vi for yi, vi in eff)
    df = k - 1
    return {"k": k, "estimate": est, "se": se, "ci": [est - Z975 * se, est + Z975 * se],
            "p": 2 * Z.cdf(-abs(est / se)), "tau2": 0.0, "i2": max(0.0, (q - df) / q) * 100 if q > 0 else 0.0,
            "q": q, "q_df": df, "q_p": chi2_sf(q, df), "pi": None}


def egger(y: list[float], v: list[float]) -> dict:
    """Weighted regression of y on se (weights 1/v); the slope on se is Egger's intercept (plan D14)."""
    k = len(y)
    if k < 3:
        raise ValueError("Egger's test needs at least 3 studies")
    x = [math.sqrt(vi) for vi in v]
    w = [1 / vi for vi in v]
    sw, sx = sum(w), sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, x))
    sxx = sum(a * b * b for a, b in zip(w, x))
    sy = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(w, y))
    sxy = sum(a * b * c for a, b, c in zip(w, x, y))
    det = sw * sxx - sx * sx
    b1 = (sw * sxy - sx * sy) / det
    b0 = (sxx * sy - sx * sxy) / det
    s2 = sum(wi * (yi - b0 - b1 * xi) ** 2 for wi, xi, yi in zip(w, x, y)) / (k - 2)
    se = math.sqrt(s2 * sw / det)
    t = b1 / se
    return {"intercept": b1, "se": se, "t": t, "p": t_p2(t, k - 2)}


def subgroup_test(fits: list[dict]) -> dict:
    w = [1 / f["se"] ** 2 for f in fits]
    mu = sum(a * f["estimate"] for a, f in zip(w, fits)) / sum(w)
    q = sum(a * (f["estimate"] - mu) ** 2 for a, f in zip(w, fits))
    df = len(fits) - 1
    return {"q": q, "df": df, "p": chi2_sf(q, df)}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- register and data

def load_register(project: Path) -> list[dict]:
    path = analysis_plan.register_path(project)
    out = []
    for r in analysis_plan.parse_register(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")):
        typ = (r.get("type") or "").strip().casefold()
        if r["id"] == "A0" or typ == "counts":
            continue
        a = {"id": r["id"], "type": typ, "outcome": (r.get("outcome") or "").strip(),
             "timepoint": (r.get("timepoint") or "").strip(), "measure": (r.get("measure") or "").strip().upper(),
             "model": (r.get("model") or "RE-REML").strip().upper(),
             "hksj": (r.get("hksj") or "").strip().casefold() in ("yes", "y", "true", "có"),
             "experimental": (r.get("experimental") or "").strip(), "comparator": (r.get("comparator") or "").strip(),
             "factor": (r.get("factor") or "").strip()}
        if typ not in TYPES:
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: Type {typ!r} must be one of {', '.join(TYPES)} (or counts for A0)")
        if a["measure"] not in MEASURES:
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: Measure {a['measure']!r} must be one of {', '.join(MEASURES)}")
        if a["model"] not in MODELS:
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: Model {a['model']!r} must be one of {', '.join(MODELS)}")
        if a["model"] == "CE-MH" and a["measure"] not in ("OR", "RR"):
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: Mantel-Haenszel is for OR and RR only")
        if typ == "subgroup" and not a["factor"]:
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: a subgroup analysis needs the Factor column (an extraction column)")
        if not a["experimental"] or (not a["measure"].startswith("GEN") and not a["comparator"]):
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: Experimental and Comparator name the extraction 'group' values")
        out.append(a)
    return out


def _num(text, what: str) -> float:
    t = (text or "").strip().replace(",", ".")
    try:
        return float(t)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"{what}: {text!r} is not a number") from None


def studies_for(rows: list[dict], a: dict) -> list[dict]:
    by: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = {}
    for r in rows:
        if (r.get("outcome") or "").strip().casefold() == a["outcome"].casefold() and \
                (r.get("timepoint") or "").strip().casefold() == a["timepoint"].casefold():
            by.setdefault((r.get("study_id") or "").strip(), {})[(r.get("group") or "").strip().casefold()] = r
    out = []
    for sid, groups in by.items():
        e, c = groups.get(a["experimental"].casefold()), groups.get(a["comparator"].casefold())
        where = f"{a['id']} {sid}"
        s: dict = {"study": sid}
        if a["measure"].startswith("GEN"):
            if e is None:
                raise ValueError(f"{where}: no '{a['experimental']}' row with es and se")
            s.update(yi=_num(e.get("es"), f"{where} es"), vi=_num(e.get("se"), f"{where} se") ** 2)
            s["n"] = int(_num(e.get("n"), f"{where} n")) if (e.get("n") or "").strip() else 0
        else:
            if e is None or c is None:
                raise ValueError(f"{where}: needs rows for '{a['experimental']}' and '{a['comparator']}'")
            if a["measure"] in ("OR", "RR", "RD"):
                s.update(a=int(_num(e.get("events"), f"{where} events")), n1=int(_num(e.get("n"), f"{where} n")),
                         c=int(_num(c.get("events"), f"{where} events")), n2=int(_num(c.get("n"), f"{where} n")))
            else:
                s.update(m1=_num(e.get("mean"), f"{where} mean"), sd1=_num(e.get("sd"), f"{where} sd"),
                         n1=int(_num(e.get("n"), f"{where} n")), m2=_num(c.get("mean"), f"{where} mean"),
                         sd2=_num(c.get("sd"), f"{where} sd"), n2=int(_num(c.get("n"), f"{where} n")))
            s["n"] = s["n1"] + s["n2"]
        if a["factor"]:
            level = ((e or {}).get(a["factor"]) or "").strip()
            if not level:
                raise ValueError(f"{where}: no value in the factor column {a['factor']!r}")
            s["level"] = level
        out.append(s)
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- run

class Run:
    def __init__(self, project: Path):
        self.project = project
        self.log = resultlog.ResultLog(project, EXTRACTION, LOG_REL, "meta.py")
        self.dump: dict = {}
        self.plan: list[dict] = []
        self.inputs: list[dict] = []

    def out(self, a: dict, x: float) -> float:
        return math.exp(x) if a["measure"] in RATIO else x

    def fit(self, a: dict, kept: list) -> dict:
        if a["model"] == "CE-MH":
            return pool_mh([s for s, _ in kept], a["measure"])
        return pool([e[0] for _, e in kept], [e[1] for _, e in kept], a["model"], a["hksj"])

    def remember(self, key: str, a: dict, kept: list, f: dict) -> None:
        self.dump[key] = {"estimate": f["estimate"], "ci": f["ci"], "tau2": f["tau2"], "i2": f["i2"], "q": f["q"],
                          "pi": f["pi"], "k": f["k"]}
        self.plan.append({"key": key, "measure": a["measure"], "model": a["model"], "hksj": "yes" if a["hksj"] else "no"})
        for s, _ in kept:
            self.inputs.append({"key": key, **{c: s.get(c, "") for c in INPUT_COLS if c not in ("key", "vi")},
                                "vi": s.get("vi", "")})

    def add_pooled(self, a, status, f, kept, number, label) -> None:
        n = sum(s.get("n") or 0 for s, _ in kept)
        self.log.add(a["id"], status, label, self.out(a, f["estimate"]), KIND[a["measure"]],
                     ci=[self.out(a, f["ci"][0]), self.out(a, f["ci"][1])], p=f["p"], n=n or None, count=f["k"],
                     number=number)

    def add_het(self, a, status, f, base: int, scope: str = "") -> None:
        if a["model"] in ("RE-REML", "RE-DL"):
            scale = ", log scale" if a["measure"] in RATIO else ""
            self.log.add(a["id"], status, f"tau² (between-study variance{scale}){scope}", f["tau2"], "tau2", number=base + 1)
        self.log.add(a["id"], status, f"I² (heterogeneity){scope}", f["i2"], "percent", number=base + 2)

    def pool_analysis(self, a: dict, status: str, kept: list) -> None:
        kind = KIND[a["measure"]]
        for j, (s, (yi, vi)) in enumerate(kept, start=11):
            se = math.sqrt(vi)
            self.log.add(a["id"], status, f"{s['study']} {a['measure']}", self.out(a, yi), kind,
                         ci=[self.out(a, yi - Z975 * se), self.out(a, yi + Z975 * se)], n=s.get("n") or None, number=j)
        k = len(kept)
        if k < 2:
            self.log.log(f"{a['id']} {a['outcome']}: {k} {'study' if k == 1 else 'studies'}; not pooled (narrative synthesis)")
            return
        f = self.fit(a, kept)
        self.remember(a["id"], a, kept, f)
        self.add_pooled(a, status, f, kept, 1, f"pooled {a['measure']} ({MODEL_TEXT[a['model']]}"
                                               f"{', HKSJ' if a['hksj'] else ''}, {k} studies)")
        self.add_het(a, status, f, 1)
        self.log.add(a["id"], status, "Q (heterogeneity)", f["q"], "other", p=f["q_p"], number=4)
        if f["pi"]:
            self.log.add(a["id"], status, "95% prediction interval", self.out(a, f["estimate"]), kind,
                         ci=[self.out(a, f["pi"][0]), self.out(a, f["pi"][1])], number=5)
        if k >= 10:
            eg = egger([e[0] for _, e in kept], [e[1] for _, e in kept])
            self.log.add(a["id"], status, "Egger's test intercept", eg["intercept"], "other", p=eg["p"], number=6)

    def subgroup_analysis(self, a: dict, status: str, kept: list) -> None:
        levels = sorted({s["level"] for s, _ in kept})
        if len(levels) > 98:
            raise ValueError(f"{a['id']}: too many subgroup levels")
        fits = []
        for j, lev in enumerate(levels, start=1):
            sub = [(s, e) for s, e in kept if s["level"] == lev]
            if len(sub) < 2:
                (s, (yi, vi)), = sub
                self.log.log(f"{a['id']} {a['factor']} = {lev}: 1 study; not pooled")
                fits.append({"estimate": yi, "se": math.sqrt(vi)})
                continue
            f = self.fit(a, sub)
            self.remember(f"{a['id']}|{lev}", a, sub, f)
            self.add_pooled(a, status, f, sub, 10 * j + 1,
                            f"pooled {a['measure']} in {a['factor']} = {lev} ({len(sub)} studies)")
            self.add_het(a, status, f, 10 * j + 1, f" in {a['factor']} = {lev}")
            fits.append(f)
        if len(fits) >= 2:
            t = subgroup_test(fits)
            self.log.add(a["id"], status, f"test for subgroup differences ({a['factor']}, Q between)", t["q"], "other",
                         p=t["p"], number=1)

    def loo_analysis(self, a: dict, status: str, kept: list) -> None:
        for j, (s, _) in enumerate(kept, start=1):
            rest = [x for x in kept if x[0] is not s]
            if len(rest) < 2:
                continue
            f = self.fit(a, rest)
            self.remember(f"{a['id']}|-{s['study']}", a, rest, f)
            self.add_pooled(a, status, f, rest, j, f"pooled {a['measure']} without {s['study']}")

    def write_side_files(self) -> None:
        base = self.project / "05_analysis" / "meta"
        base.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.project / PY_REL, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(self.dump, indent=2) + "\n")
        screening.write_csv(self.project / INPUT_REL, INPUT_COLS, self.inputs)
        screening.write_csv(self.project / PLAN_REL, ["key", "measure", "model", "hksj"], self.plan)


def run(project: Path) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    if projectcfg.read_project_yaml(project).get("design") == "scoping-review":
        raise Refused("scoping reviews chart and summarise sources; they do not pool or rate risk of bias "
                      "(PRISMA-ScR). To pool, convert to a systematic review protocol and lock it again (G2).")
    analyses = load_register(project)
    if not analyses:
        raise ValueError("the analysis register has no pool, subgroup or leave-one-out row")
    rows = screening.read_csv(project / EXTRACTION)
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"no {EXTRACTION}: the consensus extraction is needed (extract_check.py)")
    dec_path = project / "decisions.md"
    deviations = analysis_plan.declared(dec_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") if dec_path.is_file() else "")["DEVIATION"]
    r = Run(project)
    for a in analyses:
        status = "deviation" if a["id"] in deviations else "prespecified"
        kept = []
        for s in studies_for(rows, a):
            e = study_effect(a["measure"], s)
            if e is None:
                r.log.log(f"{a['id']} {s['study']}: excluded from pooling (no events, or only events, in both groups)")
            else:
                kept.append((s, e))
        {"pool": r.pool_analysis, "subgroup": r.subgroup_analysis, "leave-one-out": r.loo_analysis}[a["type"]](a, status, kept)
    doc = r.log.save(DOC_REL)
    r.write_side_files()
    return resultlog.import_merged(project, doc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- figures and R

def forest_spec(project: Path) -> list[str]:
    led = derived.ledger_by_id(project)
    spec_path = project / FIG_REL
    spec = json.loads(spec_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) if spec_path.is_file() else {"figures": []}
    figs = {f.get("id"): f for f in spec.get("figures", [])}
    order = [f.get("id") for f in spec.get("figures", [])]
    made = []
    for a in (x for x in load_register(project) if x["type"] == "pool"):
        pooled = f"R-{a['id']}-001"
        if pooled not in led:
            continue
        rows = [{"label": led[rid]["label"].rsplit(" ", 1)[0], "result": rid}
                for rid in sorted(led) if rid.startswith(f"R-{a['id']}-") and 11 <= int(rid[-3:]) < 900]
        fig = {"id": f"F-{a['id']}", "type": "forest", "title": f"{a['outcome']} ({a['timepoint']})",
               "null": 1 if a["measure"] in RATIO else 0, "log_scale": a["measure"] in RATIO,
               "x_label": f"{a['measure']} (95% CI)", "source": LOG_REL, "rows": rows, "pooled": pooled}
        if f"R-{a['id']}-005" in led:
            fig["prediction"] = f"R-{a['id']}-005"
        if fig["id"] not in figs:
            order.append(fig["id"])
        figs[fig["id"]] = fig
        made.append(fig["id"])
    spec["figures"] = [figs[i] for i in order]
    spec_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(spec_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps(spec, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    return made


R_TEXT = r'''# Generated by meta.py r-script: cross-check of the Python meta-analysis with the R package metafor.
# Run from the project folder: Rscript 05_analysis/meta/metafor.R   (meta.py r-check does this)
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(metafor))
inp <- read.csv("05_analysis/meta/meta-input.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
plan <- read.csv("05_analysis/meta/meta-plan.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
num <- function(x) if (length(x) == 0 || is.null(x) || is.na(x[1])) "null" else formatC(x[1], digits = 15, format = "g")
fit <- function(d, measure, model, hksj) {
  method <- switch(model, "RE-REML" = "REML", "RE-DL" = "DL", "CE-IV" = "FE", "CE-MH" = "MH")
  if (method == "MH") return(rma.mh(ai = d$a, bi = d$n1 - d$a, ci = d$c, di = d$n2 - d$c, measure = measure,
                                    add = 1/2, to = "only0", drop00 = TRUE))
  if (measure %in% c("OR", "RR", "RD")) {
    es <- escalc(measure = measure, ai = d$a, bi = d$n1 - d$a, ci = d$c, di = d$n2 - d$c, add = 1/2,
                 to = "only0", drop00 = TRUE)
  } else if (measure %in% c("MD", "SMD")) {
    es <- escalc(measure = measure, m1i = d$m1, sd1i = d$sd1, n1i = d$n1, m2i = d$m2, sd2i = d$sd2, n2i = d$n2)
  } else {
    es <- data.frame(yi = d$yi, vi = d$vi)
  }
  rma(yi, vi, data = es, method = method, test = if (hksj == "yes") "knha" else "z")
}
out <- character(0)
for (i in seq_len(nrow(plan))) {
  p <- plan[i, ]
  r <- fit(inp[inp$key == p$key, ], p$measure, p$model, p$hksj)
  tau2 <- if (is.null(r$tau2)) 0 else r$tau2
  pi <- if (p$model %in% c("RE-REML", "RE-DL") && r$k >= 3) r$b[1] + c(-1, 1) * qt(0.975, r$k - 2) * sqrt(tau2 + r$se^2) else c(NA, NA)
  out <- c(out, sprintf('  "%s": {"estimate": %s, "ci": [%s, %s], "tau2": %s, "i2": %s, "q": %s, "pi": [%s, %s], "k": %d}',
                        p$key, num(r$b[1]), num(r$ci.lb), num(r$ci.ub), num(tau2), num(r$I2), num(r$QE),
                        num(pi[1]), num(pi[2]), as.integer(r$k)))
}
eng <- sprintf('  "engine": "%s; metafor %s"', R.version.string, as.character(packageVersion("metafor")))
con <- file("05_analysis/meta/metafor-results.json", open = "wb")
writeLines(c("{", paste(c(eng, out), collapse = ",\n"), "}"), con, sep = "\n")
close(con)
'''


def write_r_script(project: Path) -> Path:
    if not (project / PLAN_REL).is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"no {PLAN_REL}: run meta.py run first")
    path = project / R_SCRIPT
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(R_TEXT)
    return path


def _version_key(p: Path) -> tuple:
    return tuple(int(x) for x in re.findall(r"\d+", p.name))


def rscript_candidates(project: Path, env=None, system=None, which=shutil.which) -> list[Path]:
    env = os.environ if env is None else env
    system = system or platform.system()
    exe = "Rscript.exe" if system == "Windows" else "Rscript"
    out: list[Path] = []
    cfg = projectcfg.get(project, "tools.rscript")
    if isinstance(cfg, str) and cfg:
        out.append(Path(cfg))
    w = which(exe) or which("Rscript")
    if w:
        out.append(Path(w))
    if env.get("R_HOME"):
        out.append(Path(env["R_HOME"]) / "bin" / exe)
    if system == "Windows":
        for var, sub in (("LOCALAPPDATA", ("Programs", "R")), ("ProgramFiles", ("R",))):
            if env.get(var):
                root = Path(env[var]).joinpath(*sub)
                if root.is_dir():
                    out += [d / "bin" / exe for d in sorted(root.glob("R-*"), key=_version_key, reverse=True)]
    elif system == "Darwin":
        out += [Path("/Library/Frameworks/R.framework/Resources/bin/Rscript"), Path("/opt/homebrew/bin/Rscript"),
                Path("/usr/local/bin/Rscript")]
    else:
        out += [Path("/usr/bin/Rscript"), Path("/usr/local/bin/Rscript")]
    return out


def find_rscript(project: Path, env=None, system=None, which=shutil.which, is_file=None) -> Path | None:
    is_file = is_file or (lambda p: p.is_file())
    return next((c for c in rscript_candidates(project, env, system, which) if is_file(c)), None)


def r_versions(rscript: Path) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    code = ("cat(paste(R.version$major, R.version$minor, sep = '.'), "
            "if (requireNamespace('metafor', quietly = TRUE)) as.character(packageVersion('metafor')) else 'none')")
    r = subprocess.run([str(rscript), "-e", code], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)
    parts = r.stdout.split()
    if r.returncode != 0 or len(parts) != 2:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{rscript} did not run: {r.stderr.strip()[:200]}")
    return parts[0], (None if parts[1] == "none" else parts[1])


def install_hint(system: str) -> str:
    get_r = {"Windows": "install R from https://cran.r-project.org/bin/windows/base/",
             "Darwin": "install R from https://cran.r-project.org/bin/macosx/ (or with Homebrew: brew install r)",
             }.get(system, "install R with your package manager (for example: sudo apt install r-base) or from "
                           "https://cran.r-project.org/")
    return (f"R is optional; every result is computed in Python. To cross-check with metafor: {get_r}, then in R run "
            'install.packages("metafor"). If R is installed somewhere unusual, set project.yaml → tools.rscript '
            "to the full path of Rscript. This tool never installs software.")


def _close(a, b) -> bool:
    if a is None or b is None:
        return a is None and b is None
    return abs(a - b) <= 1e-4 * max(abs(a), abs(b)) or abs(a - b) <= 1e-8


def compare(py: dict, rr: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    found = []
    for key, p in py.items():
        r = rr.get(key)
        if r is None:
            found.append(("warn", f"{R_OUT}:1", f"{key}: missing from the metafor results"))
            continue
        pairs = [("estimate", p["estimate"], r.get("estimate")), ("ci lower", p["ci"][0], (r.get("ci") or [None, None])[0]),
                 ("ci upper", p["ci"][1], (r.get("ci") or [None, None])[1]), ("tau2", p["tau2"], r.get("tau2")),
                 ("i2", p["i2"], r.get("i2")), ("q", p["q"], r.get("q"))]
        rpi = r.get("pi") or [None, None]
        ppi = p.get("pi") or [None, None]
        pairs += [("pi lower", ppi[0], rpi[0]), ("pi upper", ppi[1], rpi[1])]
        for name, a, b in pairs:
            if not _close(a, b):
                found.append(("warn", f"{PY_REL}:1", f"{key} {name}: Python {a!r}, metafor {b!r}"))
    return found


def r_check(project: Path) -> tuple[list, str]:
    rs = find_rscript(project)
    if rs is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError("R not found. " + install_hint(platform.system()))
    rv, mf = r_versions(rs)
    if mf is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"metafor not installed for R {rv}. " + install_hint(platform.system()))
    if not (project / PY_REL).is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"no {PY_REL}: run meta.py run first")
    write_r_script(project)
    r = subprocess.run([str(rs), R_SCRIPT], cwd=project, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"metafor.R failed: {r.stderr.strip()[:500]}")
    rr = json.loads((project / R_OUT).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    py = json.loads((project / PY_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return compare(py, rr), f"R {rv}; metafor {mf}"


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["run", "forest-spec", "r-script", "r-status", "r-check"])
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "project.yaml").is_file():
        print(f"meta: {a.project} is not a project folder (no project.yaml)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "run":
            n, bad = run(project)
            for x in bad:
                print(x)
            if bad:
                return 1
            print(f"meta: {n} results written to {LOG_REL} and merged into the results ledger")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "forest-spec":
            made = forest_spec(project)
            print(f"meta: forest entries {', '.join(made) or 'none'} in {FIG_REL}")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "r-script":
            print(f"meta: wrote {write_r_script(project).relative_to(project).as_posix()}")
            return 0
        if a.cmd == "r-status":
            rs = find_rscript(project)
            if rs is None:
                print("r-status: R not found. " + install_hint(platform.system()), file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
            rv, mf = r_versions(rs)
            if mf is None:
                print(f"r-status: Rscript {rs} (R {rv}); metafor not installed. " + install_hint(platform.system()),
                      file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
            print(f"r-status: Rscript {rs} (R {rv}); metafor {mf}; r-check is available")
            return 0
        found, engine = r_check(project)
        for sev, loc, msg in found:
            print(f"E-META {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
        print(f"r-check: {len(found)} mismatches ({engine}; Python stays the reference for the ledger)")
        return 0
    except Refused as e:
        print(f"meta: {e}")
        return 1
    except results_ledger.LockedError as e:
        print(f"meta: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except results_ledger.RelabelError as e:
        print(f"{e}\nmeta: labels changed; ledger not written", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f"meta: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError, csv.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
        print(f"meta: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Notes for the implementer: in `r-check` without R the message goes to stderr and the exit is 2 (`FileNotFoundError` branch). `r_versions` is not called by any unit test except through `mock`. The R script reads only relative paths, so nothing machine-specific is written.

`tests/unit/fixtures/meta/make_goldens.R`:

```r
# Generates tests/unit/fixtures/meta/metafor-goldens.json once, on a maintainer machine with R and metafor.
# Run from the repository root:  Rscript tests/unit/fixtures/meta/make_goldens.R
# Inputs: metafor's example datasets dat.bcg and dat.normand1999, and one small constructed zero-cell set
# written below. Every input is written into the JSON next to the results, so the Python tests read their
# inputs from the file and no number is typed by hand. The unit suite never needs R.
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(metafor))

num <- function(x) if (length(x) == 0 || is.null(x) || is.na(x[1])) "null" else formatC(x[1], digits = 15, format = "g")
arr <- function(x) paste0("[", paste(vapply(x, num, ""), collapse = ", "), "]")
q <- function(x) paste0('"', gsub('"', '\\\\"', as.character(x)), '"')

studies2x2 <- function(id, a, n1, c, n2)
  paste0("[", paste(sprintf('{"study": %s, "a": %d, "n1": %d, "c": %d, "n2": %d}', vapply(as.character(id), q, ""),
                            as.integer(a), as.integer(n1), as.integer(c), as.integer(n2)), collapse = ", "), "]")

summ <- function(r) {
  tau2 <- if (is.null(r$tau2)) 0 else r$tau2
  re <- !is.null(r$method) && r$method %in% c("REML", "DL")
  pi <- if (re && r$k >= 3) r$b[1] + c(-1, 1) * qt(0.975, r$k - 2) * sqrt(tau2 + r$se^2) else NULL
  sprintf('"estimate": %s, "se": %s, "ci": %s, "p": %s, "tau2": %s, "i2": %s, "q": %s, "q_p": %s, "pi": %s, "k": %d',
          num(r$b[1]), num(r$se), arr(c(r$ci.lb, r$ci.ub)), num(r$pval), num(tau2), num(r$I2), num(r$QE),
          num(r$QEp), if (is.null(pi)) "null" else arr(pi), as.integer(r$k))
}
case <- function(name, measure, model, hksj, studies, body)
  sprintf('%s: {"measure": %s, "model": %s, "hksj": %s, "studies": %s, %s}', q(name), q(measure), q(model),
          if (hksj) "true" else "false", studies, body)

bcg <- dat.bcg
b2 <- studies2x2(bcg$trial, bcg$tpos, bcg$tpos + bcg$tneg, bcg$cpos, bcg$cpos + bcg$cneg)
es2 <- function(measure, data = bcg) escalc(measure = measure, ai = tpos, bi = tneg, ci = cpos, di = cneg,
                                            data = data, add = 1/2, to = "only0", drop00 = TRUE)
re2 <- function(measure, method, test = "z", data = bcg) rma(yi, vi, data = es2(measure, data), method = method, test = test)

cases <- c(
  case("bcg_rr_reml", "RR", "RE-REML", FALSE, b2, summ(re2("RR", "REML"))),
  case("bcg_rr_dl", "RR", "RE-DL", FALSE, b2, summ(re2("RR", "DL"))),
  case("bcg_or_reml_hksj", "OR", "RE-REML", TRUE, b2, summ(re2("OR", "REML", "knha"))),
  case("bcg_rd_reml", "RD", "RE-REML", FALSE, b2, summ(re2("RD", "REML"))),
  case("bcg_or_ce_iv", "OR", "CE-IV", FALSE, b2, summ(re2("OR", "FE"))),
  case("bcg_or_mh", "OR", "CE-MH", FALSE, b2,
       summ(rma.mh(ai = tpos, bi = tneg, ci = cpos, di = cneg, data = bcg, measure = "OR"))),
  case("bcg_rr_mh", "RR", "CE-MH", FALSE, b2,
       summ(rma.mh(ai = tpos, bi = tneg, ci = cpos, di = cneg, data = bcg, measure = "RR")))
)

er <- es2("RR")
eg <- coef(summary(lm(yi ~ sqrt(vi), weights = 1 / vi, data = er)))[2, ]
cases <- c(cases, case("bcg_rr_egger", "RR", "RE-REML", FALSE, b2,
  sprintf('"egger": {"intercept": %s, "se": %s, "t": %s, "p": %s}', num(eg[1]), num(eg[2]), num(eg[3]), num(eg[4]))))

lev <- sort(unique(as.character(bcg$alloc)))
fits <- lapply(lev, function(l) re2("RR", "REML", data = bcg[bcg$alloc == l, ]))
qb <- rma(yi = sapply(fits, function(f) f$b[1]), sei = sapply(fits, function(f) f$se), method = "FE")
cases <- c(cases, case("bcg_rr_subgroup_alloc", "RR", "RE-REML", FALSE, b2, sprintf(
  '"factor": {%s}, "levels": {%s}, "qb": %s, "qb_df": %d, "qb_p": %s',
  paste(sprintf("%s: %s", vapply(as.character(bcg$trial), q, ""), vapply(as.character(bcg$alloc), q, "")), collapse = ", "),
  paste(sprintf("%s: {%s}", vapply(lev, q, ""), vapply(fits, summ, "")), collapse = ", "),
  num(qb$QE), length(lev) - 1L, num(qb$QEp))))

l1 <- leave1out(re2("RR", "REML"))
cases <- c(cases, case("bcg_rr_loo", "RR", "RE-REML", FALSE, b2, sprintf('"loo": [%s]', paste(
  sprintf('{"omit": %s, "estimate": %s, "ci": %s}', vapply(as.character(bcg$trial), q, ""),
          vapply(l1$estimate, num, ""), mapply(function(a, b) arr(c(a, b)), l1$ci.lb, l1$ci.ub)), collapse = ", "))))

nd <- dat.normand1999
nds <- paste0("[", paste(sprintf('{"study": %s, "m1": %s, "sd1": %s, "n1": %d, "m2": %s, "sd2": %s, "n2": %d}',
  vapply(as.character(nd$study), q, ""), vapply(nd$m1i, num, ""), vapply(nd$sd1i, num, ""), as.integer(nd$n1i),
  vapply(nd$m2i, num, ""), vapply(nd$sd2i, num, ""), as.integer(nd$n2i)), collapse = ", "), "]")
cont <- function(measure) rma(yi, vi, method = "REML", data = escalc(measure = measure, m1i = m1i, sd1i = sd1i,
  n1i = n1i, m2i = m2i, sd2i = sd2i, n2i = n2i, data = nd))
cases <- c(cases, case("normand_md_reml", "MD", "RE-REML", FALSE, nds, summ(cont("MD"))),
           case("normand_smd_reml", "SMD", "RE-REML", FALSE, nds, summ(cont("SMD"))))

zc <- data.frame(study = c("Z1", "Z2", "Z3", "Z4", "Z5"), a = c(0, 3, 5, 0, 7), n1 = c(20, 25, 30, 15, 40),
                 c = c(2, 6, 4, 0, 12), n2 = c(20, 24, 31, 15, 38))
zes <- escalc(measure = "OR", ai = a, bi = n1 - a, ci = c, di = n2 - c, data = zc, add = 1/2, to = "only0", drop00 = TRUE)
cases <- c(cases, case("zero_cell_or_reml", "OR", "RE-REML", FALSE, studies2x2(zc$study, zc$a, zc$n1, zc$c, zc$n2),
                       summ(rma(yi, vi, data = zes, method = "REML"))))

eng <- sprintf("%s; metafor %s", R.version.string, as.character(packageVersion("metafor")))
txt <- c("{", sprintf('  "engine": %s,', q(eng)), '  "script": "tests/unit/fixtures/meta/make_goldens.R",',
         '  "cases": {', paste0("    ", paste(cases, collapse = ",\n    ")), "  }", "}")
con <- file("tests/unit/fixtures/meta/metafor-goldens.json", open = "wb")
writeLines(txt, con, sep = "\n")
close(con)
```

---
### Task 6: `grade.py` — certainty, absolute effects, summary of findings, E-GRADE (spec 6.5)

**Files:**
- Create: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/grade.py`, `plugins/orchestra/references/review/grade-wording.md`, `tests/unit/test_grade.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `crolib.resultlog` (Task 1), `crolib.drafts.draft_files`, `crolib.derived.ledger_by_id`, `crolib.projectcfg.output_lang`, `analysis_plan._paragraphs(text) -> iterator[(start_line, paragraph)]`, `results_ledger.LockedError`.
- Produces CLI `grade.py <project> run [--table N]` and `grade.py <project> check [--text FILE ...]`.
- Produces importable `grade.certainty(outcome: dict) -> str` (`"high" | "moderate" | "low" | "very low"`), `grade.validate(doc: dict, ledger: dict) -> list[str]`, `grade.absolute(kind: str, x: float, cr: float) -> float` (intervention risk), `grade.certainty_by_result(project) -> dict[str, str]` (pooled id, its `-005` and `-901…903` ids → certainty), `grade.check_wording(project, files) -> list[tuple[level, loc, msg]]` (E-GRADE warns, used by `audit.py` in Task 9), `grade.load_wording(lang) -> dict[str, list[str]]`; constants `GRADE_JSON = "05_analysis/meta/grade.json"`, `LOG_REL = "05_analysis/output/grade-log.txt"`, `DOC_REL = "05_analysis/output/grade-results.json"`, `SOF = "06_writing/review/sof.md"`.
- `grade.json` schema: `{"outcomes": [{"outcome", "result": "R-A<n>-001", "design": "rct" | "nrsi", "rob_tool", "downgrades": {"risk_of_bias", "inconsistency", "indirectness", "imprecision", "publication_bias": [0|-1|-2, "reason"]}, "upgrades": {"large_effect", "dose_response", "plausible_confounding": [0|1|2, "reason"]} (nrsi only), "comparator_risk": 0 < r < 1 (ratio and RD measures), "comparator_source": "…"}]}`. Start level high for RCTs and for NRSI assessed with ROBINS-I, low otherwise; certainty = start + downgrades (+ upgrades for NRSI), clamped to very low…high. Absolute effects (D17): `R-A<n>-901` comparator risk per 1000, `902` intervention risk per 1000 with CI, `903` difference per 1000 with CI, kind `absolute`, status of the pooled record; risk with intervention from RR `x·r`, OR `x·r / (1 − r + x·r)`, HR `1 − (1 − r)^x`, RD `r + x`; MD/SMD have no absolute row.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `tests/unit/test_grade.py`:

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import gate
import grade
from p5_helpers import review_project

LOG = "05_analysis/output/meta-log.txt"


def rec(rid, kind, value, ci, status="prespecified"):
    return {"id": rid, "analysis_id": rid.split("-")[1], "status": status, "label": f"pooled {kind}", "value": value,
            "ci": ci, "count": 3, "n": 300, "kind": kind, "source_file": LOG, "locator": "line 1", "raw": "x"}


def outcome(result="R-A1-001", design="rct", rob="RoB 2", down=(0, -1, 0, -1, 0), up=(0, 0, 0), cr=0.2):
    keys = ("risk_of_bias", "inconsistency", "indirectness", "imprecision", "publication_bias")
    o = {"outcome": "Mortality at 30 days", "result": result, "design": design, "rob_tool": rob,
         "downgrades": {k: [v, "fictional reason" if v else ""] for k, v in zip(keys, down)},
         "upgrades": {k: [v, "fictional reason" if v else ""] for k, v in zip(("large_effect", "dose_response",
                                                                             "plausible_confounding"), up)},
         "comparator_source": "median control-group risk of the included studies (fictional)"}
    if cr is not None:
        o["comparator_risk"] = cr
    return o


def cli(*argv):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        code = grade.main([str(a) for a in argv])
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


class TestGrade(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = review_project(self.t)
        (self.p / LOG).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (self.p / LOG).write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.led = [rec("R-A1-001", "rr", 0.75, [0.5, 1.1]), rec("R-A2-001", "or", 2.0, [1.5, 3.0]),
                    rec("R-A3-001", "md", -0.8, [-1.2, -0.4])]
        self.write_ledger()

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def write_ledger(self):
        (self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text(json.dumps({"results": self.led}), encoding="utf-8")

    def write_grade(self, *outcomes):
        path = self.p / grade.GRADE_JSON
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"outcomes": list(outcomes)}), encoding="utf-8")

    def ledger(self):
        return {r["id"]: r for r in json.loads((self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]}

    def test_certainty_levels(self):
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(down=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))), "high")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(down=(0, -1, 0, 0, 0))), "moderate")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome()), "low")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(down=(-2, -1, 0, -1, 0))), "very low")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(design="nrsi", rob="NOS", down=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))), "low")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(design="nrsi", rob="ROBINS-I", down=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))), "high")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty(outcome(design="nrsi", rob="NOS", down=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0), up=(1, 0, 0))), "moderate")

    def test_absolute_conversions(self):
        self.assertAlmostEqual(grade.absolute("rr", 0.75, 0.2), 0.15, places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(grade.absolute("or", 2.0, 0.2), 2.0 * 0.2 / (1 - 0.2 + 2.0 * 0.2), places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(grade.absolute("hr", 0.5, 0.2), 1 - 0.8 ** 0.5, places=12)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(grade.absolute("rd", -0.05, 0.2), 0.15, places=12)

    def test_run_writes_absolute_records_and_sof(self):
        (self.p / "06_writing" / "review" / "04-results.md").write_text("# Results\n\nTable 1. Included studies\n| a |\n|---|\n| b |\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.write_grade(outcome(), outcome(result="R-A2-001", down=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0)), outcome(result="R-A3-001", cr=None))
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out + err)
        led = self.ledger()
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-901"]["value"], 200, places=9)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-902"]["value"], 0.75 * 0.2 * 1000, places=9)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-902"]["ci"][0], 0.5 * 0.2 * 1000, places=9)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-903"]["value"], (0.75 - 1) * 0.2 * 1000, places=9)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A1-903"]["ci"][1], (1.1 - 1) * 0.2 * 1000, places=9)
        self.assertEqual({led[i]["kind"] for i in ("R-A1-901", "R-A1-902", "R-A1-903")}, {"absolute"})
        self.assertEqual(led["R-A1-902"]["status"], "prespecified")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(led["R-A2-902"]["value"], 1000 * 2.0 * 0.2 / (1 - 0.2 + 2.0 * 0.2), places=9)
        self.assertNotIn("R-A3-901", led)
        sof = (self.p / grade.SOF).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("Table 2. Summary of findings", sof)
        self.assertIn("{{R-A1-001.full}}", sof)
        self.assertIn("{{R-A1-903.full}}", sof)
        self.assertIn("⊕⊕◯◯ Low", sof)
        self.assertIn("⊕⊕⊕⊕ High", sof)
        self.assertIn("median control-group risk", sof)
        self.assertIn("inconsistency: down one level (fictional reason)", sof)
        self.assertFalse([ln for ln in sof.splitlines() if ln.startswith("- ") and re.search(r"\d", ln)])  # footnotes: words, not digits (E-NUM)
        self.assertIn("not-a-result", sof)
        self.assertIn("low certainty", out.lower())

    def test_vietnamese_sof(self):
        y = (self.p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("output: en", "output: vi")
        (self.p / "project.yaml").write_text(y, encoding="utf-8")
        self.write_grade(outcome())
        self.assertEqual(cli(self.p, "run")[0], 0)
        sof = (self.p / grade.SOF).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("Bảng 1. Tóm tắt kết quả", sof)
        self.assertIn("⊕⊕◯◯ Thấp", sof)

    def test_invalid_grade_json_exit_2(self):
        bad = outcome()
        bad["downgrades"]["inconsistency"] = [-1, ""]
        self.write_grade(bad)
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn("inconsistency: a downgrade needs a reason", err)
        self.write_grade(outcome(up=(1, 0, 0)))
        self.assertIn("upgrades are for non-randomised", cli(self.p, "run")[2])
        self.write_grade(outcome(result="R-A9-001"))
        self.assertIn("R-A9-001 is not in the results ledger", cli(self.p, "run")[2])
        self.write_grade(outcome(cr=1.5))
        self.assertIn("comparator_risk", cli(self.p, "run")[2])

    def test_g3_lock_refuses(self):
        self.write_grade(outcome())
        gate.open_gate(self.p, "G3", by="A", files=["ledgers/results-ledger.json"])
        code, out, err = cli(self.p, "run")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("G3 is locked", err)

    def test_wording_check(self):
        self.write_grade(outcome(), outcome(result="R-A2-001", down=(-2, -1, 0, -1, 0)))
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty_by_result(self.p)["R-A1-001"], "low")
        self.assertEqual(grade.certainty_by_result(self.p)["R-A1-903"], "low")
        d = self.p / "06_writing" / "review"
        (d / "04-results.md").write_text(
            "# Results\n\nDrug X reduces mortality ({{R-A1-001.full}}).\n\n"
            "Drug X may reduce mortality ({{R-A1-001.full}}).\n\n"
            "Drug Y may increase harm ({{R-A2-001.full}}).\n\n"
            "We are very uncertain whether drug Y increases harm ({{R-A2-001.full}}).\n\n"
            "| Outcome | Effect |\n|---|---|\n| Mortality | {{R-A1-001.full}} |\n", encoding="utf-8")
        found = grade.check_wording(self.p, [d / "04-results.md"])
        locs = sorted(loc for _, loc, _ in found)
        self.assertEqual(locs, ["06_writing/review/04-results.md:3", "06_writing/review/04-results.md:7"])
        self.assertTrue(all(s == "warn" for s, _, _ in found))
        code, out, _ = cli(self.p, "check", "--text", d / "04-results.md")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertIn("E-GRADE warn 06_writing/review/04-results.md:3", out)

    def test_wording_lists(self):
        for lang in ("vi", "en"):
            w = grade.load_wording(lang)
            self.assertTrue(w["low"] and w["very low"])
        self.assertIn("có thể", grade.load_wording("vi")["low"])
        self.assertIn("chưa chắc chắn", grade.load_wording("vi")["very low"])
        self.assertIn("may", grade.load_wording("en")["low"])
        self.assertIn("very uncertain", grade.load_wording("en")["very low"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`references/review/grade-wording.md`:

```markdown
# GRADE wording for claims

`grade.py check` (E-GRADE, warn) reads the lists below. A paragraph that cites a pooled result (or its absolute
effects) whose certainty is **low** must contain a word from the low or the very-low list; **very low** needs the
very-low list. High and moderate certainty need no hedge (moderate: prefer "probably"). Tables are not checked:
the summary-of-findings table states the certainty itself. Matching ignores case.

Suggested sentence frames (adapted to the outcome): high "X reduces Y"; moderate "X probably reduces Y"; low
"X may reduce Y"; very low "We are very uncertain whether X reduces Y".

## en

### low
- may
- might
- could
- possibly
- low certainty
- low-certainty

### very low
- very uncertain
- very low certainty
- very low-certainty
- uncertain whether

## vi

### low
- có thể
- độ tin cậy thấp
- mức chắc chắn thấp
- chắc chắn thấp

### very low
- rất không chắc chắn
- chưa chắc chắn
- độ tin cậy rất thấp
- mức chắc chắn rất thấp
```

`grade.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GRADE certainty, absolute effects and the summary of findings for a systematic review; E-GRADE wording check.

  python grade.py <project> run [--table N]
  python grade.py <project> check [--text FILE ...]

run reads 05_analysis/meta/grade.json (per outcome: the pooled result id, design rct|nrsi, the RoB tool,
downgrades risk_of_bias/inconsistency/indirectness/imprecision/publication_bias of 0/-1/-2 with a reason, upgrades
for NRSI only, the assumed comparator risk with its source), computes certainty (start high for RCTs and for NRSI
assessed with ROBINS-I, low otherwise), writes absolute effects per 1000 as R-A<n>-901 (comparator), -902
(intervention, CI), -903 (difference, CI) with kind "absolute" through 05_analysis/output/grade-log.txt into the
results ledger, and writes 06_writing/review/sof.md with placeholders (the table number follows the tables of the
sections before it unless --table is given). check warns (E-GRADE) on a paragraph that cites a low or very low
certainty result without hedged wording from references/review/grade-wording.md (language from project.yaml).
Exit codes: 0 = done / no block (warnings allowed); 1 = the ledger is locked by G3 or a label change is refused;
2 = usage error, invalid grade.json, unknown result id, I/O error.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import derived, drafts, projectcfg, resultlog  # noqa: E402
import analysis_plan  # noqa: E402
import results_ledger  # noqa: E402

GRADE_JSON = "05_analysis/meta/grade.json"
LOG_REL = "05_analysis/output/grade-log.txt"
DOC_REL = "05_analysis/output/grade-results.json"
SOF = "06_writing/review/sof.md"
WORDING = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "references" / "review" / "grade-wording.md"
DOWN = ("risk_of_bias", "inconsistency", "indirectness", "imprecision", "publication_bias")
UP = ("large_effect", "dose_response", "plausible_confounding")
LEVELS = {4: "high", 3: "moderate", 2: "low", 1: "very low"}
SYMBOL = {"high": "⊕⊕⊕⊕", "moderate": "⊕⊕⊕◯", "low": "⊕⊕◯◯", "very low": "⊕◯◯◯"}
NAME = {"en": {"high": "High", "moderate": "Moderate", "low": "Low", "very low": "Very low"},
        "vi": {"high": "Cao", "moderate": "Trung bình", "low": "Thấp", "very low": "Rất thấp"}}
ABSOLUTE_KINDS = ("rr", "or", "hr", "rd")
STEP = {"en": {"down": "down", "up": "up", 1: "one level", 2: "two levels"},  # words, not digits: sof.md is E-NUM checked
        "vi": {"down": "hạ", "up": "nâng", 1: "một bậc", 2: "hai bậc"}}
PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\{\{(R-A(\d+)-(\d{3}))(?:\.\w+)?\}\}")
CAPTION = re.compile(r"^(?:\*\*)?(?:Table|Bảng)\s+(\d+)\.", re.M)
TEXT = {
    "en": {"caption": "Table {n}. Summary of findings",
           "head": "| Outcome | Studies (participants) | Relative effect (95% CI) | Risk with comparator (per 1000 "
                   "<!-- not-a-result: GRADE denominator -->) | Risk with intervention (95% CI) | Difference (95% CI) "
                   "| Certainty |",
           "na": "not applicable (continuous outcome)",
           "foot": "Certainty (GRADE): high, moderate, low, very low. Assumed comparator risk: {src}. Reasons: {why}."},
    "vi": {"caption": "Bảng {n}. Tóm tắt kết quả (summary of findings)",
           "head": "| Kết cục | Số nghiên cứu (người tham gia) | Hiệu quả tương đối (KTC 95%) | Nguy cơ ở nhóm so sánh "
                   "(trên 1000 <!-- not-a-result: mẫu số GRADE -->) | Nguy cơ ở nhóm can thiệp (KTC 95%) | "
                   "Chênh lệch (KTC 95%) | Mức chắc chắn |",
           "na": "không áp dụng (biến liên tục)",
           "foot": "Mức chắc chắn (GRADE): cao, trung bình, thấp, rất thấp. Nguy cơ giả định ở nhóm so sánh: {src}. "
                   "Lý do: {why}."},
}


class Invalid(Exception):
    """grade.json cannot be used (exit 2)."""


def _pair(o: dict, group: str, key: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    v = (o.get(group) or {}).get(key, [0, ""])
    if not (isinstance(v, list) and len(v) == 2 and isinstance(v[0], int) and not isinstance(v[0], bool)):
        raise Invalid(f"{o.get('outcome')}: {group}.{key} must be [integer, reason]")
    return v[0], str(v[1] or "").strip()


def start_level(o: dict) -> int:
    if o.get("design") == "rct" or (o.get("design") == "nrsi" and o.get("rob_tool") == "ROBINS-I"):
        return 4
    return 2


def certainty(o: dict) -> str:
    total = start_level(o) + sum(_pair(o, "downgrades", k)[0] for k in DOWN)
    if o.get("design") == "nrsi":
        total += sum(_pair(o, "upgrades", k)[0] for k in UP)
    return LEVELS[max(1, min(4, total))]


def validate(doc: dict, ledger: dict) -> list[str]:
    probs = []
    outs = doc.get("outcomes") if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(outs, list) or not outs:
        return ["grade.json needs a non-empty 'outcomes' list"]
    for o in outs:
        name = str(o.get("outcome") or "?")
        rid = o.get("result")
        if not (isinstance(rid, str) and re.fullmatch(r"R-A\d+-001", rid)):
            probs.append(f"{name}: result must be a pooled result id R-A<n>-001")
            continue
        if rid not in ledger:
            probs.append(f"{name}: {rid} is not in the results ledger (run meta.py run first)")
            continue
        if o.get("design") not in ("rct", "nrsi"):
            probs.append(f"{name}: design must be rct or nrsi")
        try:
            for k in DOWN:
                v, why = _pair(o, "downgrades", k)
                if v not in (0, -1, -2):
                    probs.append(f"{name}: {k}: a downgrade is 0, -1 or -2")
                elif v and not why:
                    probs.append(f"{name}: {k}: a downgrade needs a reason")
            for k in UP:
                v, why = _pair(o, "upgrades", k)
                if v and o.get("design") != "nrsi":
                    probs.append(f"{name}: {k}: upgrades are for non-randomised studies only")
                elif v not in (0, 1, 2):
                    probs.append(f"{name}: {k}: an upgrade is 0, 1 or 2")
                elif v and not why:
                    probs.append(f"{name}: {k}: an upgrade needs a reason")
        except Invalid as e:
            probs.append(str(e))
        if ledger[rid].get("kind") in ABSOLUTE_KINDS:
            cr = o.get("comparator_risk")
            if not (isinstance(cr, (int, float)) and not isinstance(cr, bool) and 0 < cr < 1):
                probs.append(f"{name}: comparator_risk must be a proportion between 0 and 1")
            if not str(o.get("comparator_source") or "").strip():
                probs.append(f"{name}: comparator_source must say where the assumed risk comes from")
    return probs


def absolute(kind: str, x: float, cr: float) -> float:
    if kind == "rr":
        return min(1.0, x * cr)
    if kind == "or":
        return x * cr / (1 - cr + x * cr)
    if kind == "hr":
        return 1 - (1 - cr) ** x
    if kind == "rd":
        return min(1.0, max(0.0, cr + x))
    raise ValueError(f"no absolute effect for kind {kind!r}")


def _next_table(project: Path) -> int:
    """1 + the highest "Table n." / "Bảng n." caption in the sections placed before sof.md (D21 order)."""
    n = 0
    base = Path(project) / "06_writing" / "review"
    for name in ("02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "04-results.md"):
        f = base / name
        if f.is_file():
            n = max([n] + [int(m.group(1)) for m in CAPTION.finditer(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))])
    return n + 1


def run(project: Path, table: int | None = None) -> tuple[int, list[str], list[str]]:
    doc = json.loads((project / GRADE_JSON).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    ledger = derived.ledger_by_id(project)
    probs = validate(doc, ledger)
    if probs:
        raise Invalid("\n".join(probs))
    lang = "vi" if projectcfg.output_lang(project) == "vi" else "en"
    t = TEXT[lang]
    log = resultlog.ResultLog(project, GRADE_JSON, LOG_REL, "grade.py")
    rows, notes, summary = [], [], []
    for o in doc["outcomes"]:
        rid = o["result"]
        rec, aid = ledger[rid], rid.split("-")[1]
        level = certainty(o)
        reasons = [f"{k.replace('_', ' ')}: {STEP[lang]['down']} {STEP[lang][abs(v)]} ({why})"
                   for k in DOWN for v, why in [_pair(o, "downgrades", k)] if v]
        reasons += [f"{k.replace('_', ' ')}: {STEP[lang]['up']} {STEP[lang][v]} ({why})"
                    for k in UP for v, why in [_pair(o, "upgrades", k)] if v]
        summary.append(f"{o['outcome']}: {level} certainty ({SYMBOL[level]})")
        cells = [o["outcome"], f"{{{{{rid}.count}}}} ({{{{{rid}.n}}}})", f"{{{{{rid}.full}}}}"]
        kind = rec.get("kind")
        if kind in ABSOLUTE_KINDS and rec.get("ci"):
            cr = float(o["comparator_risk"])
            lo, hi = rec["ci"]
            risk = [absolute(kind, x, cr) * 1000 for x in (rec["value"], lo, hi)]
            st = rec.get("status", "prespecified")
            log.add(aid, st, f"{o['outcome']}: assumed comparator risk per 1000", cr * 1000, "absolute", number=901)
            log.add(aid, st, f"{o['outcome']}: risk with intervention per 1000", risk[0], "absolute",
                    ci=[min(risk[1:]), max(risk[1:])], number=902)
            diff = [r - cr * 1000 for r in risk]
            log.add(aid, st, f"{o['outcome']}: difference per 1000", diff[0], "absolute",
                    ci=[min(diff[1:]), max(diff[1:])], number=903)
            cells += [f"{{{{R-{aid}-901.value}}}}", f"{{{{R-{aid}-902.full}}}}", f"{{{{R-{aid}-903.full}}}}"]
        else:
            cells += [t["na"], t["na"], t["na"]]
        cells.append(f"{SYMBOL[level]} {NAME[lang][level]}")
        rows.append("| " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
        notes.append(t["foot"].format(src=o.get("comparator_source") or "—", why="; ".join(reasons) or "—"))
    n = 0
    if log.results:
        path = log.save(DOC_REL)
        n, bad = resultlog.import_merged(project, path)
        if bad:
            raise ValueError("; ".join(bad))
    sof = project / SOF
    sof.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    body = [t["caption"].format(n=table or _next_table(project)), t["head"],
            "|" + "---|" * (t["head"].count("|") - 1)] + rows + [""] + [f"- {x}" for x in notes]
    with open(sof, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write("\n".join(body) + "\n")
    return n, summary, [SOF]


def load_wording(lang: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    out: dict[str, list[str]] = {"low": [], "very low": []}
    section, sub = None, None
    for line in WORDING.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        s = line.strip()
        if s.startswith("## "):
            section, sub = s[3:].strip(), None
        elif s.startswith("### "):
            sub = s[4:].strip()
        elif s.startswith("- ") and section == lang and sub in out:
            out[sub].append(s[2:].strip().casefold())
    return out


def certainty_by_result(project: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    path = Path(project) / GRADE_JSON
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}
    out = {}
    for o in json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")).get("outcomes", []):
        rid = o.get("result")
        if not isinstance(rid, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"R-A\d+-001", rid):
            continue
        try:
            level = certainty(o)
        except Invalid:
            continue
        aid = rid.split("-")[1]
        for suffix in ("001", "005", "901", "902", "903"):
            out[f"R-{aid}-{suffix}"] = level
    return out


def _hedged(text: str, words: list[str], lang: str) -> bool:
    low = text.casefold()
    if lang == "en":
        return any(re.search(r"(?<![\w-])" + re.escape(w) + r"(?![\w-])", low) for w in words)
    return any(w in low for w in words)


def check_wording(project: Path, files: list) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    project = Path(project)
    levels = certainty_by_result(project)
    if not levels:
        return []
    lang = "vi" if projectcfg.output_lang(project) == "vi" else "en"
    words = load_wording(lang)
    found = []
    for f in files:
        f = Path(f)
        rel = f.resolve().relative_to(project.resolve()).as_posix() if f.is_absolute() else f.as_posix()
        for start, para in analysis_plan._paragraphs(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")):
            if para.lstrip().startswith("|"):
                continue
            cited = [m.group(1) for m in PLACEHOLDER.finditer(para) if levels.get(m.group(1)) in ("low", "very low")]
            if not cited:
                continue
            worst = "very low" if any(levels[c] == "very low" for c in cited) else "low"
            allowed = words["very low"] if worst == "very low" else words["low"] + words["very low"]
            if not _hedged(para, allowed, lang):
                found.append(("warn", f"{rel}:{start}", f"{cited[0]} has {worst} certainty (GRADE); hedge the claim "
                                                        f"({', '.join(repr(w) for w in allowed[:3])}, …)"))
    return found


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["run", "check"])
    ap.add_argument("--table", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--text", nargs="*", default=None)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "project.yaml").is_file():
        print(f"grade: {a.project} is not a project folder (no project.yaml)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        if a.cmd == "run":
            n, summary, written = run(project, a.table)
            for line in summary:
                print(line)
            print(f"grade: {n} absolute effects in the results ledger; wrote {', '.join(written)}")
            return 0
        files = [Path(x) for x in a.text] if a.text is not None else [
            p for p in drafts.draft_files(project, "review") if drafts.results_bearing(p)]
        found = check_wording(project, files)
        for sev, loc, msg in found:
            print(f"E-GRADE {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
        print(f"grade: 0 block, {len(found)} warn")
        return 0
    except results_ledger.LockedError as e:
        print(f"grade: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except results_ledger.RelabelError as e:
        print(f"{e}\ngrade: labels changed; ledger not written", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except Invalid as e:
        print(f"grade: grade.json is not usable:\n{e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as e:
        print(f"grade: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`check` without `--text` uses `drafts.draft_files(project, "review")`, which exists only after Task 9; until then call it with `--text` (the tests do). Do not import anything else from Task 9.

@unittest.skipUnless(HAVE_MPL, "matplotlib is not installed")
class TestP5Figures(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.p = Path(self.tmp.name)
        (self.p / "ledgers").mkdir()
        (self.p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text(json.dumps(P5_LEDGER), encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "project.yaml").write_text("language:\n  output: vi\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr").mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr" / "rob.csv").write_text(ROB_CSV, encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "07_present" / "figures").mkdir(parents=True)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def run_spec(self, spec, *extra):
        (self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / "figures.json").write_text(json.dumps(spec), encoding="utf-8")
        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SCRIPT), str(self.p), "--dpi", "80", *extra], capture_output=True,
                              text=True, encoding="utf-8")

    def data(self, fid):
        return json.loads((self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / f"{fid}.data.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_all_p5_types_render(self):
        r = self.run_spec(P5_SPEC)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        for fid in ("F5", "F6", "F7"):
            self.assertTrue((self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / f"{fid}.png").read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG"))

    def test_forest_pooled_diamond_and_prediction(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_spec(P5_SPEC, "--only", "F5").returncode, 0)
        d = self.data("F5")
        self.assertEqual(d["chart"]["pooled"], {"label": "Ước lượng gộp", "est": 0.78, "lo": 0.55, "hi": 1.1})
        self.assertEqual(d["chart"]["prediction"], [0.3, 2.0])
        last = d["values"][-1]
        self.assertEqual(last["ref"], "R-A1-001")
        self.assertEqual(last["text"], "0,78 (0,55–1,10); KDB 95% 0,30–2,00")
        bad = {"figures": [dict(P5_SPEC["figures"][0], pooled=None)]}
        r = self.run_spec(bad)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn("'prediction' needs 'pooled'", r.stderr)

    def test_prisma_boxes_from_ledger(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_spec(P5_SPEC, "--only", "F6").returncode, 0)
        ch = self.data("F6")["chart"]
        texts = [n["text"] for n in ch["nodes"]]
        self.assertIn("Bản ghi từ cơ sở dữ liệu\nvà sổ đăng ký (n = 0)\nn = 6", texts)
        self.assertIn("Bản ghi được sàng lọc\nn = 4", texts)
        self.assertIn("Báo cáo bị loại\nwrong population (n = 1)\nn = 1", texts)
        self.assertIn("Nghiên cứu được đưa vào\nBáo cáo của các nghiên cứu (n = 1)\nn = 1", texts)
        self.assertEqual([s["label"] for s in ch["stages"]], ["Nhận diện", "Sàng lọc", "Đưa vào"])
        refs = {v["ref"] for v in self.data("F6")["values"]}
        self.assertTrue({"R-A0-001.value", "R-A0-051.value", "R-A0-002.value"} <= refs)
        bad = {"figures": [{k: v for k, v in P5_SPEC["figures"][1].items() if k != "assessed"}]}
        r = self.run_spec(bad)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn("assessed", r.stderr)

    def test_rob_traffic_light(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_spec(P5_SPEC, "--only", "F7").returncode, 0)
        d = self.data("F7")
        self.assertEqual(d["data_file"], "04_data/sr/rob.csv")
        self.assertEqual(len(d["data_sha256"]), 64)
        ch = d["chart"]
        self.assertEqual((ch["kind"], ch["tool"]), ("rob", "RoB 2"))
        self.assertEqual(ch["studies"], ["S01", "S02"])
        self.assertEqual(ch["domains"], ["D1", "D2", "D3", "D4", "D5", "overall"])
        self.assertEqual(ch["cells"][1][0], "high")
        svg = (self.p / "07_present" / "figures" / "F7.svg").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for sym in ("+", "−", "×"):
            self.assertIn(sym, svg)                       # symbols for greyscale printing
        (self.p / "04_data" / "sr" / "rob.csv").write_text(ROB_CSV.replace(",high,", ",awful,"), encoding="utf-8")
        r = self.run_spec(P5_SPEC, "--only", "F7")
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn("awful", r.stderr)
        r = self.run_spec({"figures": [dict(P5_SPEC["figures"][2], outcome="pain")]})
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn("no rows for outcome 'pain'", r.stderr)
