Replace `_draw_forest` with:

```python
def _draw_forest(ctx: Ctx, fig: dict, rows: list[dict], pooled: dict | None = None):
    from matplotlib.patches import Polygon
    from matplotlib.transforms import blended_transform_factory
    every = rows + ([pooled] if pooled else [])
    n, em, s = len(every), ctx.em, ctx.s
    row_h = 2.6 * em
    hdr = _lang_text(ctx.lang, "Ước lượng (KTC 95%)", "Estimate (95% CI)")
    left = 0.8 * em + max(ctx.width(r["label"]) for r in every) + 0.5 * em
    right = 1.0 * em + max([ctx.width(hdr, bold=True)] + [ctx.width(r["text"]) for r in every]) + 0.8 * em
    width = max(ctx.width_cm, left + right + MIN_AXES_EM * em)
    axes_w = width - left - right
    fr = Frame(ctx, fig, fig.get("source"), width)
    xlab, xblock = _xlabel_block(ctx, fig.get("x_label", ""), axes_w)
    hdr_h = 1.6 * em
    axes_h = n * row_h
    height = fr.top + hdr_h + axes_h + xblock + fr.bottom0
    f = ctx.figure(height, width)
    ax = f.add_axes([left / width, (fr.bottom0 + xblock) / height, axes_w / width, axes_h / height])
    null = fig.get("null", 1)
    ys = list(range(n, 0, -1))
    col = ctx.color("forest", 0, label=False)
    for y, r in zip(ys, rows):
        ax.plot([r["lo"], r["hi"]], [y, y], color=col, lw=1.1 * s, solid_capstyle="butt")
        ax.plot([r["est"]], [y], marker="s", ms=5.5 * s, color=col, mfc=col if r["filled"] else "white", mew=1.1 * s)
    lows, highs = [r["lo"] for r in every] + [null], [r["hi"] for r in every] + [null]
    if pooled:
        if pooled.get("pi"):
            ax.plot(list(pooled["pi"]), [1, 1], color=GREY, lw=2.0 * s, solid_capstyle="butt", zorder=1)
            lows.append(pooled["pi"][0])
            highs.append(pooled["pi"][1])
        h = 0.3
        ax.add_patch(Polygon([(pooled["lo"], 1), (pooled["est"], 1 + h), (pooled["hi"], 1), (pooled["est"], 1 - h)],
                             closed=True, facecolor=col, edgecolor=col, zorder=2))
        ax.axhline(1.5, color=GREY, lw=0.6 * s)
    ax.axvline(null, color=GREY, lw=0.8 * s, ls="--")
    if fig.get("log_scale"):
        ax.set_xscale("log")
        lo, hi = min(lows), max(highs)
        ticks = [t for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 50) if lo / 1.6 <= t <= hi * 1.6]
        if ticks:
            ax.set_xticks(ticks)
        ax.set_xticklabels([ctx.fmt_axis(t) for t in ticks])
        ax.minorticks_off()
    elif pooled:
        pad = 0.05 * (max(highs) - min(lows) or 1)
        ax.set_xlim(min(lows) - pad, max(highs) + pad)
    ax.set_yticks(ys)
    ax.set_yticklabels([_esc(r["label"]) for r in every])
    ax.set_ylim(0.5, n + 0.5)
    ax.set_xlabel(_esc(xlab), fontsize=ctx.size, labelpad=0.4 * ctx.size)
    _style_axes(ctx, ax)
    ax.spines["left"].set_visible(False)
    ax.tick_params(axis="y", length=0, pad=0.5 * ctx.size)
    tr = blended_transform_factory(f.transFigure, ax.transData)
    x_text = (left + axes_w + 1.0 * em) / width
    ax.text(x_text, n + 0.5, hdr, transform=tr, ha="left", va="bottom", fontsize=ctx.size, fontweight="bold",
            clip_on=False)
    for y, r in zip(ys, every):
        ax.text(x_text, y, _esc(r["text"]), transform=tr, ha="left", va="center", fontsize=ctx.size, clip_on=False,
                fontweight="bold" if r is pooled else "normal")
    fr.finish(f, height)
    return f
```

(The `set_xlim` on a linear axis runs only when a pooled row exists, so every P3 forest is drawn exactly as before.)

Replace `build_forest` with:

```python
def build_forest(ctx: Ctx, fig: dict, by_id: dict, lang: str, **_):
    fid = fig["id"]
    rows = _rows(fig, by_id, lang, need_ci=True)
    pooled = None
    if fig.get("pooled"):
        rec = _record(fid, by_id, fig["pooled"], need_ci=True)
        pooled = {"label": fig.get("pooled_label") or _lang_text(lang, "Ước lượng gộp", "Pooled estimate"),
                  "ref": fig["pooled"], "est": _num(rec["value"]), "lo": _num(rec["ci"][0]), "hi": _num(rec["ci"][1]),
                  "text": _est_text(rec, lang), "filled": True, "pi": None}
        if fig.get("prediction"):
            prec = _record(fid, by_id, fig["prediction"], need_ci=True)
            pooled["pi"] = (_num(prec["ci"][0]), _num(prec["ci"][1]))
            pooled["text"] += _lang_text(lang, "; KDB 95% ", "; 95% PI ") + numfmt.render(prec, "ci", lang)
    elif fig.get("prediction"):
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: 'prediction' needs 'pooled'")
    f = _draw_forest(ctx, fig, rows, pooled)
    log = bool(fig.get("log_scale"))
    null = fig.get("null", 1)
    every = rows + ([pooled] if pooled else [])
    data = {"values": [{k: r[k] for k in ("label", "ref", "est", "lo", "hi", "text")} for r in every],
            "chart": {"kind": "forest", "log": log, "null": null, "x_label": fig.get("x_label", ""),
                      "rows": [{k: r[k] for k in ("label", "est", "lo", "hi")} for r in rows]}}
    if pooled:
        data["chart"]["pooled"] = {k: pooled[k] for k in ("label", "est", "lo", "hi")}
        data["chart"]["prediction"] = list(pooled["pi"]) if pooled["pi"] else None
    return f, fig.get("source", ""), data
```

Add after `build_flow`:

```python
def _prisma_ref(fid: str, item: dict, key: str) -> str:
    v = item.get(key)
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not derived.RID.search(v):
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: prisma '{key}' must be a ledger id such as R-A0-001")
    return v if "." in v else f"{v}.value"


def build_prisma(ctx: Ctx, fig: dict, by_id: dict, lang: str, **_):
    fid = fig["id"]
    missing = [k for k in PRISMA_REQUIRED if k not in fig]
    if missing:
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: prisma needs ledger ids for {', '.join(missing)}")
    t = lambda vi, en: _lang_text(lang, vi, en)  # noqa: E731
    extra: list[dict] = []

    def shown(item: dict, key: str, label: str) -> str:
        ref = _prisma_ref(fid, item, key)
        est, txt = _n_of(fid, by_id, ref, lang)
        extra.append({"label": label, "ref": ref, "est": est, "text": f"n = {txt}"})
        return txt

    ident = t("Bản ghi từ cơ sở dữ liệu", "Records identified from databases")
    if "registers" in fig:
        ident += "\n" + t("và sổ đăng ký", "and registers") + f" (n = {shown(fig, 'registers', 'registers')})"
    reasons = fig.get("reasons") or []
    if not isinstance(reasons, list):
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: 'reasons' must be a list of {{text, n}}")
    excl = t("Báo cáo bị loại", "Reports excluded")
    for r in reasons:
        if not isinstance(r, dict) or not isinstance(r.get("text"), str):
            raise FigureError(f"{fid}: every reason needs 'text' and 'n'")
        excl += f"\n{r['text']} (n = {shown(r, 'n', r['text'])})"
    included = t("Nghiên cứu được đưa vào", "Studies included in review")
    if "reports" in fig:
        included += "\n" + t("Báo cáo của các nghiên cứu", "Reports of included studies") + \
            f" (n = {shown(fig, 'reports', 'reports')})"
    ref = lambda k: _prisma_ref(fid, fig, k)  # noqa: E731
    flow = {"id": fid, "title": fig.get("title"), "source": fig.get("source"),
            "boxes": [{"text": ident, "n": ref("identified")},
                      {"text": t("Bản ghi được sàng lọc", "Records screened"), "n": ref("screened")},
                      {"text": t("Báo cáo cần tìm toàn văn", "Reports sought for retrieval"), "n": ref("sought")},
                      {"text": t("Báo cáo được đánh giá tính phù hợp", "Reports assessed for eligibility"), "n": ref("assessed")},
                      {"text": included, "n": ref("studies")}],
            "exclusions": [{"text": t("Bản ghi trùng lặp bị loại", "Duplicate records removed"), "n": ref("duplicates"), "after": 1},
                           {"text": t("Bản ghi bị loại", "Records excluded"), "n": ref("excluded"), "after": 2},
                           {"text": t("Báo cáo không tìm được", "Reports not retrieved"), "n": ref("not_retrieved"), "after": 3},
                           {"text": excl, "n": ref("reports_excluded"), "after": 4}]}
    f, source, data = build_flow(ctx, flow, by_id, lang)
    ax = f.axes[0]
    nodes = {n["id"]: n for n in data["chart"]["nodes"]}
    stages = [(t("Nhận diện", "Identification"), "n1", "n1"), (t("Sàng lọc", "Screening"), "n2", "n4"),
              (t("Đưa vào", "Included"), "n5", "n5")]
    for label, a, b in stages:
        mid = (nodes[a]["y"] + nodes[b]["y"] + nodes[b]["h"]) / 2
        ax.text(0.035, mid, _esc(label), rotation=90, ha="center", va="center", fontsize=ctx.size, fontweight="bold")
    data["values"] += extra
    data["chart"]["stages"] = [{"label": lab, "from": a, "to": b} for lab, a, b in stages]
    return f, source, data


def _read_rob(project: Path, fig: dict) -> tuple[list, list, dict, str]:
    fid = fig["id"]
    for k in ("outcome", "tool"):
        if not isinstance(fig.get(k), str) or not fig[k].strip():
            raise FigureError(f"{fid}: rob needs '{k}'")
    try:
        raw = (project / ROB_REL).read_bytes()
    except OSError as e:
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: cannot read {ROB_REL}: {e}") from e
    cells: dict[tuple, str] = {}
    for r in csv.DictReader(raw.decode("utf-8-sig").splitlines()):
        if (r.get("outcome") or "").strip() != fig["outcome"] or (r.get("tool") or "").strip() != fig["tool"]:
            continue
        j = (r.get("judgement") or "").strip().casefold()
        if j not in ROB_STYLE:
            raise FigureError(f"{fid}: judgement {j!r} is not one of {', '.join(ROB_STYLE)}")
        cells[((r.get("study_id") or "").strip(), (r.get("domain") or "").strip())] = j
    if not cells:
        raise FigureError(f"{fid}: no rows for outcome {fig['outcome']!r} and tool {fig['tool']!r} in {ROB_REL}")
    studies = sorted({st for st, _ in cells})
    doms = sorted({d for _, d in cells if d != "overall"}, key=lambda d: (int(d[1:]) if d[1:].isdigit() else 99, d))
    if any(d == "overall" for _, d in cells):
        doms.append("overall")
    return studies, doms, cells, hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def _draw_rob(ctx: Ctx, fig: dict, studies: list, domains: list, cells: dict):
    from matplotlib.patches import Circle
    em, s = ctx.em, ctx.s
    heads = [_lang_text(ctx.lang, "Tổng thể", "Overall") if d == "overall" else d for d in domains]
    cell = 2.2 * em
    left = 0.8 * em + max(ctx.width(x) for x in studies) + 0.6 * em
    width = max(ctx.width_cm, left + cell * len(domains) + 1.0 * em)
    fr = Frame(ctx, fig, fig.get("source"), width)
    used = [j for j in ROB_STYLE if j in set(cells.values())]
    legend = ctx.wrap("   ".join(f"{ROB_STYLE[j][1]} {ROB_NAME[ctx.lang if ctx.lang in ROB_NAME else 'en'][j]}" for j in used),
                      width - 2 * fr.margin)
    legend_h = ctx.lines_h(legend) + 0.8 * em
    hdr_h, grid_h = 1.6 * em, len(studies) * cell
    height = fr.top + hdr_h + grid_h + legend_h + fr.bottom0
    f = ctx.figure(height, width)
    ax = f.add_axes([left / width, (fr.bottom0 + legend_h) / height, cell * len(domains) / width, grid_h / height])
    ax.set_xlim(0, len(domains))
    ax.set_ylim(len(studies), 0)
    ax.axis("off")
    for i, st in enumerate(studies):
        ax.text(-0.25, i + 0.5, _esc(st), ha="right", va="center", fontsize=ctx.size, clip_on=False)
        for j, d in enumerate(domains):
            colour, sym = ROB_STYLE.get(cells.get((st, d)), ("white", ""))
            ax.add_patch(Circle((j + 0.5, i + 0.5), 0.38, facecolor=colour, edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.6 * s))
            ax.text(j + 0.5, i + 0.5, sym, ha="center", va="center", fontsize=ctx.size, fontweight="bold")
    for j, h in enumerate(heads):
        ax.text(j + 0.5, -0.2, _esc(h), ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=ctx.size, fontweight="bold", clip_on=False)
    f.text(fr.margin / width, (fr.bottom0 + legend_h - 0.3 * em) / height, _esc(legend), ha="left", va="top",
           fontsize=ctx.size, linespacing=1.2)
    fr.finish(f, height)
    return f


def build_rob(ctx: Ctx, fig: dict, project: Path, lang: str, **_):
    studies, domains, cells, sha = _read_rob(Path(project), fig)
    f = _draw_rob(ctx, fig, studies, domains, cells)
    values = [{"label": f"{st} {d}", "ref": ROB_REL, "text": cells[(st, d)]}
              for st in studies for d in domains if (st, d) in cells]
    return f, fig.get("source", ""), {"values": values, "data_file": ROB_REL, "data_sha256": sha,
                                       "chart": {"kind": "rob", "tool": fig["tool"], "studies": studies,
                                                 "domains": domains,
                                                 "cells": [[cells.get((st, d), "") for d in domains] for st in studies]}}
```

`BUILDERS = {"forest": build_forest, "rates": build_rates, "km": build_km, "flow": build_flow, "prisma": build_prisma, "rob": build_rob}`. In `render_figure` use `by_id = None if fig["type"] in ("km", "rob") else derived.ledger_by_id(project)`; in `_run` use `if fig["type"] not in ("km", "rob") and by_id is None:`. `build_rob` receives `project` through the existing `**` call (`BUILDERS[...](ctx, fig, by_id=by_id, lang=lang, project=project)`).

`references/presenting/figures-spec.md`: in the types list add after `flow`:

```markdown
- `forest` (P5): optional `pooled` (a whole result id with `ci`, drawn as a diamond in its own bottom row, label
  `pooled_label` or "Ước lượng gộp"/"Pooled estimate") and `prediction` (a result id whose `ci` is the 95% prediction
  interval, drawn as a grey bar through the diamond; needs `pooled`). `meta.py forest-spec` writes these entries.
- `prisma` (P5): the PRISMA 2020 flow. Required ledger ids `identified`, `duplicates`, `screened`, `excluded`, `sought`,
  `not_retrieved`, `assessed`, `reports_excluded`, `studies`; optional `registers`, `reports` and `reasons`
  (`[{"text": "wrong population", "n": "R-A0-051"}]`). Ids written without a part mean `.value`. Stage labels
  Identification / Screening / Included are drawn on the left. Every number comes from `screening.py counts`.
- `rob` (P5): traffic-light summary from `04_data/sr/rob.csv` for one `outcome` and one `tool` (domains D1… plus
  overall). Judgements have colour and a symbol for greyscale printing: + low, − some concerns / moderate,
  × high / serious, ! critical, ? no information.
```

and in the `data.json` section: "`forest` with `pooled`: `chart.pooled` `{label, est, lo, hi}` and `chart.prediction` `[lo, hi]` or null; the pooled row is the last item of `values`. `prisma`: the `flow` chart plus `stages`. `rob`: `chart` = `{"kind": "rob", "tool", "studies", "domains", "cells"}` with `data_file` and `data_sha256`."

### Task 8: `case_lint.py` — E-CASE (spec 5.1, 5.3)

**Files:**
- Create: `plugins/orchestra/scripts/case_lint.py`, `tests/unit/test_case_lint.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `crolib.projectcfg.get` (Task 3), `crolib.drafts.is_draft`, `crolib.ledgers.load_references`; lazily (inside functions, to avoid an import cycle once `audit.py` imports this module in Task 9) `audit.find_phi_scan()`, `audit.PHI_LINE`, `audit.citation_problem(rec)`; phi-guard's `phi_scan.py --file`.
- Produces CLI `case_lint.py <project> [--strict]`; importable `case_lint.lint(project, strict=False, phi=True) -> list[tuple[code, level, loc, msg]]` (codes `E-CASE`, and `E-REF` for `similar-cases.csv`), `case_lint.consent_recorded(project) -> bool`, `case_lint.mask_relative_time(text) -> str` (D20, used by `audit.py`), `case_lint.bmj_mode(project) -> bool`, constants `DOSSIER = "01_case/dossier.md"`, `SIMILAR = "01_case/similar-cases.csv"`, `DRAFTS = "06_writing/case-report"`, `CONSENT` (compiled regex).
- Rules (spec 5.3, D19): blocks — calendar dates (`12/03/2025`, `2025-03-12`, `tháng 3 năm 2025`, `tháng 03/2025`, `March 2025`, `12 March 2025`, `March 12, 2025`), exact ages (`45 tuổi`, `5 tháng tuổi`, `45-year-old`, `aged 45`, `45 years of age`; ranges such as `40–49 tuổi` and `in her 40s` pass), the `institution.name` or `institution.city` text, phi-guard strong identifiers, missing consent when `--strict`, unverified `similar-cases.csv` references (E-REF); warns — missing consent without `--strict`, title with rare/first/hiếm/đầu tiên (and "case report" in BMJ mode), summary/abstract over `target.word_limit`, learning points outside 3–5 (BMJ), similar-cases table with fewer than 3 rows or missing, no timeline table, phi-guard weak identifiers. Consent line in `decisions.md`: `CONSENT: obtained <form, who signed>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `tests/unit/test_case_lint.py`:

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import audit
import case_lint
from p5_helpers import case_project

OK_REF = {"id": "lee2020", "title": "Fictional similar case", "authors": ["Lee A"], "journal": "J", "year": 2020,
          "pmid": "1", "verified_by": "verify_refs.py 2026-11-22 VERIFIED"}
SIMILAR = ("ref_id,age_range,sex,presentation,key_finding,treatment,outcome\n"
           "lee2020,40-49,F,fever,finding,drug,recovered\nlee2020,50-59,M,rash,finding,drug,recovered\n"
           "lee2020,30-39,F,fever,finding,surgery,recovered\n")


def set_institution(p: Path, name: str, city: str) -> None:
    y = (p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    y = re.sub(r"(?m)^institution:\n(?:  .*\n)*", f'institution:\n  name: "{name}"\n  city: "{city}"\n  template: ""\n', y)
    (p / "project.yaml").write_text(y, encoding="utf-8")


class TestCaseLint(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.p = case_project(self.t)
        set_institution(self.p, "Bệnh viện Mô phỏng", "Thành phố Giả định")
        (self.p / "ledgers" / "references.json").write_text(json.dumps({"references": [OK_REF]}), encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / case_lint.SIMILAR).write_text(SIMILAR, encoding="utf-8")
        self.d = self.p / case_lint.DRAFTS
        (self.d / "00-title.md").write_text("# Fever and rash after a fictional exposure\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.d / "05-timeline.md").write_text("## Timeline\n\n| Day | Event |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | admission |\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.p / "decisions.md").write_text("# Decisions\n\nCONSENT: obtained written consent form, signed by the patient\n",
                                             encoding="utf-8")

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def found(self, strict=False, level=None, code="E-CASE"):
        return [(loc, msg) for c, lv, loc, msg in case_lint.lint(self.p, strict=strict, phi=False)
                if c == code and level in (None, lv)]

    def dossier(self, text):
        (self.p / case_lint.DOSSIER).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")

    def test_clean_case_has_no_finding(self):
        self.dossier("Bệnh nhân nữ ở độ tuổi 40–49 tuổi, nhập viện ngày 0 vì sốt; ngày +3 nổi ban. "
                     "A woman in her 40s; on day +3 a rash appeared, 3 days later it faded.\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.found(strict=True), [])

    def test_dates_block(self):
        for s in ("Nhập viện 12/03/2025.", "Admitted 2025-03-12.", "Vào tháng 3 năm 2025.", "Vào tháng 03/2025.",
                  "Seen in March 2025.", "Seen on 12 March 2025.", "Seen on March 12, 2025."):
            with self.subTest(text=s):
                self.dossier(s + "\n")
                blocks = self.found(level="block")
                self.assertTrue(any("calendar date" in m for _, m in blocks), blocks)
                self.assertEqual(blocks[0][0], "01_case/dossier.md:1")

    def test_exact_ages_block_ranges_pass(self):
        for s in ("Bệnh nhân 45 tuổi.", "Trẻ 5 tháng tuổi.", "A 45-year-old woman.", "A woman aged 45.",
                  "45 years of age."):
            with self.subTest(text=s):
                self.dossier(s + "\n")
                self.assertTrue(any("exact age" in m for _, m in self.found(level="block")))
        for s in ("Bệnh nhân 40–49 tuổi.", "A woman in her 40s.", "A man in his fifth decade."):
            with self.subTest(text=s):
                self.dossier(s + "\n")
                self.assertEqual(self.found(level="block"), [])

    def test_institution_and_city_block(self):
        (self.d / "03-patient-information.md").write_text("Admitted to Bệnh viện Mô phỏng.\nLives in thành phố giả định.\n",
                                                          encoding="utf-8")
        blocks = self.found(level="block")
        self.assertIn("06_writing/case-report/03-patient-information.md:1", [loc for loc, _ in blocks])
        self.assertIn("06_writing/case-report/03-patient-information.md:2", [loc for loc, _ in blocks])

    def test_consent(self):
        (self.p / "decisions.md").write_text("# Decisions\n\nCONSENT: obtained\n", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertFalse(case_lint.consent_recorded(self.p))
        self.assertTrue(any("consent" in m for _, m in self.found(level="warn")))
        self.assertTrue(any("consent" in m for _, m in self.found(strict=True, level="block")))
        (self.p / "decisions.md").write_text("- CONSENT: obtained written form, signed by the patient's mother\n",
                                             encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertTrue(case_lint.consent_recorded(self.p))
        self.assertEqual(self.found(strict=True, level="block"), [])

    def test_structure_warnings(self):
        y = (self.p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        y = y.replace("word_limit: null", "word_limit: 20") + "\n" if "word_limit: null" in y else y
        y = re.sub(r"(?m)^  journal_style:.*$", "  journal_style: bmj", y) if "journal_style" in y else \
            y.replace("target:\n", "target:\n  journal_style: bmj\n")
        (self.p / "project.yaml").write_text(y, encoding="utf-8")
        (self.d / "00-title.md").write_text("# A rare first case report of fictional fever\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.d / "01-summary.md").write_text("# Summary\n\n" + "word " * 30 + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.d / "09-learning-points.md").write_text("# Learning points\n\n- one\n- two\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.d / "05-timeline.md").unlink()
        (self.p / case_lint.SIMILAR).write_text(SIMILAR.split("\n", 2)[0] + "\n" + SIMILAR.split("\n")[1] + "\n",
                                                encoding="utf-8")
        warns = " | ".join(m for _, m in self.found(level="warn"))
        for s in ("'rare'", "'first'", "'case report'", "over the 20-word limit", "2 learning points",
                  "fewer than 3 rows", "no timeline table"):
            self.assertIn(s, warns)

    def test_similar_cases_references_must_be_verified(self):
        (self.p / case_lint.SIMILAR).write_text(SIMILAR + "ghost2021,20-29,M,x,y,z,w\n", encoding="utf-8")
        refs = self.found(level="block", code="E-REF")
        self.assertTrue(any("ghost2021" in m for _, m in refs), refs)

    def test_phi_guard_identifiers(self):
        self.dossier("Họ tên: Nguyễn Văn An\n")
        found = [f for f in case_lint.lint(self.p, phi=True) if f[0] == "E-CASE" and f[1] == "block"]
        self.assertTrue(any("identifier" in f[3] for f in found), found)
        with mock.patch.object(audit, "find_phi_scan", return_value=None):
            found = case_lint.lint(self.p, phi=True)
            self.assertTrue(any(f[1] == "warn" and "phi-guard not found" in f[3] for f in found))

    def test_mask_relative_time(self):
        text = "Day +3 rash; ngày 14 sốt; tuần 6 khỏi; 3 days later; sau 3 ngày; week 6; Hb 78 g/L"
        masked = case_lint.mask_relative_time(text)
        for tok in ("+3", "14", "tuần 6", "3 days", "sau 3", "week 6"):
            self.assertNotIn(tok, masked)
        self.assertIn("78 g/L", masked)
        self.assertEqual(len(masked), len(text))

    def test_cli_exit_codes(self):
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
            self.assertEqual(case_lint.main([str(self.p), "--strict"]), 0)
            self.dossier("Bệnh nhân 45 tuổi.\n")
            self.assertEqual(case_lint.main([str(self.p)]), 1)
            self.assertEqual(case_lint.main([str(self.t / "none")]), 2)
        self.assertIn("E-CASE block 01_case/dossier.md:1:", out.getvalue())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

(Before Task 9 the YAML has no `journal_style` key; the test adds it under `target:`; after Task 9 it replaces the existing `journal_style: null` line.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `case_lint.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""E-CASE: anonymisation, consent and structure checks for a case report.

  python case_lint.py <project> [--strict]

Scans 01_case/dossier.md and the drafts in 06_writing/case-report/. Blocks: calendar dates (dd/mm/yyyy,
yyyy-mm-dd, "tháng 3 năm 2025", "March 2025"), exact ages ("45 tuổi", "45-year-old", "aged 45"; ranges and
decades pass), the institution or city named in project.yaml (institution.name, institution.city), identifiers
phi-guard reports as strong, unverified references in 01_case/similar-cases.csv (E-REF), and — with --strict, the
final-export path — consent not recorded in decisions.md as "CONSENT: obtained <form, who signed>". Warns:
consent missing (without --strict), a title with "rare"/"first"/"hiếm"/"đầu tiên" (and "case report" in BMJ mode),
a summary over target.word_limit, learning points outside 3-5 (BMJ mode), a similar-cases table with fewer than 3
rows, no timeline table, phi-guard weak identifiers.
Exit codes: 0 = no block; 1 = block findings; 2 = usage or I/O error.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

for _s in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    try:
        _s.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from crolib import drafts, ledgers, projectcfg  # noqa: E402

DOSSIER = "01_case/dossier.md"
SIMILAR = "01_case/similar-cases.csv"
DRAFTS = "06_writing/case-report"
SIMILAR_COLS = ("ref_id", "age_range", "sex", "presentation", "key_finding", "treatment", "outcome")
MONTH = (r"(?:Jan(?:uary)?|Feb(?:ruary)?|Mar(?:ch)?|Apr(?:il)?|May|June?|July?|Aug(?:ust)?|Sep(?:t(?:ember)?)?"
         r"|Oct(?:ober)?|Nov(?:ember)?|Dec(?:ember)?)")
DATES = [re.compile(r"\b\d{1,2}[/.-]\d{1,2}[/.-](?:19|20)\d{2}\b"),
         re.compile(r"\b(?:19|20)\d{2}-\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}\b"),
         re.compile(r"(?i)\btháng\s+\d{1,2}(?:\s+năm\s+|\s*/\s*)(?:19|20)\d{2}\b"),
         re.compile(rf"\b(?:\d{{1,2}}\s+)?{MONTH}\.?\s+(?:\d{{1,2}},?\s+)?(?:19|20)\d{{2}}\b")]
AGES = [re.compile(r"(?i)(?<![\d–—-])\b\d{1,3}\s*(?:(?:tháng|ngày)\s+)?tuổi\b"),
        re.compile(r"(?i)\b\d{1,3}[- ](?:year|month|week|day)[- ]old\b"),
        re.compile(r"(?i)\baged\s+\d{1,3}\b"),
        re.compile(r"(?i)\b\d{1,3}\s+(?:years?|months?)\s+of\s+age\b")]
CONSENT = re.compile(r"(?im)^\s*(?:[-*]\s+)?CONSENT:\s*obtained\b[ \t]*[,:;-]?[ \t]*(\S.*)$")
TITLE_WORDS = [("rare", r"\brare\b"), ("first", r"\bfirst\b"), ("hiếm", r"hiếm"), ("đầu tiên", r"đầu tiên")]
TIMELINE = re.compile(r"(?i)timeline|dòng thời gian|diễn tiến theo thời gian")
BULLET = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:[-*]|\d+\.)\s+\S")
RELTIME = [re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:days?|weeks?|months?|hours?|years?|ngày|tuần|tháng|giờ|năm)\s*(?:thứ\s*)?[+−-]?\s*\d+\b"),
           re.compile(r"(?i)\b\d+\s*(?:days?|weeks?|months?|hours?)\s+(?:later|after|before|earlier)\b"),
           re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:after|within|for|over)\s+\d+\s*(?:days?|weeks?|months?|hours?)\b"),
           re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:sau|trong|trước)\s+\d+\s*(?:ngày|tuần|tháng|giờ)\b"),
           re.compile(r"(?i)\b\d+\s*(?:ngày|tuần|tháng|giờ)\s+(?:sau|trước)\b")]


def bmj_mode(project: Path) -> bool:
    return str(projectcfg.get(project, "target.journal_style", "") or "").casefold() == "bmj"


def mask_relative_time(text: str) -> str:
    """Blank out relative-time tokens (day +3, ngày 14, 3 days later …) keeping the length (plan D20)."""
    for rx in RELTIME:
        text = rx.sub(lambda m: " " * len(m.group(0)), text)
    return text


def consent_recorded(project: Path) -> bool:
    p = Path(project) / "decisions.md"
    return p.is_file() and CONSENT.search(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) is not None


def _files(project: Path) -> list[Path]:
    out = [project / DOSSIER] if (project / DOSSIER).is_file() else []
    d = project / DRAFTS
    if d.is_dir():
        out += sorted((p for p in d.glob("*.md") if p.is_file() and drafts.is_draft(p.name)), key=lambda p: p.name)
    return out


def _rel(project: Path, p: Path) -> str:
    return p.resolve().relative_to(project.resolve()).as_posix()


def _words(path: Path) -> int:
    text = re.sub(r"<!--.*?-->", " ", path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"), flags=re.S)
    return sum(len(ln.split()) for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith("#"))


def _phi(project: Path, files: list[Path]) -> list[tuple]:
    import audit  # lazy: audit.py imports this module
    script = audit.find_phi_scan()
    if script is None:
        return [("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}:1", "phi-guard not found; identifiers not scanned (install phi-guard)")]
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script), "--file", *map(str, files)], capture_output=True, text=True,
                       encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if r.returncode not in (0, 1):
        return [("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}:1", f"phi_scan could not run (exit {r.returncode})")]
    out = []
    for line in r.stdout.splitlines():
        m = audit.PHI_LINE.match(line.strip())
        if m:
            loc = Path(m.group(2))
            rel = _rel(project, loc) if loc.is_absolute() else loc.as_posix()
            out.append(("E-CASE", m.group(1), f"{rel}:{m.group(3)}", f"possible patient identifier: {m.group(4)}"))
    return out


def lint(project: Path, strict: bool = False, phi: bool = True) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
    import audit  # lazy, see _phi
    project = Path(project)
    found: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []
    files = _files(project)
    names = [str(projectcfg.get(project, k) or "").strip() for k in ("institution.name", "institution.city")]
    names = [n for n in names if len(n) >= 3]
    for f in files:
        rel = _rel(project, f)
        for i, line in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines(), 1):
            for rx in DATES:
                for m in rx.finditer(line):
                    found.append(("E-CASE", "block", f"{rel}:{i}", f'calendar date "{m.group(0)}"; use relative time '
                                                                   "(day 0, day +3, week 6)"))
            for rx in AGES:
                for m in rx.finditer(line):
                    found.append(("E-CASE", "block", f"{rel}:{i}", f'exact age "{m.group(0)}"; use a range '
                                                                   '("in her 40s", "40–49 tuổi")'))
            for n in names:
                if n.casefold() in line.casefold():
                    found.append(("E-CASE", "block", f"{rel}:{i}", f'names the institution or city "{n}" '
                                                                   "(project.yaml institution); remove it"))
    if phi and files:
        found += _phi(project, files)
    if not consent_recorded(project):
        found.append(("E-CASE", "block" if strict else "warn", "decisions.md:1",
                      "consent not recorded: add 'CONSENT: obtained <form, who signed>' to decisions.md "
                      "(the final export is refused without it)"))
    bmj = bmj_mode(project)
    d = project / DRAFTS
    title = d / "00-title.md"
    if title.is_file():
        first = next((ln.lstrip("# ").strip() for ln in title.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines() if ln.strip()), "")
        words = TITLE_WORDS + ([("case report", r"case report")] if bmj else [])
        for word, rx in words:
            if re.search(rx, first, re.I):
                found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}/00-title.md:1", f"title contains '{word}'; journals "
                                                                           "discourage it (claim it only with evidence)"))
    limit = projectcfg.get(project, "target.word_limit")
    summary = d / ("01-summary.md" if bmj else "01-abstract.md")
    if summary.is_file() and isinstance(limit, str) and limit.isdigit() and _words(summary) > int(limit):
        found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}/{summary.name}:1",
                      f"{_words(summary)} words, over the {limit}-word limit (target.word_limit)"))
    lp = d / "09-learning-points.md"
    if bmj and lp.is_file():
        n = sum(1 for ln in lp.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines() if BULLET.match(ln))
        if not 3 <= n <= 5:
            found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}/09-learning-points.md:1", f"{n} learning points; BMJ asks for 3-5"))
    sim = project / SIMILAR
    if not sim.is_file():
        found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{SIMILAR}:1", "no similar-cases table (01_case/similar-cases.csv)"))
    else:
        with open(sim, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
            reader = csv.DictReader(fh)
            missing = [c for c in SIMILAR_COLS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or [])]
            rows = list(reader)
        if missing:
            found.append(("E-CASE", "block", f"{SIMILAR}:1", f"missing column(s) {', '.join(missing)}"))
        if len(rows) < 3:
            found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{SIMILAR}:1", f"similar-cases table has fewer than 3 rows ({len(rows)})"))
        refs = {r.get("id"): r for r in ledgers.load_references(project / "ledgers" / "references.json") if isinstance(r, dict)}
        for i, r in enumerate(rows, 2):
            why = audit.citation_problem(refs.get((r.get("ref_id") or "").strip()))
            if why:
                found.append(("E-REF", "block", f"{SIMILAR}:{i}", f"[@{r.get('ref_id')}] {why}"))
    has_timeline = any(TIMELINE.search(t) and any(ln.lstrip().startswith("|") for ln in t.splitlines())
                       for t in (p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") for p in files if p.parent == d))
    if d.is_dir() and not has_timeline:
        found.append(("E-CASE", "warn", f"{DRAFTS}:1", "no timeline table (CARE item 7)"))
    return found


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project")
    ap.add_argument("--strict", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not (project / "project.yaml").is_file():
        print(f"case_lint: {a.project} is not a project folder (no project.yaml)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    try:
        found = lint(project, a.strict)
    except (OSError, ValueError, csv.Error) as e:
        print(f"case_lint: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    for code, sev, loc, msg in found:
        print(f"{code} {sev} {loc}: {msg}")
    blocks = sum(1 for f in found if f[1] == "block")
    print(f"case_lint: {blocks} block, {len(found) - blocks} warn")
    return 1 if blocks else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Implementation notes: `test_clean_case_has_no_finding` contains "40–49 tuổi" (range) and "in her 40s" and relative times — none may match `DATES` or `AGES`. "ngày 0" is relative time, not a date; the date patterns need a 4-digit year. If phi-guard's patterns flag a fixture sentence in `test_phi_guard_identifiers` differently than expected, read `plugins/phi-guard/scripts/phi_scan.py` (labelled-name rule) and adjust the fixture sentence, not the rule.

```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import _paths  # noqa: F401
import assemble
import audit
import case_facts
import gate
import gate_hook
import guideline_checklist as gc
import worklog
from crolib import drafts, reviews
from p5_helpers import case_project, review_project

REF = {"id": "lee2020", "title": "Fictional similar case", "authors": ["Lee A"], "journal": "J", "year": 2020,
       "pmid": "1", "verified_by": "verify_refs.py 2026-11-22 VERIFIED", "abstract": "A fictional similar case."}
FACTS = "id,label,value,unit,day,source\nC01,haemoglobin at admission,78,g/L,0,lab report\n"
SIMILAR = ("ref_id,age_range,sex,presentation,key_finding,treatment,outcome\n" +
           "lee2020,40-49,F,fever,rash,drug,recovered\n" * 3)
CASE = {
    "00-title.md": "# Fever and rash after a fictional exposure\n",
    "01-abstract.md": "# Abstract\n\nA fictional woman in her 40s had haemoglobin {{R-A0-001.value}} g/L on day 0.\n",
    "03-patient-information.md": "# Patient information\n\nA woman in her 40s with fever since day -2.\n",
    "04-clinical-findings.md": "# Clinical findings\n\nHaemoglobin was {{R-A0-001.value}} g/L on day 0; a rash appeared on day +14.\n",
    "05-timeline.md": "# Timeline\n\nTable 1. Timeline\n| Day | Event |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | admission |\n| 3 | rash |\n",
    "09-discussion.md": "# Discussion\n\nSimilar fictional cases were reported [@lee2020].\n",
    "11-consent.md": "# Informed consent\n\nWritten consent was obtained from the patient.\n",
}
CARE_IDS = ["1", "2", "3a", "3b", "3c", "3d", "4", "5a", "5b", "5c", "5d", "6", "7", "8a", "8b", "8c", "8d", "9a", "9b",
            "9c", "10a", "10b", "10c", "10d", "11a", "11b", "11c", "11d", "12", "13"]


def quiet(fn, *args):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        code = fn(*args)
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


def write_reports(p: Path, d: str, guideline: str, ids: list[str]) -> None:
    dg = drafts.digest(p, d)
    for agent, (rel, crits) in reviews.REVIEWERS.items():
        (p / rel).write_text(f"STATUS: PASS\nFINDINGS\n| code | location | fix |\ndrafts-digest: {d} {dg}\n"
                             f"```scores\n{json.dumps({'deliverable': d, **dict.fromkeys(crits, 2)})}\n```\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (p / "reviews" / f"guideline-checklist-{d}.json").write_text(json.dumps(
        {"guideline": guideline, "deliverable": d, "items": {i: {"status": "na", "reason": "fixture"} for i in ids}}),
        encoding="utf-8")


def set_style(p: Path, style: str) -> None:
    y = (p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    (p / "project.yaml").write_text(re.sub(r"(?m)^  journal_style:.*$", f"  journal_style: {style}", y), encoding="utf-8")


class TestDrafts(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_orders_required_and_roles(self):
        c = case_project(self.t)
        self.assertEqual(drafts.variant(c, "case-report"), "care")
        self.assertEqual(drafts.default_order(c, "case-report")[3], "03-patient-information.md")
        self.assertIn("11-consent.md", drafts.required(c, "case-report"))
        set_style(c, "bmj")
        self.assertEqual(drafts.variant(c, "case-report"), "bmj")
        self.assertEqual(set(drafts.required(c, "case-report")),
                         {"03-case-presentation.md", "08-discussion.md", "09-learning-points.md", "11-consent.md"})
        s = review_project(self.t / "s")
        self.assertEqual(drafts.review_kind(s), "systematic")
        self.assertIn("sof.md", drafts.default_order(s, "review"))
        sc = review_project(self.t / "sc", design="scoping-review")
        self.assertNotIn("sof.md", drafts.default_order(sc, "review"))
        nr = review_project(self.t / "n", design="narrative-review")
        self.assertIn("04-*.md", drafts.default_order(nr, "review"))
        self.assertEqual(set(drafts.required(nr, "review")), {"02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "06-conclusion.md"})
        self.assertEqual(drafts.default_order(c, "thesis"), drafts.DEFAULT_ORDER["thesis"])   # unchanged
        self.assertEqual(drafts.role_of("06_writing/case-report/04-clinical-findings.md"), "case-presentation")
        self.assertTrue(drafts.results_bearing("06_writing/case-report/01-abstract.md"))
        self.assertTrue(drafts.results_bearing("06_writing/review/sof.md"))
        self.assertTrue(drafts.is_literature("06_writing/review/04-themes.md"))
        self.assertFalse(drafts.is_literature("06_writing/review/04-results.md"))
        self.assertFalse(drafts.is_literature("06_writing/case-report/02-introduction.md"))
        self.assertTrue(drafts.results_bearing("06_writing/thesis/ch4-discussion.md"))
        self.assertTrue(reviews.DIGEST.search("drafts-digest: case-report " + "a" * 64))
        self.assertTrue(reviews.DIGEST.search("drafts-digest: review " + "b" * 64))
        self.assertEqual(gc.DELIVERABLES, drafts.DELIVERABLES)
        self.assertEqual(gate.DELIVERABLES, drafts.DELIVERABLES)
        self.assertEqual(worklog.TEXT["vi"]["case-report"], "báo cáo ca bệnh")


class TestCaseReportDeliverable(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        p = self.p = case_project(self.t)
        (p / "01_case" / "facts.csv").write_text(FACTS, encoding="utf-8")
        (p / "01_case" / "similar-cases.csv").write_text(SIMILAR, encoding="utf-8")
        (p / "ledgers" / "references.json").write_text(json.dumps({"references": [REF]}), encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(quiet(case_facts.main, [str(p)])[0], 0)
        self.d = p / "06_writing" / "case-report"
        for name, text in CASE.items():
            (self.d / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        (p / "decisions.md").write_text("# Decisions\n\nCONSENT: obtained written consent form, signed by the patient\n",
                                        encoding="utf-8")

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_assemble_draft_and_required_sections(self):
        code, out, _ = quiet(assemble.main, [str(self.p), "case-report"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out)
        text = (self.d / "case-report.assembled.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn("haemoglobin 78 g/L", text)
        self.assertTrue((self.d / "case-report.draft.docx").is_file())
        (self.d / "11-consent.md").unlink()
        code, out, _ = quiet(assemble.main, [str(self.p), "case-report"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("required section missing: Informed consent (11-consent.md)", out)

    def test_audit_case_report(self):
        write_reports(self.p, "case-report", "CARE", CARE_IDS)
        code, out, err = quiet(audit.main, [str(self.p), "case-report"])
        data = json.loads((self.p / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        codes = {f["code"] for f in data["findings"] if f["level"] == "block"}
        self.assertNotIn("E-STAT", {f["code"] for f in data["findings"]})       # no register in a case report
        self.assertNotIn("E-NUM", codes, data["findings"])                       # "day +14" is relative time (D20)
        self.assertEqual(data["deliverables"], ["case-report"])
        self.assertIn("01_case/facts.csv", data["files"])
        self.assertIn("E-CASE", (self.p / "reviews" / "audit.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        (self.d / "03-patient-information.md").write_text("# Patient information\n\nA 45-year-old woman.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        write_reports(self.p, "case-report", "CARE", CARE_IDS)
        quiet(audit.main, [str(self.p), "case-report"])
        data = json.loads((self.p / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertTrue(any(f["code"] == "E-CASE" and f["level"] == "block" for f in data["findings"]))

    def test_final_export_needs_recorded_consent(self):
        (self.p / "reviews" / "audit.json").write_text(json.dumps(
            {"deliverable": "case-report", "deliverables": ["case-report"], "at": "t", "blocks": 0, "warns": 0,
             "files": {rel: gate.sha256(self.p / rel) for rel in
                       [p.relative_to(self.p).as_posix() for p in drafts.tracked_files(self.p, "case-report")]}}),
            encoding="utf-8")
        gate.open_gate(self.p, "G4", by="A")
        self.assertEqual(gate.export_problems(self.p, "case-report"), [])
        (self.p / "decisions.md").write_text("# Decisions\n", encoding="utf-8")
        probs = gate.export_problems(self.p, "case-report")
        self.assertTrue(any("consent" in x for x in probs), probs)
        d = gate_hook.decide({"tool_name": "Bash", "tool_input": {"command": "python /x/assemble.py . case-report --final"},
                              "cwd": str(self.p)})
        self.assertEqual(d["permissionDecision"], "deny")
        self.assertIn("consent", d["permissionDecisionReason"])


class TestReviewDeliverable(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.t = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.t, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_hook_prep_marker_needs_g2_and_final_review_needs_g4(self):
        p = review_project(self.t, g2=False)
        payload = {"tool_name": "Agent", "tool_input": {"prompt": "orchestra-prep: screen-suggest\nSuggest decisions."},
                   "cwd": str(p)}
        d = gate_hook.decide(payload)
        self.assertEqual(d["permissionDecision"], "deny")
        self.assertIn("G2", d["permissionDecisionReason"])
        gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by="A", files=["03_protocol/sr-protocol.md"])
        self.assertIsNone(gate_hook.decide(payload))
        d = gate_hook.decide({"tool_name": "Agent", "tool_input": {"prompt": "orchestra-role: review-systematic\nDraft."},
                              "cwd": str(p)})
        self.assertEqual(d["permissionDecision"], "deny")                     # writing still needs G3
        d = gate_hook.decide({"tool_name": "Bash", "cwd": str(p), "tool_input": {
            "command": "python /x/build_docx.py 06_writing/review/review.assembled.md out/review.docx --project ."}})
        self.assertEqual(d["permissionDecision"], "deny")

    def test_audit_review_rows(self):
        p = review_project(self.t)
        d = p / "06_writing" / "review"
        for name in ("02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "04-results.md", "05-discussion.md"):
            (d / name).write_text(f"# {name[3:-3].title()}\n\nFictional text.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (d / "04-results.md").write_text("# Results\n\nDrug X reduces mortality ({{R-A1-001.full}}).\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (p / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").write_text(json.dumps({"results": [
            {"id": "R-A1-001", "analysis_id": "A1", "status": "prespecified", "label": "pooled RR", "value": 0.7,
             "ci": [0.5, 0.98], "count": 3, "n": 300, "kind": "rr", "source_file": "x", "locator": "line 1", "raw": "x"}]}),
            encoding="utf-8")
        (p / "05_analysis" / "meta").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (p / "05_analysis" / "meta" / "grade.json").write_text(json.dumps({"outcomes": [{
            "outcome": "Mortality", "result": "R-A1-001", "design": "rct", "rob_tool": "RoB 2",
            "downgrades": {"risk_of_bias": [-1, "fictional"], "inconsistency": [-1, "fictional"]}}]}), encoding="utf-8")
        write_reports(p, "review", "PRISMA-2020", [])
        quiet(audit.main, [str(p), "review"])
        data = json.loads((p / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        codes = {(f["code"], f["level"]) for f in data["findings"]}
        self.assertIn(("E-SCR", "block"), codes)            # no screening log
        self.assertIn(("E-GRADE", "warn"), codes)           # low certainty, unhedged
        self.assertTrue(any(f["code"] == "E-STAT" for f in data["findings"]))   # register read from sr-protocol.md
        n = review_project(self.t / "n", design="narrative-review")
        (n / "06_writing" / "review" / "02-introduction.md").write_text("# Introduction\n\nText.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        write_reports(n, "review", "SANRA", [])
        quiet(audit.main, [str(n), "review"])
        data = json.loads((n / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertFalse({"E-SCR", "E-STAT"} & {f["code"] for f in data["findings"]})

    def test_worklog_digest_and_gate_check_accept_new_deliverables(self):
        p = review_project(self.t)
        (p / "06_writing" / "review" / "02-introduction.md").write_text("# Introduction\n", encoding="utf-8")
        code, out, _ = quiet(worklog.main, ["digest", str(p), "review"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertRegex(out, r"drafts-digest: review [0-9a-f]{64}")
        self.assertEqual(quiet(gate.main, ["check", str(p), "G4", "--deliverable", "review"])[0], 1)
```

```python
        if not shutil.which("git"):
            self.skipTest("git not installed")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:

                                                 capture_output=True).returncode == 0
            self.assertFalse(ignored("04_data/sr/extraction.csv"))
            self.assertTrue(ignored("04_data/raw.csv"))
```

`crolib/drafts.py` (full replacement; thesis and manuscript behaviour is unchanged):

```python
"""Draft files of a deliverable (thesis, manuscript, case-report, review): default section order, required sections,
roles, the digest reviewers quote, and the output language. Standard library only."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path

from . import projectcfg

DELIVERABLES = ("thesis", "manuscript", "case-report", "review")
DEFAULT_ORDER = {
    "thesis": ["00-tom-tat.md", "00-dat-van-de.md", "ch1-*.md", "ch2-methods.md", "ch3-results.md",
               "ch4-discussion.md", "05-ket-luan.md", "06-kien-nghi.md", "90-phu-luc.md"],
    "manuscript": ["00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "04-results.md",
                   "05-discussion.md", "06-conclusion.md"],
}
REQUIRED = {
    "thesis": {"ch2-methods.md": "Chương 2", "ch3-results.md": "Chương 3", "ch4-discussion.md": "Chương 4",
               "05-ket-luan.md": "Kết luận"},
    "manuscript": {"02-introduction.md": "Introduction", "03-methods.md": "Methods", "04-results.md": "Results",
                   "05-discussion.md": "Discussion"},
}
_REVIEW = ["00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "04-results.md", "sof.md",
           "05-discussion.md", "06-conclusion.md", "07-other-information.md"]
ORDERS = dict(DEFAULT_ORDER, **{
    "care": ["00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "02-introduction.md", "03-patient-information.md",
             "04-clinical-findings.md", "05-timeline.md", "06-diagnostic-assessment.md",
             "07-therapeutic-intervention.md", "08-follow-up.md", "09-discussion.md", "10-patient-perspective.md",
             "11-consent.md"],
    "bmj": ["00-title.md", "01-summary.md", "02-background.md", "03-case-presentation.md", "04-investigations.md",
            "05-differential-diagnosis.md", "06-treatment.md", "07-outcome.md", "08-discussion.md",
            "09-learning-points.md", "10-patient-perspective.md", "11-consent.md"],
    "systematic": _REVIEW,
    "scoping": [x for x in _REVIEW if x != "sof.md"],
    "narrative": ["00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "02-introduction.md", "03-methods.md", "04-*.md",
                  "05-discussion.md", "06-conclusion.md"],
})
_IMRAD = {"02-introduction.md": "Introduction", "03-methods.md": "Methods", "04-results.md": "Results",
          "05-discussion.md": "Discussion"}
REQUIRED_BY = dict(REQUIRED, **{
    "care": {"03-patient-information.md": "Patient information", "04-clinical-findings.md": "Clinical findings",
             "05-timeline.md": "Timeline", "09-discussion.md": "Discussion", "11-consent.md": "Informed consent"},
    "bmj": {"03-case-presentation.md": "Case presentation", "08-discussion.md": "Discussion",
            "09-learning-points.md": "Learning points", "11-consent.md": "Patient consent"},
    "systematic": _IMRAD,
    "scoping": _IMRAD,
    "narrative": {"02-introduction.md": "Introduction", "03-methods.md": "Methods (scope and search)",
                  "06-conclusion.md": "Conclusion"},
})
ROLE = {"00-tom-tat.md": "abstract", "00-dat-van-de.md": "introduction", "ch2-methods.md": "methods",
        "ch3-results.md": "results", "ch4-discussion.md": "discussion", "05-ket-luan.md": "conclusion",
        "06-kien-nghi.md": "conclusion", "01-abstract.md": "abstract", "02-introduction.md": "introduction",
        "03-methods.md": "methods", "04-results.md": "results", "05-discussion.md": "discussion",
        "06-conclusion.md": "conclusion"}
CASE_ROLE = dict.fromkeys(["00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "01-summary.md", "02-introduction.md", "02-background.md"],
                          "case-front")
CASE_ROLE.update(dict.fromkeys(["03-patient-information.md", "04-clinical-findings.md", "05-timeline.md",
                                "06-diagnostic-assessment.md", "07-therapeutic-intervention.md", "08-follow-up.md",
                                "03-case-presentation.md", "04-investigations.md", "05-differential-diagnosis.md",
                                "06-treatment.md", "07-outcome.md"], "case-presentation"))
CASE_ROLE.update(dict.fromkeys(["09-discussion.md", "08-discussion.md", "09-learning-points.md",
                                "10-patient-perspective.md", "11-consent.md"], "case-discussion"))
RESULTS_ROLES = {"results", "discussion", "conclusion", "abstract", "case-front", "case-presentation", "case-discussion"}
DERIVED = (".rendered.md", ".numbered.md", ".humanized.md", ".assembled.md")
REVIEW_KIND = {"systematic-review": "systematic", "scoping-review": "scoping", "narrative-review": "narrative"}


def folder(project, deliverable: str) -> Path:
    if deliverable not in DELIVERABLES:
        raise ValueError(f"deliverable must be one of {DELIVERABLES}")
    return Path(project) / "06_writing" / deliverable


def review_kind(project) -> str:
    return REVIEW_KIND.get(str(projectcfg.read_project_yaml(project).get("design", "")), "narrative")


def variant(project, deliverable: str) -> str:
    if deliverable == "case-report":
        return "bmj" if str(projectcfg.get(project, "target.journal_style", "") or "").casefold() == "bmj" else "care"
    if deliverable == "review":
        return review_kind(project)
    folder(project, deliverable)  # validates the name
    return deliverable


def default_order(project, deliverable: str) -> list[str]:
    return list(ORDERS[variant(project, deliverable)])


def required(project, deliverable: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    return dict(REQUIRED_BY[variant(project, deliverable)])


def is_draft(name: str) -> bool:
    return name.endswith(".md") and not name.endswith(DERIVED)


def draft_files(project, deliverable: str) -> list[Path]:
    base = folder(project, deliverable)
    if not base.is_dir():
        return []
    return sorted((p for p in base.glob("*.md") if p.is_file() and is_draft(p.name)), key=lambda p: p.name)


def tracked_files(project, deliverable: str) -> list[Path]:
    """Drafts plus order.txt: what the digest covers and what a revert restores."""
    order = folder(project, deliverable) / "order.txt"
    return draft_files(project, deliverable) + ([order] if order.is_file() else [])


def order_entries(project, deliverable: str) -> list[str]:
    order = folder(project, deliverable) / "order.txt"
    if order.is_file():
        return [ln.strip() for ln in order.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines()
                if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith("#")]
    return default_order(project, deliverable)


def ordered(project, deliverable: str) -> tuple[list[Path], list[str], list[str]]:
    """(section files in order, order entries not found, required sections missing)."""
    base = folder(project, deliverable)
    files: list[Path] = []
    missing: list[str] = []
    for entry in order_entries(project, deliverable):
        if "/" in entry or "\\" in entry or ".." in entry:
            missing.append(entry)
            continue
        if any(c in entry for c in "*?["):
            hits = sorted((p for p in base.glob(entry) if p.is_file() and is_draft(p.name)), key=lambda p: p.name)
        else:
            hits = [base / entry] if (base / entry).is_file() and is_draft(entry) else []
        if not hits:
            missing.append(entry)
        files += [h for h in hits if h not in files]
    names = {p.name for p in files}
    return files, missing, [n for n in required(project, deliverable) if n not in names]


def role_of(path) -> str | None:
    p = Path(path)
    if p.parent.name == "case-report":
        return CASE_ROLE.get(p.name)
    if p.parent.name == "review":
        if p.name == "sof.md":
            return "results"
        if p.name.startswith("04-") and p.name != "04-results.md":
            return "synthesis"
    return ROLE.get(p.name)


def results_bearing(path) -> bool:
    return role_of(path) in RESULTS_ROLES


def is_literature(path) -> bool:
    p = Path(path)
    if p.parent.name == "case-report":
        return False
    return p.name.startswith("ch1-") or p.name == "02-introduction.md" or role_of(p) == "synthesis"


def digest(project, deliverable: str) -> str:
    paths = tracked_files(project, deliverable)
    if not draft_files(project, deliverable):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no drafts in 06_writing/{deliverable}/")
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for p in sorted(paths, key=lambda x: x.name):
        h.update(p.name.encode("utf-8") + b"\0" + hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest().encode("ascii") + b"\n")
    return h.hexdigest()


def lang_of(project, deliverable: str) -> str:
    target = projectcfg.read_project_yaml(project).get("target", {})
    if deliverable != "thesis" and isinstance(target, dict) and target.get("language") in ("vi", "en"):
        return target["language"]
    return projectcfg.output_lang(project)
```

`crolib/reviews.py`: `DIGEST = re.compile(r"^drafts-digest:[ \t]*(thesis|manuscript|case-report|review)[ \t]+([0-9a-f]{64})[ \t]*$", re.M)`.

`assemble.py`: in `format_findings` replace the two `drafts.REQUIRED[deliverable]` uses with a local `req = drafts.required(project, deliverable)` (`req[n]`, `e not in req`); in `_join` replace `prev in ("00-title.md", "01-abstract.md")` with `prev in ("00-title.md", "01-abstract.md", "01-summary.md")`. Docstring: first line "Assemble a thesis, manuscript, case report or review …", usage `thesis|manuscript|case-report|review`, and a sentence "Case report (CARE or, with target.journal_style: bmj, BMJ headings) and review (systematic, scoping or narrative from project.yaml design) orders and required sections: crolib/drafts.py ORDERS and REQUIRED_BY. A case report's --final also needs consent recorded in decisions.md (gate.export_problems)."

`gate.py`: replace `DELIVERABLES = ("thesis", "manuscript")` with `DELIVERABLES = drafts.DELIVERABLES` (move the line below the `drafts` import if needed). In `export_problems`, before `return problems`:

```python
    if tool in AGENT:
        text = " ".join(str(tin.get(k, "")) for k in ("prompt", "description")) if isinstance(tin, dict) else str(tin)
        m, prep = ROLE.search(text), PREP.search(text)

        project = _find_project(cwd)

        if prep:

            if not ok:
                return _deny(f"[orchestra] review-work brief '{prep.group(1)}' (screening suggestions, draft extraction "
                             f"or risk of bias) needs the locked protocol and G2 is not open: {'; '.join(why)}. The author "

                             "Sàng lọc, trích xuất và đánh giá nguy cơ sai lệch chỉ làm sau khi tác giả khoá đề cương (G2).")
        if m:
            ok, why = gate.check_gate(project, "G3")
            if not ok:
                return _deny(f"[orchestra] writer role '{m.group(1)}' needs locked results and G3 is not open: "
                             f"{'; '.join(why)}. The author reviews the results and locks them first "

    return None
```

In `check_numbers_`, before `check_numbers.find_unmatched`, use `checked = case_lint.mask_relative_time(rendered) if d == "case-report" else rendered` and pass `checked` to `find_unmatched` (keep `rendered` for `check_derived`). Add:

```python
def stat_applies(project: Path, d: str) -> bool:
    return d in ("thesis", "manuscript") or (d == "review" and drafts.review_kind(project) in ("systematic", "scoping"))


def check_case(a: Audit) -> None:
    for code, level, loc, msg in case_lint.lint(a.project, strict=False, phi=False):
        a.add("case report" if code == "E-CASE" else "citations", code, level, loc, msg)


def check_screening(a: Audit) -> None:
    for sev, loc, msg in screening.check(a.project) + extract_check.check(a.project):
        a.add("screening and extraction", "E-SCR", sev, loc, msg)


def check_grade(a: Audit, files: list[Path]) -> None:
    for sev, loc, msg in grade.check_wording(a.project, [f for f in files if drafts.results_bearing(f)]):
        a.add("GRADE wording", "E-GRADE", sev, loc, msg)
```
