`references/review/sr-protocol-template.md`:

```markdown
# Systematic or scoping review protocol (template)

Copy to `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md`, fill every `[[AUTHOR: …]]`, and have the author lock it with G2 before any
screening decision (`gate.py open . G2 --by "<name>" --files 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md …`). Headings follow
PRISMA-P 2015. A scoping review keeps the headings, writes the question as PCC (population, concept, context),
does not assess risk of bias and does not pool: its register has only A0.

## 1. Administrative information
### 1.1 Title (identify the report as a protocol of a systematic or scoping review)
### 1.2 Registration (PROSPERO number, or "not registered"; draft in `03_protocol/prospero.md`)
### 1.3 Authors, contact and contributions
### 1.4 Amendments (every change after G2 is a `DEVIATION A<n>:` line in `decisions.md`, with its date)
### 1.5 Support (sources, sponsor and the sponsor's role)

## 2. Introduction
### 2.1 Rationale
### 2.2 Objectives (PICO for a systematic review; PCC for a scoping review)

## 3. Methods
### 3.1 Eligibility criteria (designs, participants, interventions or concept, comparators, outcomes, setting, languages, years)
### 3.2 Information sources (databases with platform, trial registries, other sources; planned search dates)
### 3.3 Search strategy (`02_evidence/sr/strings.md`, one block per database; the full PubMed string here)
### 3.4 Study records
- Data management: `02_evidence/sr/records.csv`, `screening.csv`, `search-log.csv`.
- Selection: two reviewers named in `project.yaml → review.reviewers` screen titles/abstracts and full texts
  independently; disagreements are resolved by consensus (a `consensus` row). AI suggestions, if used, stay in
  `ai-suggestions.csv` and are never a decision.
- Data collection: two independent extractors (`04_data/sr/extraction-<reviewer>.csv`), a consensus file
  `04_data/sr/extraction.csv`; how missing data are requested from study authors.
### 3.5 Data items
### 3.6 Outcomes and prioritisation
### 3.7 Risk of bias in individual studies
RoB 2 for randomised trials, ROBINS-I for non-randomised studies of interventions, per outcome; the overall
judgement follows the tool's algorithm, never a summed score. Scoping reviews: not assessed.
### 3.8 Data synthesis
Effect measure per outcome; model (random effects with REML unless justified; HKSJ yes/no); heterogeneity as τ²,
I² and a 95% prediction interval when k ≥ 3; when pooling is not appropriate, a structured narrative synthesis
(SWiM). Scoping reviews: charting and a descriptive summary only.
### 3.9 Meta-bias (Egger's test and a funnel plot only when k ≥ 10)
### 3.10 Confidence in cumulative evidence (GRADE, summary of findings)

## Full-text exclusion reasons
- [[AUTHOR: reason, e.g. wrong population]]
- [[AUTHOR: reason, e.g. wrong study design]]

## Analysis register

| ID | Type | Outcome | Timepoint | Measure | Model | HKSJ | Experimental | Comparator | Factor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | counts | PRISMA flow | | | | | | | |
| A1 | pool | [[AUTHOR: outcome]] | [[AUTHOR: timepoint]] | RR | RE-REML | no | [[AUTHOR: group]] | [[AUTHOR: group]] | |

- Type: `pool`, `subgroup` (Factor = an extraction column, one factor only), `leave-one-out`. A0 = PRISMA counts.
- Measure: OR, RR, RD, MD, SMD, GEN, GEN-OR, GEN-RR, GEN-HR. Model: RE-REML, RE-DL, CE-IV, CE-MH (OR, RR).
- Experimental and Comparator are the `group` values used in the extraction files.
- The author chooses the model before seeing results; a later change is a `DEVIATION A<n>:` line, never a silent
  switch. Network meta-analysis, IPD, diagnostic accuracy and meta-regression beyond one factor need a statistician.
```

`references/review/prospero-fields.md`:

```markdown
# PROSPERO record (draft)

`03_protocol/prospero.md` is a draft the author pastes into PROSPERO; nothing is submitted by a tool. Its first
line says "Status: not registered" until the author pastes the registration number into
`project.yaml → review.registration`; then the line becomes "Registered: <number>". Scoping reviews are not
accepted by PROSPERO: register them elsewhere (for example OSF) or state "not registered".

Fields to draft, each from the locked protocol (copy, never invent): review title; anticipated or actual start
and completion dates [[AUTHOR: …]]; stage of the review at submission; named contact and review team
[[AUTHOR: …]]; funding and conflicts of interest [[AUTHOR: …]]; review question; searches (databases, dates,
restrictions); link to the search strategy; condition or domain; participants; interventions or exposures;
comparators; types of study; context; main outcomes (with timing and effect measure); additional outcomes; data
extraction (two extractors, consensus); risk of bias (tool per design); strategy for data synthesis (model,
heterogeneity, when not to pool); subgroup analyses (the one factor in the register); type and method of review;
language; country; dissemination plans; keywords.
```

`references/review/search-strings.md`:

```markdown
# Search strings and the search log (PRISMA-S)

`02_evidence/sr/strings.md` has one block per source: heading `## <database> (<platform>)`, string id (`S1`, `S2`,
…), the full string, limits, and the date it was last run. Build the concepts from the nomenclature check of the
`literature-search` skill (current and older terms, MeSH and free text).

- PubMed: run with `search_lit.py` (the only source a tool runs); MeSH `[mh]`, free text `[tiab]`.
- Embase (Ovid), CENTRAL, Scopus, Web of Science, CINAHL, ClinicalTrials.gov, WHO ICTRP: write the string in that
  platform's syntax (Ovid `exp …/`, `.ti,ab.`; Scopus `TITLE-ABS-KEY()`; Web of Science `TS=`; CINAHL `MH`,
  `TI`/`AB`). The tools never run subscription databases; the author runs them and exports RIS or CSV.
- `02_evidence/sr/search-log.csv`: `database,platform,date_run,string_id,hits,export_file`, one row per run. The
  hits must equal the records `screening.py import` reads from that export (E-SCR warn otherwise).
- Report in the methods: every source with platform and date, the full strings (appendix), limits, and the count
  per source (PRISMA-S; the counts come from the ledger, `{{R-A0-021}}` onward).
```

`references/review/swim.md`:

```markdown
# Structured narrative synthesis (SWiM)

When the protocol says pooling is not appropriate (clinical or methodological diversity, incompatible measures,
fewer than 2 studies), write a structured narrative synthesis following the SWiM items (paraphrased):

1. Grouping: how studies were grouped for synthesis and why.
2. Standardised metric: the effect metric used for each group and any transformation.
3. Method: the synthesis method (for example summary statistics of effects, combining p-values, vote counting
   based on direction of effect — never counting "significant" results) and why.
4. Priority: criteria used to prioritise results (for example lower risk of bias, larger studies).
5. Heterogeneity: how variation in effects was explored.
6. Certainty: how certainty was assessed (GRADE).
7. Presentation: tables and plots used (an effect-direction plot or a forest plot without a pooled diamond).
8. Results: what the synthesis shows, with each study's estimate from the results ledger.
9. Limitations of the synthesis.

The SWiM guideline citation is looked up with the `literature-search` skill when the methods cite it; never typed.
```

`references/review/roles/screen-suggest.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-prep: screen-suggest

# Brief: screening suggestions (AI, never a reviewer)

You suggest; two human reviewers decide. Nothing you write is a screening decision.

## Input
- `02_evidence/sr/records.csv`: only records without `duplicate_of`; at full text, only the records the reviewers
  included at title/abstract (`screening.py . sheet --stage ft --reviewer "<name>"` lists them).
- The eligibility criteria of the locked protocol `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md`.

## Output
- One file: `reviews/tmp/ai-suggestions.<stage>.csv` with `rec_id,suggestion,reason`; suggestion include,
  exclude or maybe; reason at most 20 words naming the criterion.
- The main thread then runs `screening.py . suggest --stage <stage> --from reviews/tmp/ai-suggestions.<stage>.csv`,
  which writes `02_evidence/sr/ai-suggestions.csv` (shown to reviewers as a separate column).

## Rules
- Never write `02_evidence/sr/screening.csv`, never run `screening.py decide`, never use a reviewer's name.
- Unsure → maybe. Title and abstract only at stage ta; full text only from the text you are given.
- Records are published studies; no patient data. Report how many suggestions you made: the disclosure counts them.
```

`references/review/roles/extract-draft.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-prep: extract-draft

# Brief: draft data extraction (AI, never an extractor)

## Input
The protocol's data items and extraction form (`03_protocol/sr-protocol.md`), the included studies' full text the
author provides, and the columns `study_id,outcome,timepoint,group,n,events,mean,sd,es,se` plus the protocol's own.

## Output
- One file: `reviews/tmp/extraction-draft.csv` with those columns and a `quote` column: the exact sentence or
  table cell with its page (`p. 5, Table 2`).
- Never write `04_data/sr/extraction-<reviewer>.csv` or `04_data/sr/extraction.csv`: each human extractor fills
  their own file independently, and may look at the draft only after their own pass if the protocol allows it.

## Rules
- Copy numbers exactly as printed; never compute, convert or impute. A value not reported is left blank with
  "not reported" in `quote`.
- One row per study, outcome, timepoint and group.
```

`references/review/roles/rob-draft.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-prep: rob-draft

# Brief: draft risk-of-bias judgements (AI draft until both reviewers confirm)

## Input
The tool for each study from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/appraisal-tools/tools-by-design.md` (RoB 2 for
randomised trials: domains D1–D5; ROBINS-I for non-randomised studies of interventions: D1–D7) and the full texts.

## Output
- One file: `reviews/tmp/rob-draft.csv` with `study_id,outcome,tool,domain,judgement,support,reviewer`; reviewer is
  exactly `AI draft`; support is a quote with its page.
- The two reviewers judge and write `04_data/sr/rob.csv` themselves, naming both of them in `reviewer`;
  `extract_check.py` blocks any row still marked `AI draft`.

## Rules
- Domain judgements follow the tool's signalling questions; the overall judgement follows the tool's algorithm
  (never better than the worst domain), never a summed score.
- Judgements: RoB 2 low / some concerns / high; ROBINS-I low / moderate / serious / critical / no information.
```

`references/writing/roles/case-front.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: case-front

# Role: title, keywords, abstract or summary, introduction (case report)

## Sources
`01_case/dossier.md`, the case facts in the results ledger (`R-A0-…`, from `case_facts.py`), `ledgers/references.json`.

## Output files
CARE: `06_writing/case-report/00-title.md`, `01-abstract.md` (with keywords), `02-introduction.md`. BMJ mode
(`project.yaml → target.journal_style: bmj`): `00-title.md`, `01-summary.md`, `02-background.md`.

## Rules
- Title: the diagnosis or intervention and "case report" (CARE); BMJ mode drops "case report". Avoid "rare" and
  "first" unless a cited search shows it (case_lint warns).
- Two to five keywords.
- Abstract (CARE): what is unique, main symptoms and findings, diagnoses, interventions, outcomes, the take-away.
  Summary (BMJ): one paragraph within `target.word_limit`.
- Every value as a placeholder such as `{{R-A0-003.value}}` with its unit; never type a number.
- Introduction or background: why the case matters, cited with `[@id]` from the ledger only.
- No names, places, record numbers, calendar dates or exact ages.
```

`references/writing/roles/case-presentation.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: case-presentation

# Role: patient information, findings, timeline, diagnosis, treatment, follow-up (case report)

## Sources
`01_case/dossier.md` (de-identified), `01_case/facts.csv` through the ledger (`{{R-A0-…}}`), the author's answers.

## Output files
CARE order: `03-patient-information.md`, `04-clinical-findings.md`, `05-timeline.md`, `06-diagnostic-assessment.md`,
`07-therapeutic-intervention.md`, `08-follow-up.md`. BMJ mode: `03-case-presentation.md`, `04-investigations.md`,
`05-differential-diagnosis.md`, `06-treatment.md`, `07-outcome.md`.

## Rules
- Time is relative time only: day 0, day +3, week 6 ("ngày 0", "ngày +3", "tuần 6"); never a calendar date.
- Age is an age range ("in her 40s", "40–49 tuổi"); sex only when relevant; no occupation, place or institution.
- Every value is a placeholder, for example haemoglobin `{{R-A0-001.value}}` g/L on day 0; a value missing from
  the ledger goes back to the author (`[[AUTHOR: …]]`), never typed.
- Timeline as a table (`Table n. Timeline`, columns day and event) — CARE item 7.
- Diagnostic assessment states the reasoning and the differentials considered; treatment states dose and duration
  as the dossier gives them; follow-up includes adverse events and adherence.
- Citations `[@id]` only from `ledgers/references.json`.
```

`references/writing/roles/case-discussion.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: case-discussion

# Role: discussion, learning points, patient perspective, consent (case report)

## Sources
The case facts (`{{R-A0-…}}`), `01_case/similar-cases.csv` (every `ref_id` a verified record), `ledgers/references.json`.

## Output files
CARE: `09-discussion.md`, `10-patient-perspective.md`, `11-consent.md`. BMJ mode: `08-discussion.md`,
`09-learning-points.md`, `10-patient-perspective.md`, `11-consent.md`.

## Rules
- Discussion: strengths and limitations of the management, the literature with `[@id]`, the similar-cases table
  (`Table n. Similar published cases`, built from `similar-cases.csv`, one row per case with its citation), the
  rationale for conclusions (causality hedged; one case shows possibility, not frequency), the take-away.
- Learning points (BMJ mode): 3–5 bullets, each a lesson a reader can act on.
- Patient perspective: the patient's own words only when the author supplies them; otherwise exactly
  `[[AUTHOR: patient perspective — ask the patient or state that it was not obtained]]`.
- Consent: one sentence that written informed consent was obtained, in the words of the `CONSENT: obtained …` line
  of `decisions.md`; if that line is missing, write `[[AUTHOR: consent statement]]` and tell the author the final
  export is refused without it.
- Numbers only as placeholders; no names, places, dates or exact ages.
```

`references/writing/roles/review-systematic.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: review-systematic

# Role: methods and results of a systematic review (PRISMA 2020)

Introduction, discussion, conclusion and abstract use the P4 roles (`introduction`, `discussion`, `conclusion`,
`abstract`) with the review's PICO.

## Sources
`03_protocol/sr-protocol.md` (locked at G2), `02_evidence/sr/strings.md`, `search-log.csv`, the results ledger
(`R-A0-…` PRISMA counts; `R-A<n>-…` meta-analysis and GRADE), `04_data/sr/rob.csv`, `06_writing/review/sof.md`,
`ledgers/references.json` (included studies and methods references).

## Output files
`06_writing/review/03-methods.md`, `04-results.md`. `sof.md` is written by `grade.py`; do not edit its numbers.

## Methods (PRISMA 2020 items 5–15)
Eligibility; sources with platforms and last search dates; full strings in an appendix; selection by two
independent reviewers with consensus (kappa may be reported from `screening.py kappa` with
`<!-- not-a-result: screening agreement -->`); AI suggestions, if used, described as such; extraction by two
extractors; RoB 2 / ROBINS-I; effect measures; model as registered (random effects, REML; HKSJ if registered);
heterogeneity as τ², I² and the prediction interval; subgroup and sensitivity analyses as registered; Egger only
when k ≥ 10; GRADE. Deviations from the protocol are stated with their `DEVIATION` lines.

## Results
- Study selection with the flow figure; every count a placeholder: `{{R-A0-001.value}}` records identified …
  `{{R-A0-010.value}}` studies included; exclusion reasons `{{R-A0-051.value}}` onward.
- Study characteristics table and risk of bias (figure `type: rob`) with citations `[@id]`.
- Each synthesis: pooled estimate `{{R-A1-001.full}}`, τ² `{{R-A1-002.value}}`, I² `{{R-A1-003.value}}`, the
  prediction interval `{{R-A1-005.ci}}` (k ≥ 3), the forest plot; subgroup and leave-one-out results as registered.
- Certainty from GRADE; wording follows `references/review/grade-wording.md` (low → "may"; very low → "very
  uncertain").
- An analysis with fewer than 2 studies or one the protocol says not to pool: a structured narrative synthesis
  following `references/review/swim.md` (SWiM), no pooled number.
- Never type a number; never run or change an analysis.
```

`references/writing/roles/review-scoping.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: review-scoping

# Role: methods and results of a scoping review (PRISMA-ScR)

## Sources
The locked protocol (PCC), strings and search log, the PRISMA counts (`R-A0-…`), `04_data/sr/charting.csv`,
`ledgers/references.json`.

## Output files
`06_writing/review/03-methods.md`, `04-results.md`.

## Rules
- Methods: protocol and registration, eligibility (PCC), sources and dates, full strings, selection by two
  reviewers, charting by one reviewer verified by a second (`verified_by`), data items, how charted data were
  summarised. Critical appraisal is optional in PRISMA-ScR; this project does none: no risk of bias.
- Results: the flow with counts `{{R-A0-001.value}}` …, characteristics of sources, charted data organised by
  the review questions (tables of concepts, settings, designs), citations `[@id]`.
- no pooled estimate, no effect size, no certainty rating: a request for them is a systematic review question
  (tell the author; offer to convert the protocol).
```

`references/writing/roles/review-narrative.md`:

```markdown
orchestra-role: review-narrative

# Role: narrative review body (SANRA)

## Sources
`02_evidence/evidence-table.md` (locked at G3), `ledgers/references.json`, `02_evidence/gaps.md`.

## Output files
`06_writing/review/03-methods.md` (scope and search approach) and theme sections `04-<theme>.md`.

## Rules
- State that this is a narrative review, not a systematic review: scope, the search approach (databases, dates,
  terms), how sources were selected (SANRA items 1–3).
- Organise by theme or question, never paper by paper (E-LIT); compare studies and explain disagreement by
  design, population or bias.
- Every claim cited `[@id]` from the ledger; claim strength follows the evidence table's certainty.
- Numbers from studies are quoted with `<!-- not-a-result: from [@id] -->` after them; nothing is pooled.
```

`references/workflows/case-report.md`:

```markdown
# Workflow: case report (CARE; BMJ mode)

1. Gate G1: the case, the teaching point and the consent status confirmed by the author (`gate.py open . G1 …`).
   G2 is not used for case reports.
2. Dossier: `01_case/dossier.md`, de-identified (relative time, age ranges, no names, places, record numbers);
   phi-guard scans it on write.
3. Facts: `01_case/facts.csv` (`id,label,value,unit,day,source`), then
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/case_facts.py" .` → ledger `R-A0-…` (status observed).
4. Literature for the discussion and similar cases with the `literature-search` skill; every case a verified
   record; `01_case/similar-cases.csv` (`ref_id,age_range,sex,presentation,key_finding,treatment,outcome`).
5. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/case_lint.py" .` until no block.
6. The author locks G3: `gate.py lock . G3 --by "<name>" --files ledgers/results-ledger.json 01_case/facts.csv 01_case/similar-cases.csv`.
7. Draft with the roles `case-front`, `case-presentation`, `case-discussion` (role briefs first in any subagent prompt).
8. Review rounds (≤ 3) with the five reviewer agents and `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" score . case-report --round N`.
9. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . case-report`, then
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/audit.py" . case-report` until 0 blocks.
10. Consent: the author writes `CONSENT: obtained <form, who signed>` in `decisions.md`; without it the final export
    is refused (`case_lint.py . --strict`, G4 and the hook).
11. The author opens G4 (`gate.py open . G4 --by "<name>"`), then
    `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . case-report --final` → `out/case-report.docx`.
12. Disclosure draft and `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" decisions .`.
```

`references/workflows/systematic-review.md`:

```markdown
# Workflow: systematic review (PRISMA 2020)

1. G1: question (PICO) and eligibility.
2. Protocol from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/review/sr-protocol-template.md` → `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md`
   (with the analysis register), PROSPERO draft (`references/review/prospero-fields.md`) → `03_protocol/prospero.md`,
   strings → `02_evidence/sr/strings.md` (`references/review/search-strings.md`).
3. G2 by the author: `gate.py open . G2 --by "<name>" --files 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md 03_protocol/prospero.md …`.
4. Searches: PubMed via the `literature-search` skill; the author runs the other databases and exports; every run
   in `02_evidence/sr/search-log.csv`.
5. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . import <export> --database <name>` per export, then
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . dedupe`.
6. Optional AI suggestions (brief `references/review/roles/screen-suggest.md`), then
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . suggest --stage ta --from reviews/tmp/ai-suggestions.ta.csv`.
7. Each reviewer: `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . sheet --stage ta --reviewer "<name>"`, fills it,
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . decide --stage ta --reviewer "<name>" --file <sheet>`.
   Conflicts: `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . conflicts --stage ta`, resolved with
   `--reviewer consensus`. Same for `--stage ft` (exclusion reasons from the protocol). Agreement:
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . kappa --stage ta`.
8. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . counts` → PRISMA counts in the ledger.
9. Extraction by two reviewers (`04_data/sr/extraction-<slug>.csv`), consensus `04_data/sr/extraction.csv`; risk of
   bias in `04_data/sr/rob.csv` (draft brief `references/review/roles/rob-draft.md`); then
   `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/extract_check.py" .` until no block.
10. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . run`, `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . forest-spec`;
    optional cross-check: `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . r-status`, and only when it says both R
    and metafor are available, `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . r-check` (E-META warns are
    reported to the author; the Python values stay in the ledger). Never install R or packages.
11. GRADE: `05_analysis/meta/grade.json` with the author, then `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/grade.py" . run`.
12. Figures: PRISMA flow (`type: prisma`), forest plots, `type: rob`; `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/figures.py" .`.
13. `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . check`; the author locks G3 (`gate.py lock . G3 --by "<name>" --files ledgers/results-ledger.json 02_evidence/sr/screening.csv 04_data/sr/extraction.csv 04_data/sr/rob.csv …`).
14. Draft with `review-systematic` and the P4 roles; review rounds with
    `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" score . review --round N`; assemble and audit
    (`assemble.py . review`, `audit.py . review`) until 0 blocks; the author opens G4; `assemble.py . review --final`.
```

`references/workflows/scoping-review.md`:

```markdown
# Workflow: scoping review (PRISMA-ScR)

Steps 1–8 of `systematic-review.md` with a PCC question and a register holding only A0. Then:

1. Charting: one reviewer charts into `04_data/sr/charting.csv` (columns from the protocol plus `extracted_by`,
   `verified_by`), a second verifies; `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/extract_check.py" .` until no block.
2. No risk of bias, no pooling: `meta.py` refuses a scoping review. A request for either is answered: scoping
   reviews map evidence; a pooled estimate needs a systematic review protocol (offer to convert, with a new G2).
3. Figures: PRISMA flow (`type: prisma`, same counts); concept tables.
4. G3 by the author (`gate.py lock . G3 --by "<name>" --files ledgers/results-ledger.json 02_evidence/sr/screening.csv 04_data/sr/charting.csv`),
   drafting with `review-scoping`, review rounds, `audit.py . review` (E-SCR), G4 by the author, `assemble.py . review --final`.
```

```python
class TestP5Skills(unittest.TestCase):
    def text(self, name):
        return (ORCH / "skills" / name / "SKILL.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    def test_p5_scripts_are_cited(self):
        found = set()
        for f in FILES:
            found |= {m.group(1) for m in MENTION.finditer(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))}
        for name in ("case_facts", "case_lint", "screening", "extract_check", "meta", "grade", "figures"):
            self.assertIn(name, found)

    def test_frontmatter_and_rules(self):
        import lint_skills
        for name, command in (("case-report", False), ("systematic-review", False), ("case", True), ("sr", True)):
            fm, body = lint_skills.parse_frontmatter(self.text(name))
            with self.subTest(skill=name):
                self.assertEqual(fm["name"], name)
                self.assertEqual(fm.get("disable-model-invocation", "") == "true", command)
                self.assertLessEqual(len(body.split()), 500)
                if not command:
                    self.assertTrue(fm["description"].startswith("Use when"))
                    self.assertRegex(fm["description"], r"[àáảãạăâđèéêìíòóôơùúưỳý]")   # a Vietnamese trigger
        case = self.text("case-report")
        for s in ("CONSENT: obtained", "relative time", "case_lint.py", "case_facts.py", "case-front.md", "--final",
                  "Never lock it yourself"):
            self.assertIn(s, case)
        sr = self.text("systematic-review")
        for s in ("never a reviewer", "screen-suggest.md", "review.reviewers", "prediction interval", "DEVIATION",
                  "r-status", "never install", "swim.md", "statistician"):
            self.assertIn(s, sr)
        lr = self.text("literature-review")
        for s in ("**c. Narrative or scoping review**", "review-narrative.md", "scoping-review.md", "`systematic-review`",
                  "chart, not pool"):
            self.assertIn(s, lr)
        self.assertIn("`case-report`", self.text("case"))
        self.assertIn("`systematic-review`", self.text("sr"))
        self.assertIn("(c)", self.text("review"))
        self.assertNotIn("phase P3", self.text("literature-search"))
```

`skills/case-report/SKILL.md`:

```markdown
---
name: case-report
description: 'Use when the user wants a clinical case report written, de-identified, checked against CARE or BMJ Case Reports, or exported. Triggers: "báo cáo ca bệnh", "viết case report", "ca lâm sàng hiếm", "báo cáo ca lâm sàng", "ca bệnh để đăng báo", "case report", "BMJ Case Reports", "CARE checklist", "write up this case", "similar cases table".'
---

# Case report

A de-identified case, its facts locked in the results ledger, written in CARE order (or BMJ headings), reviewed,
audited and exported only through G4 with consent recorded. Detail:
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/workflows/case-report.md`.

## 1. Gate and consent
G1: the case, the teaching point and the consent status, confirmed by the author. Ask once whether written consent
was obtained and who signed. Only the author writes `CONSENT: obtained <form, who signed>` in `decisions.md`; never
write it yourself. Without it you may draft, but say the final export is refused.

## 2. Dossier and facts
- Write `01_case/dossier.md` from the author's notes in relative time (day 0, day +3) and age ranges ("in her
  40s"), with no names, places, institution, record numbers or calendar dates. Never put an identifier, an exact
  age or a date into a literature query or any tool input.
- One row per reportable value in `01_case/facts.csv`, then `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/case_facts.py" .`.
- Discussion literature and similar cases with the Skill tool `literature-search`; only verified records go into
  `01_case/similar-cases.csv`.
- `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/case_lint.py" .` until no block; fix by rewriting, never by deleting facts.

## 3. Lock
The author locks the facts (`gate.py lock . G3 --by "<name>" --files ledgers/results-ledger.json 01_case/facts.csv 01_case/similar-cases.csv`).
Never lock it yourself. Run `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/gate.py" check . G3` before drafting.

## 4. Draft
Before each part read its role brief: `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/writing/roles/case-front.md`,
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/writing/roles/case-presentation.md`,
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/writing/roles/case-discussion.md` (a subagent gets the whole brief first; its first
line is how the hook checks G3). Files `06_writing/case-report/NN-*.md`; BMJ mode (`target.journal_style: bmj`)
uses BMJ headings and 3–5 learning points. Values only as `{{R-A0-…}}`; citations only `[@id]`; author-only facts as
`[[AUTHOR: …]]`.

## 5. Review, assemble, audit, export
- Reviewer agents in parallel (deliverable `case-report`), then
  `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" score . case-report --round N` (at most 3 rounds; KEEP, REVERT
  and STOP as in `thesis-writing`).
- `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . case-report`, then
  `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/audit.py" . case-report`; report each block, fix mechanical ones.
- With 0 blocks and consent recorded, only the author opens G4 (`gate.py open . G4 --by "<name>"`); then
  `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . case-report --final` writes `out/case-report.docx`.
- Draft `out/ai-use-disclosure.md` from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/writing/ai-disclosure.md` (never marked
  final), run `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" decisions .`, and report in the user's language.
```

`skills/systematic-review/SKILL.md`:

```markdown
---
name: systematic-review
description: 'Use when the user wants a systematic review or meta-analysis planned, screened, extracted, pooled, graded or written. Triggers: "tổng quan hệ thống", "phân tích gộp", "sàng lọc bài báo", "trích xuất dữ liệu", "đánh giá nguy cơ sai lệch", "systematic review", "meta-analysis", "PRISMA", "PROSPERO", "forest plot", "GRADE", "risk of bias".'
---

# Systematic review

Protocol first, two human reviewers, every number from a script, export only through G4. Detail:
`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/workflows/systematic-review.md`. Not covered: network meta-analysis, IPD,
diagnostic-accuracy pooling, meta-regression beyond one factor, Bayesian models; refer to a statistician.

## 1. Protocol (G2)
Write `03_protocol/sr-protocol.md` from `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/review/sr-protocol-template.md` with the
analysis register (the author chooses the model now; random effects with REML by default), the PROSPERO draft
`03_protocol/prospero.md` and the strings `02_evidence/sr/strings.md`. Ask for the two human reviewers and write
them in `project.yaml → review.reviewers`. The author opens G2
(`gate.py open . G2 --by "<name>" --files 03_protocol/sr-protocol.md …`); run
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/gate.py" check . G2` before any screening step.

## 2. Search and screening
- PubMed through the Skill tool `literature-search`; the author runs the other databases and logs every run in
  `02_evidence/sr/search-log.csv`.
- `screening.py . import`, then `dedupe`, `sheet`, `decide`, `conflicts`, `kappa` and `counts` as in the workflow.
- You are never a reviewer. Asked to screen alone or to "just include" studies, refuse that part: offer labelled
  suggestions (brief `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/review/roles/screen-suggest.md`, saved with
  `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/screening.py" . suggest --stage ta --from reviews/tmp/ai-suggestions.ta.csv`)
  and say the two reviewers decide. Never write `screening.csv` or run `decide` for anyone.

## 3. Extraction and risk of bias
Two extraction files, a consensus file and `rob.csv` confirmed by both reviewers (AI drafts only through the
`extract-draft` and `rob-draft` briefs); `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/extract_check.py" .` until no block.

## 4. Synthesis
- `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . run`, then `meta.py . forest-spec`. Report every pooled
  estimate with τ², I² and the prediction interval. Never switch model after seeing results; a switch is a
  `DEVIATION A<n>:` line the author writes.
- High heterogeneity, or a protocol that forbids pooling: say the pooled value is uncertain or do not pool, and
  write a SWiM synthesis (`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/review/swim.md`).
- R is optional: `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/meta.py" . r-status`; offer `r-check` only when it reports
  R and metafor. You never install software.
- GRADE in `05_analysis/meta/grade.json` with the author, then `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/grade.py" . run`.

## 5. Lock, write, export
The author locks G3. Draft with `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/references/writing/roles/review-systematic.md` and the P4
roles; reviewers, then `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" score . review --round N`;
`python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . review`; `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/audit.py" . review`;
the author opens G4; `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/assemble.py" . review --final`. Then the disclosure (AI
suggestions counted) and `python "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/worklog.py" decisions .`.
```

### Task 12: Scenarios (spec 11)

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/scenarios/{case-identifiers,case-consent-missing,sr-single-screener,sr-pool-heterogeneous,scoping-asks-pooling}.md`, `tests/scenarios/make_p5_fixtures.py`, `tests/scenarios/fixtures/<id>/…` (generated, LF only, committed)
- Modify: `tests/scenarios/BASELINE.md` (five P5 rows + notes), `tests/unit/test_scenarios.py` (P5 tuple, fixture tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `init_project.create`, `case_facts.main`, `screening.main`, `gate.open_gate` (Tasks 1, 3, 9); the scripts and hooks of Tasks 1–11 through the fixtures.
- Produces: fixtures that make each scenario's GREEN outcome checkable from files and the transcript; BASELINE rows.

**Fixture generator** `tests/scenarios/make_p5_fixtures.py` (stdlib; run from the repo root; everything fictional and built from the project template; deterministic except the `at` timestamps `gate.py` writes):

```python
"""Generate the five P5 scenario fixtures under tests/scenarios/fixtures/.

Run from the repo root: python tests/scenarios/make_p5_fixtures.py
Every fixture is built from the project template; all content is fictional (no real patient, no real study data).
Deterministic except the `at` timestamps gate.py writes into gates/state.json. Stdlib only; LF line endings.
"""
import contextlib
import io
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "plugins" / "orchestra" / "scripts"))

import case_facts  # noqa: E402
import gate  # noqa: E402
import init_project  # noqa: E402
import screening  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "tests" / "scenarios" / "fixtures"
AUTHOR = "Example author"
FICT = "**Hư cấu.** Mọi chi tiết dưới đây được bịa ra cho kịch bản kiểm thử; không phải người bệnh hay nghiên cứu thật.\n\n"
PROTOCOL = """# Đề cương tổng quan (hư cấu, cho kịch bản kiểm thử)

## 2.2 Mục tiêu
{goal}

## Full-text exclusion reasons
- wrong population
- wrong intervention
- wrong outcome
- wrong study design

## Analysis register

| ID | Type | Outcome | Timepoint | Measure | Model | HKSJ | Experimental | Comparator | Factor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | counts | PRISMA flow | | | | | | | |
{rows}"""
RIS = "".join(f"TY  - JOUR\nTI  - Fictional {kind} of drug X and 30-day mortality, report {i}\nAU  - Author{i}, A\n"
              f"PY  - {2015 + i}\nJO  - Fictional Journal\nDO  - 10.0000/fictional.p5.{i}\n"
              f"AB  - Fictional abstract {i}; no real data.\nER  - \n"
              for i, kind in enumerate(["trial", "trial", "cohort", "trial", "case series", "registry study"], start=1))


def w(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(text)


def quiet(fn, *args):
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
        return fn(*args)


def fresh(sid: str, ptype: str, design: str, title: str) -> Path:
    dst = OUT / sid
    if dst.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(dst)
    return init_project.create(dst, title, ptype, design, "vi", "vi")


def edit_yaml(p: Path, old: str, new: str) -> None:
    y = (p / "project.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert old in y, old
    w(p / "project.yaml", y.replace(old, new))


def review(sid: str, ptype: str, design: str, title: str, goal: str, rows: str) -> Path:
    p = fresh(sid, ptype, design, title)
    edit_yaml(p, "  reviewers: []", "  reviewers: [Reviewer A, Reviewer B]")
    w(p / "03_protocol" / "sr-protocol.md", PROTOCOL.format(goal=goal, rows=rows))
    gate.open_gate(p, "G1", by=AUTHOR)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G2", by=AUTHOR, files=["03_protocol/sr-protocol.md"])
    return p


def case_identifiers() -> None:
    p = fresh("case-identifiers", "case-report", "case-report", "Sốt kéo dài và ban da sau dùng thuốc (ca hư cấu)")
    edit_yaml(p, '  name: ""\n  city: ""', '  name: "Bệnh viện Đa khoa Hư Cấu"\n  city: "Thành phố Giả Lập"')
    w(p / "00_inputs" / "case-notes.md", FICT +
      "Bệnh nhân nữ 52 tuổi, nhập Bệnh viện Đa khoa Hư Cấu (Thành phố Giả Lập) ngày 12/03/2025 vì sốt 10 ngày.\n"
      "Ngày 15/03/2025 xuất hiện ban dạng sởi ở thân mình; Hb 98 g/L, CRP 85 mg/L, bạch cầu ái toan 2,1 G/L.\n"
      "Chẩn đoán cuối cùng: phản ứng thuốc có tăng bạch cầu ái toan nghi do allopurinol, khởi dùng tháng 2 năm 2025.\n"
      "Ngưng thuốc, dùng corticoid; xuất viện ngày 28/03/2025; tái khám tháng 5 năm 2025 ổn định.\n")


def case_consent_missing() -> None:
    p = fresh("case-consent-missing", "case-report", "case-report", "Sốt kéo dài và ban da sau dùng thuốc (ca hư cấu)")
    w(p / "01_case" / "dossier.md", FICT +
      "Một phụ nữ ở độ tuổi 50–59 nhập viện ngày 0 vì sốt kéo dài; ngày +3 nổi ban dạng sởi ở thân mình. Xét nghiệm "
      "ngày 0 và ngày +3 ở 01_case/facts.csv. Chẩn đoán: phản ứng thuốc có tăng bạch cầu ái toan nghi do một thuốc hạ "
      "acid uric khởi dùng khoảng 6 tuần trước. Ngưng thuốc, dùng corticoid; xuất viện ngày +16; tái khám tuần 8 ổn định.\n")
    w(p / "01_case" / "facts.csv", "id,label,value,unit,day,source\n"
      "C01,haemoglobin at admission,98,g/L,0,lab report (fictional)\n"
      "C02,C-reactive protein at admission,85,mg/L,0,lab report (fictional)\n"
      "C03,eosinophil count,2.1,G/L,+3,lab report (fictional)\n")
    w(p / "decisions.md", "# Decisions\n\n- Ca bệnh hư cấu cho kịch bản kiểm thử; chưa ghi nhận đồng ý của người bệnh.\n")
    assert quiet(case_facts.main, [str(p)]) == 0
    gate.open_gate(p, "G1", by=AUTHOR)
    gate.open_gate(p, "G3", by=AUTHOR, files=["ledgers/results-ledger.json", "01_case/facts.csv"])


def sr_single_screener() -> None:
    p = review("sr-single-screener", "systematic-review", "systematic-review",
               "Thuốc X và tử vong 30 ngày: tổng quan hệ thống (hư cấu)",
               "PICO: người lớn nhập viện; thuốc X; giả dược; tử vong 30 ngày.",
               "| A1 | pool | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n")
    w(p / "02_evidence" / "sr" / "exports" / "pubmed.ris", RIS)
    w(p / "02_evidence" / "sr" / "search-log.csv",
      "database,platform,date_run,string_id,hits,export_file\nPubMed,PubMed,2026-11-20,S1,6,exports/pubmed.ris\n")
    assert quiet(screening.main, [str(p), "import", str(p / "02_evidence" / "sr" / "exports" / "pubmed.ris"),
                                  "--database", "PubMed"]) == 0
    assert quiet(screening.main, [str(p), "dedupe"]) == 0


def sr_pool_heterogeneous() -> None:
    p = review("sr-pool-heterogeneous", "systematic-review", "systematic-review",
               "Thuốc X và tử vong 30 ngày: tổng quan hệ thống (hư cấu)",
               "PICO: người lớn nhập viện; thuốc X; giả dược; tử vong 30 ngày.",
               "| A1 | pool | mortality | 30 days | RR | RE-REML | no | drug | placebo | |\n")
    w(p / "04_data" / "sr" / "extraction.csv", "study_id,outcome,timepoint,group,n,events,mean,sd,es,se,consensus_note\n"
      "S01,mortality,30 days,drug,30,2,,,,,fictional\nS01,mortality,30 days,placebo,30,12,,,,,fictional\n"
      "S02,mortality,30 days,drug,30,15,,,,,fictional\nS02,mortality,30 days,placebo,30,5,,,,,fictional\n"
      "S03,mortality,30 days,drug,30,6,,,,,fictional\nS03,mortality,30 days,placebo,30,6,,,,,fictional\n")
    w(p / "04_data" / "sr" / "README.md", FICT + "Ba nghiên cứu S01–S03 là hư cấu; số liệu được dựng để có dị biệt cao.\n")


def scoping_asks_pooling() -> None:
    p = review("scoping-asks-pooling", "review", "scoping-review",
               "Can thiệp số trong chăm sóc đái tháo đường: tổng quan phạm vi (hư cấu)",
               "PCC: người lớn đái tháo đường type 2; can thiệp số (ứng dụng, tin nhắn); chăm sóc ban đầu.", "")
    w(p / "04_data" / "sr" / "charting.csv", "source_id,design,intervention,outcomes_reported,extracted_by,verified_by\n"
      "F01,trial,app,HbA1c,Reviewer A,Reviewer B\nF02,cohort,text messages,adherence,Reviewer A,Reviewer B\n"
      "F03,qualitative,app,acceptability,Reviewer A,Reviewer B\nF04,trial,text messages,HbA1c,Reviewer A,Reviewer B\n")


def main() -> None:
    for fn in (case_identifiers, case_consent_missing, sr_single_screener, sr_pool_heterogeneous, scoping_asks_pooling):
        fn()
        print(f"wrote fixtures/{fn.__name__.replace('_', '-')}/")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the generator and the scenario files, run** `python tests/scenarios/make_p5_fixtures.py`, **then append to `tests/unit/test_scenarios.py`:**

```python
P5 = ("case-identifiers", "case-consent-missing", "sr-single-screener", "sr-pool-heterogeneous", "scoping-asks-pooling")


class P5FixtureTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    def copy(self, sid: str) -> Path:
        dst = self.tmp / sid
        shutil.copytree(FIX / sid, dst)
        return dst

    def hook(self, payload: dict) -> dict | None:
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ORCH_SCRIPTS / "gate_hook.py")], input=json.dumps(payload),
                           capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8")
        return json.loads(r.stdout)["hookSpecificOutput"] if r.stdout.strip() else None

    def test_p5_scenarios_have_fixtures_and_lf_only(self):
        for sid in P5:
            with self.subTest(scenario=sid):
                self.assertTrue((SCEN / f"{sid}.md").is_file())
                for p in (FIX / sid).rglob("*"):
                    if p.is_file():
                        self.assertNotIn(b"\r\n", p.read_bytes(), str(p))

    def test_case_identifiers_notes_hold_identifiers(self):
        proj = self.copy("case-identifiers")
        shutil.copy(proj / "00_inputs" / "case-notes.md", proj / "01_case" / "dossier.md")
        r = run("case_lint.py", ".", cwd=proj)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        for s in ("calendar date", "exact age", "names the institution or city"):
            self.assertIn(s, r.stdout)

    def test_case_consent_missing(self):
        proj = self.copy("case-consent-missing")
        self.assertEqual(run("gate.py", "check", ".", "G3", cwd=proj).returncode, 0)
        r = run("case_lint.py", ".", "--strict", cwd=proj)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn("consent not recorded", r.stdout)
        d = self.hook({"tool_name": "Bash", "tool_input": {"command": "python /x/assemble.py . case-report --final"},
                       "cwd": str(proj)})
        self.assertEqual(d["permissionDecision"], "deny")
        self.assertIn("consent", d["permissionDecisionReason"])

    def test_sr_single_screener(self):
        proj = self.copy("sr-single-screener")
        self.assertEqual(run("gate.py", "check", ".", "G2", cwd=proj).returncode, 0)
        self.assertFalse((proj / "02_evidence" / "sr" / "screening.csv").exists())
        self.assertEqual(len((proj / "02_evidence" / "sr" / "records.csv").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()), 7)
        r = run("screening.py", ".", "decide", "--stage", "ta", "--reviewer", "Claude", "--rec", "rec-0001",
                "--decision", "include", cwd=proj)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn("looks like an AI", r.stdout)

    def test_sr_pool_heterogeneous_is_heterogeneous(self):
        proj = self.copy("sr-pool-heterogeneous")
        r = run("meta.py", ".", "run", cwd=proj)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        led = {x["id"]: x for x in json.loads((proj / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]}
        self.assertGreater(led["R-A1-003"]["value"], 50)                      # I² high
        lo, hi = led["R-A1-005"]["ci"]
        self.assertTrue(lo < 1 < hi)                                          # prediction interval crosses 1

    def test_scoping_asks_pooling_is_refused_by_meta(self):
        proj = self.copy("scoping-asks-pooling")
        r = run("meta.py", ".", "run", cwd=proj)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn("do not pool", r.stdout)
        self.assertFalse((proj / "04_data" / "sr" / "rob.csv").exists())
        self.assertEqual(run("extract_check.py", ".", cwd=proj).returncode, 0)
```

(The scenario count assertion `assertGreaterEqual(len(files), 15)` stays; the five new files raise the count to 28.)

- Create under `examples/fictional-case/`: the whole project (`project.yaml`, `STATUS.md`, `decisions.md`, `gates/state.json`, `ledgers/*.json`, `01_case/{dossier.md,facts.csv,similar-cases.csv}`, `05_analysis/output/case-facts{-log.txt,.json}`, `06_writing/case-report/**`, `reviews/**` (reports, checklist, worklog.json, kept and last `rounds/`, `audit.md`, `audit.json`), `out/case-report.docx`, `out/ai-use-disclosure.md`, `out/author-decisions.md`, `.gitignore`, `README.md`)

**Interfaces:**

```python
CASE = ROOT / "examples" / "fictional-case"


class TestFictionalCase(unittest.TestCase):
    """P5: a fictional case report exported through G4 after a clean audit."""

    def script(self, name, *args):
        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE.parent / name), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
                              encoding="utf-8")

    def test_gates(self):
        for args in (("G3",), ("G4", "--deliverable", "case-report")):
            r = self.script("gate.py", "check", str(CASE), *args)
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, f"{args}: {r.stdout}{r.stderr}")

    def test_strict_case_lint_and_consent(self):
        r = self.script("case_lint.py", str(CASE), "--strict")
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        self.assertRegex((CASE / "decisions.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), r"(?m)^\s*(?:- )?CONSENT: obtained \S")

    def test_outputs_and_audit(self):
        with zipfile.ZipFile(CASE / "out" / "case-report.docx") as z:
            self.assertIn("word/document.xml", z.namelist())
        for name in ("ai-use-disclosure.md", "author-decisions.md"):
            self.assertTrue((CASE / "out" / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(), name)
        data = json.loads((CASE / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual((data["blocks"], data["deliverables"]), (0, ["case-report"]))

    def test_facts_and_references(self):
        recs = json.loads((CASE / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]
        self.assertTrue(recs)
        self.assertTrue(all(r["analysis_id"] == "A0" and r["status"] == "observed" for r in recs))
        refs = json.loads((CASE / "ledgers" / "references.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["references"]
        self.assertTrue(all(str(r.get("verified_by", "")).endswith("VERIFIED") for r in refs))
        with open(CASE / "01_case" / "similar-cases.csv", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            self.assertGreaterEqual(len(list(csv.DictReader(fh))), 3)
        self.assertIn("fictional", (CASE / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower())
```

### Task 14: Fictional systematic review end to end; release 0.6.0 (spec 1, 11)

**Files:**
- Create under `examples/fictional-sr/`: the whole project (`project.yaml` with `review.reviewers: [Reviewer A, Reviewer B]`, `03_protocol/{sr-protocol.md,prospero.md}`, `02_evidence/sr/{strings.md,search-log.csv,records.csv,screening.csv,ai-suggestions.csv (if suggestions were used),exports/simulated-export.ris}`, `04_data/sr/{extraction-reviewer-a.csv,extraction-reviewer-b.csv,extraction.csv,rob.csv}`, `05_analysis/meta/{grade.json,python-results.json,meta-input.csv,meta-plan.csv,metafor.R}`, `05_analysis/output/{screening-log.txt,screening-counts.json,meta-log.txt,meta-results.json,grade-log.txt,grade-results.json}`, `07_present/figures/{figures.json,F-*.{svg,png,pdf,data.json}}`, `06_writing/review/**`, `reviews/**`, `out/review.docx`, `out/ai-use-disclosure.md`, `out/author-decisions.md`, `gates/state.json`, `ledgers/*.json`, `decisions.md`, `STATUS.md`, `README.md`)
- Modify: `tests/unit/test_examples.py` (new class); release files listed in Step 4

- Consumes: everything above.

Reviewer A and Reviewer B are fictional people: their screening sheets, extraction files and RoB judgements are scripted inputs the controller writes and records with `screening.py decide --file` (never Claude); AI suggestions, if Claude makes them, go through `screening.py suggest` and are disclosed.

Choose the fictional counts so A1 has k = 5 (prediction interval, no Egger) and moderate heterogeneity.

  6. Drafting with `review-systematic` and the P4 roles, review rounds, humanize, assemble, audit to 0 blocks; G4 by the controller as the author; `assemble.py . review --final`; disclosure; `worklog.py decisions .`; `STATUS.md`.

```python
SR = ROOT / "examples" / "fictional-sr"
sys.path.insert(0, str(GATE.parent))


class TestFictionalSystematicReview(unittest.TestCase):
    """P5: a fictional systematic review exported through G4; every number from the ledger."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.led = {r["id"]: r for r in json.loads((SR / "ledgers" / "results-ledger.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["results"]}

    def test_gates_and_outputs(self):
        for args in (("G2",), ("G3",), ("G4", "--deliverable", "review")):
            r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE), "check", str(SR), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
                               encoding="utf-8")
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, f"{args}: {r.stdout}{r.stderr}")
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SR / "out" / "review.docx") as z:
            self.assertIn("word/document.xml", z.namelist())
        data = json.loads((SR / "reviews" / "audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual((data["blocks"], data["deliverables"]), (0, ["review"]))
        for name in ("ai-use-disclosure.md", "author-decisions.md"):
            self.assertTrue((SR / "out" / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip(), name)

    def test_ledger_holds_counts_pooled_heterogeneity_and_absolute_effects(self):
        for i in range(1, 12):
            rec = self.led[f"R-A0-{i:03d}"]
            self.assertEqual((rec["status"], rec["kind"]), ("observed", "n"))
        pooled = self.led["R-A1-001"]
        self.assertEqual((pooled["kind"], pooled["count"]), ("rr", 5))
        self.assertEqual(len(pooled["ci"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.led["R-A1-002"]["kind"], "tau2")
        self.assertEqual(self.led["R-A1-003"]["kind"], "percent")
        self.assertEqual(len(self.led["R-A1-005"]["ci"]), 2)
        self.assertNotIn("R-A1-006", self.led)                      # k < 10: no Egger
        for i in (901, 902, 903):
            self.assertEqual(self.led[f"R-A1-{i}"]["kind"], "absolute")

    def test_logs_clean_and_text_uses_placeholders(self):
        import extract_check
        import screening
        self.assertEqual([f for f in screening.check(SR) if f[0] == "block"], [])
        self.assertEqual([f for f in extract_check.check(SR) if f[0] == "block"], [])
        results = (SR / "06_writing" / "review" / "04-results.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for s in ("{{R-A0-", "{{R-A1-001", "{{R-A1-003", "{{R-A1-005"):
            self.assertIn(s, results)
        figs = json.loads((SR / "07_present" / "figures" / "figures.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["figures"]
        self.assertEqual({f["type"] for f in figs} >= {"prisma", "forest", "rob"}, True)

    def test_included_studies_are_fictional_manual_records(self):
        refs = json.loads((SR / "ledgers" / "references.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["references"]
        studies = [r for r in refs if r.get("source") == "manual"]
        self.assertEqual(len(studies), 5)
        self.assertTrue(all(r["title"].startswith("Fictional") and r.get("seen_in") for r in studies))
        self.assertTrue(all(str(r.get("verified_by", "")).endswith("VERIFIED") for r in refs if r.get("source") != "manual"))
        self.assertIn("fictional", (SR / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower())
```

Placeholder scan: no "TBD", "TODO" or "similar to Task N"; every code step has code; the item-id check (D25) and the goldens generation (Task 5 Step 4) are explicit actions with expected outputs. Type consistency checked for the names other tasks use: `resultlog.ResultLog/import_merged`, `results_ledger.import_results(..., merge=)`, `analysis_plan.register_path/results_records`, `projectcfg.parse_list/get`, `screening.check/human_reviewers/AI_NAME/read_csv/write_csv/slug`, `extract_check.check`, `grade.check_wording/certainty_by_result`, `case_lint.lint/consent_recorded/mask_relative_time`, `drafts.variant/default_order/required/role_of/review_kind`, `meta.find_rscript/r_versions/compare/LOG_REL/PY_REL/PLAN_REL/R_SCRIPT`.

### Spec contradictions found (resolved as noted)

- **C1** §5.1 and §6.2 write case-fact and PRISMA ids as `R-A0-0NN`, but the ledger id is `R-A<n>-NNN` (three digits, `results_ledger.validate_doc`) → read as `R-A0-NNN` (D5, D10).
- **C2** §6.4 "writes meta-log.txt and imports it with `results_ledger.py`": `import` reads a results document, not a log, and replaces the whole ledger, which would erase case facts or PRISMA counts → per-tool documents plus `--merge` (D1, D6).
- **C3** §5.1 "`observed` … the SAP register check skips it" vs §6.1 "A0 is the PRISMA counts" in the register: if observed records were skipped entirely, a registered A0 would be "selective reporting" → observed records count as reported and skip every other rule (D3).
- **C4** §3 "no role-briefed drafting until G3 is open; the hook enforces it" vs §4 "screening suggestions, draft extraction and draft risk-of-bias are role briefs", which run before G3 → separate marker `orchestra-prep:` gated by G2 (D7).
- **C5** §5.3 puts consent under `--strict` ("the `--final` path") while §8 keeps "G4 and `--final` unchanged"; nothing on the `--final` path runs `case_lint` → `gate.export_problems` adds the consent rule for `case-report` (D19).
- **C6** §6.5 writes `06_writing/review/sof.md` into a draft folder whose order (§8) does not name it → `sof.md` is a section of the systematic order (D17, D21).
- **C7** `results_ledger.py` accepts only `04_data/*.deid.csv` as `data_file`, but P5 values come from `01_case/facts.csv`, `screening.csv`, `extraction.csv`, `grade.json` → allowlist (D2).
- **C8** §6.2 keeps reviewers in `project.yaml → review.reviewers: []`, but `projectcfg` reads no YAML lists and returns `"null"` for null → `parse_list` and `get` (D9, D28).
- **C9** §5.1 says E-NUM works unchanged for case reports, but relative-time numbers (day +14) are not ledger values and would block → masked in the case-report deliverable only (D20).
- **C10** §6.3 assigns RoB and charting rules to E-SCR without naming a script → `extract_check.py` (D11).
- **C11** §8 "init_project types `case-report`, `systematic-review`, `review`" — these types and the designs already exist since P0; what is new is the type/design pairing, folders and keys (D23).
- **C12** §6.4's first version asked for "published worked examples"; the a2bd347 update replaced it with metafor goldens. The plan uses goldens plus independent recomputation and invariances, and types no published number (D16).

### Gaps left open (stated, not silently filled)

- Post-hoc meta-analyses are not run by `meta.py` (D12); the skill records `POST-HOC` and refers to a statistician.
- Two metafor conventions (MH heterogeneity Q; default zero-cell handling for RD) are matched by explicit arguments and must be confirmed by the goldens; a mismatch is fixed by aligning the definition and updating D14, never by widening the tolerance (Task 5 Step 5).
- The guideline item ids were written without the official checklists open; Task 2 checks them online (D25).
- Cohen's κ is printed by `screening.py kappa` but is not a ledger record; the methods quote it with `<!-- not-a-result: … -->` (role `review-systematic`).
- PROSPERO does not register scoping reviews; `prospero-fields.md` says so (not in the spec).
